     if(spec.kind!=='matching'){delete item.pairs;delete item.items;delete item.correct_order;delete item.sequence}
     if(spec.kind==='fill_blank')item.fill_choices=clone(item.options_tr||[]);
     return item;
    });
   }
  }
  function unitQuestions(stage){return clone(templates?.[stage]||[]).sort((a,b)=>a.pilot_order-b.pilot_order)}
  function audit(){
   const all=bank()||[],stages={};let pass=true;
   for(const stage of TARGETS){
    const qs=all.filter(q=>q.stage_id===stage).sort((a,b)=>a.pilot_order-b.pilot_order),counts={},issues=[];
    qs.forEach((q,index)=>{
     counts[q.activity_type]=(counts[q.activity_type]||0)+1;
     if(q.pilot_order!==index+1)issues.push(q.id+':order');
     if(index<4&&!q.teaching_card)issues.push(q.id+':teaching');
     if(q.activity_type==='matching'&&(!Array.isArray(q.pairs)||q.pairs.length<2))issues.push(q.id+':pairs');
     if(['card_choice','true_false','fill_blank','scenario','odd_one_out'].includes(q.activity_type)&&(!Array.isArray(q.options_tr)||!q.options_tr.includes(q.correct_answer)))issues.push(q.id+':answer');
     if(q.religious_release_ready!==false)issues.push(q.id+':release');
    });
    if(qs.length!==12)issues.push('question_count');
    FORMATS.forEach(type=>{if(counts[type]!==2)issues.push(type+':'+(counts[type]||0))});
    stages[stage]={total:qs.length,counts,issues,pass:issues.length===0};if(issues.length)pass=false;
   }
   return{version:VERSION,build:BUILD,stages,pass};
  }
  function install(){
   const all=bank();if(!all)return false;
   try{if(!templates)createTemplates(all)}catch(error){console.error('DEEN v9.2 template',error);return false}
   for(let i=all.length-1;i>=0;i--)if(TARGETS.includes(String(all[i]?.stage_id||'')))all.splice(i,1);
   TARGETS.forEach(stage=>all.push(...unitQuestions(stage)));
   try{TARGETS.forEach(id=>{const st=STAGES.find(x=>x.id===id);if(st)st.meta='3 aşama · 12 soru · 6 format'})}catch(_){}
   installs++;lastAudit=audit();document.documentElement.dataset.deenU01FullUnit=lastAudit.pass?'ready':'check';return lastAudit.pass;
  }
  const previousChoose=typeof chooseQuestions==='function'?chooseQuestions:window.chooseQuestions;
  const choose=function(id){return TARGETS.includes(id)?unitQuestions(id):previousChoose?.(id)||[]};
  try{window.chooseQuestions=choose;chooseQuestions=choose}catch(_){window.chooseQuestions=choose}
  install();[60,180,520,1200,2600].forEach(ms=>setTimeout(install,ms));
  window.DEEN_U01_FULL_UNIT={version:VERSION,build:BUILD,targets:[...TARGETS],formats:[...FORMATS],questions:stage=>unitQuestions(stage),audit,install,check:()=>({version:VERSION,build:BUILD,installs,...audit()})};
  try{document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('deen:u01-full-unit:installed',{detail:{version:VERSION,build:BUILD,targets:TARGETS}}))}catch(_){}

<script id="deen-v930-u01-sequence-variation">(function runtime(config){
  if(window.__deenV930SequenceVariation)return;window.__deenV930SequenceVariation=true;
  const VERSION=config.version,BUILD=config.build,ORDERS=config.orders,TARGETS=Object.keys(ORDERS);
  const clone=value=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));let plans=0,lastAudit=null;
  function format(q){return String(q?.activity_type||q?.question_type||q?.type||'unknown')}
  function plan(stage,questions){
   const order=ORDERS[stage];if(!order||!Array.isArray(questions)||questions.length!==12)return questions||[];
   const byOriginal=new Map();
   questions.forEach((question,index)=>{
    const item=clone(question),source=Number(item.sequence_source_order||item.pilot_order)||index+1;
    item.sequence_source_order=source;byOriginal.set(source,item);
   });
   const result=order.map((source,index)=>{
    const item=byOriginal.get(source);if(!item)return null;
    item.pilot_order=index+1;item.sequence_position=index+1;item.sequence_version=VERSION;return item;
   }).filter(Boolean);
   if(result.length!==12)return questions;
   plans++;return result;
  }
  const previousChoose=typeof chooseQuestions==='function'?chooseQuestions:window.chooseQuestions;
  const choose=function(id){const questions=previousChoose?.(id)||[];return TARGETS.includes(id)?plan(id,questions):questions};
  try{window.chooseQuestions=choose;chooseQuestions=choose}catch(_){window.chooseQuestions=choose}
  function audit(){
   const stages={},signatures=new Set();let pass=true;
   TARGETS.forEach(stage=>{
    const base=previousChoose?.(stage)||[],questions=plan(stage,base),types=questions.map(format),issues=[];
    const sourceOrder=questions.map(q=>q.sequence_source_order),expected=ORDERS[stage];
    if(questions.length!==12)issues.push('question_count');
    if(JSON.stringify(sourceOrder)!==JSON.stringify(expected))issues.push('order');
    for(let index=1;index<types.length;index++)if(types[index]===types[index-1])issues.push('repeat:'+index);
    if(!sourceOrder.slice(0,4).every(n=>n>=1&&n<=4))issues.push('learn_phase');
    if(!sourceOrder.slice(4,8).every(n=>n>=5&&n<=8))issues.push('apply_phase');
    if(!sourceOrder.slice(8,12).every(n=>n>=9&&n<=12))issues.push('recall_phase');
    const signature=types.join('>');signatures.add(signature);
    stages[stage]={sourceOrder,types,signature,issues,pass:issues.length===0};if(issues.length)pass=false;
   });
   if(signatures.size!==TARGETS.length)pass=false;
   lastAudit={version:VERSION,build:BUILD,uniqueSequences:signatures.size,stages,pass};return clone(lastAudit);
  }
  setTimeout(()=>{lastAudit=audit();document.documentElement.dataset.deenSequenceVariation=lastAudit.pass?'ready':'check'},80);
  window.DEEN_U01_SEQUENCE_VARIATION={version:VERSION,build:BUILD,targets:[...TARGETS],orders:clone(ORDERS),plan,audit,check:()=>({version:VERSION,build:BUILD,plans,...(lastAudit||audit())})};

  function normalize(){
   const data=store();let changed=false;

     const base=checkpointTemplate(key,learnedAt),current=entry.checkpoints[key];

    }
   }
   return changed;
  }
  function stageQuestionIds(stageId){let questions=[];try{questions=typeof chooseQuestions==='function'?chooseQuestions(stageId):window.chooseQuestions?.(stageId)||[]}catch(_){}return[...new Set(questions.map(q=>String(q?.id||'')).filter(Boolean))]}

   }
  }
  function scheduleStage(stageId,learnedAt=Date.now()){

  }
  function migrateCompleted(){
   normalize();let changed=false;

   }

  function pending(now=Date.now()){

   }

  }
  function due(now=Date.now()){return pending(now).filter(item=>item.due)}
  function markComplete(stageId,key,firstCorrect,total,at=Date.now()){

  }
  function stageTitle(stageId){try{return STAGES.find(item=>item.id===stageId)?.title||stageId}catch(_){return stageId}}
  function reviewQuestions(stageId,key){

  function openReview(stageId,key,{force=false}={}){

  function installLifecycle(){

<script id="deen-v950-age-profiles-js">
(() => {
 if(window.__deenV950AgeProfiles)return;window.__deenV950AgeProfiles=true;
 const VERSION='9.5.0-U01-AGE-PROFILES',BUILD='950age1',ONBOARDING_VERSION=3;
 const TARGETS=['U01-S01','U01-S02','U01-S03','U01-S04','U01-S05','U01-S06','U01-S07'];
 const PROFILES={
  child:{key:'child',label:'Çocuk',range:'8–12',icon:'🌱',desc:'Kısa cümleler, net yönlendirme ve daha sade geri bildirim.'},
  teen:{key:'teen',label:'Genç',range:'13–17',icon:'✦',desc:'Doğal, doğrudan ve çocukça hissettirmeyen anlatım.'},
  adult:{key:'adult',label:'Yetişkin',range:'18+',icon:'◇',desc:'Orijinal kavramsal dil ve daha yoğun açıklamalar.'}
 };
 const clone=v=>{try{return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v))}catch(_){return v}};
 let renders=0,feedbacks=0,profileChanges=0,lastAudit=null;
 function getState(){try{return state}catch(_){return window.state}}
 function getSession(){try{return session}catch(_){return window.session}}
 function ensure(){
  const s=getState();if(!s||typeof s!=='object')return null;
  if(!PROFILES[s.learningProfile])s.learningProfile='child';
  s.learningProfileVersion=1;s.stateVersion=Math.max(18,Number(s.stateVersion)||0);
  try{document.documentElement.dataset.deenLearningProfile=s.learningProfile}catch(_){}
  return s;
 }
 function active(){return ensure()?.learningProfile||'child'}
 function meta(key=active()){return PROFILES[key]||PROFILES.child}
 function tidy(text){return String(text??'').replace(/\s{2,}/g,' ').replace(/\s+([?.!,;:])/g,'$1').trim()}
 function teenQuestion(text){
  let s=String(text??'');
  s=s.replace(/Aşağıdakilerden hangisi/gi,'Hangisi')
     .replace(/başlangıç düzeyinde\s*/gi,'')
     .replace(/en doğru şekilde/gi,'en doğru biçimde')
     .replace(/Şu kavramları doğru açıklamalarıyla eşleştir:/gi,'Kavramları doğru açıklamalarla eşleştir:')
     .replace(/İpucu olmadan tamamla:/gi,'Hatırla ve tamamla:')
     .replace(/Boşluğu tamamla:/gi,'Boşluğu doldur:');
  s=tidy(s);
  if(s&&!/^(Kısa kontrol:|Boşluğu doldur:|Hatırla ve tamamla:|Kavramları doğru açıklamalarla eşleştir:)/i.test(s))s='Kısa kontrol: '+s;
  return tidy(s);
 }
 function childQuestion(text){
  let s=teenQuestion(text).replace(/^Kısa kontrol:\s*/i,'');
  s=s.replace(/en iyi hangi ifadeyle açıklanır/gi,'en doğru hangisi anlatır')
     .replace(/en doğru biçimde tanımlar/gi,'en doğru anlatır')
     .replace(/en doğru biçimde açıklar/gi,'en doğru anlatır')
     .replace(/Üç açıklama doğrudur\. Hangisi kavram haritasına uymaz\?/gi,'Üçü doğru. Hangisi bu kavramlara uymaz?')
     .replace(/Bu durumda en doğru karar hangisi\?/gi,'Bu durumda en doğru seçim hangisi?')
     .replace(/hangisi doğru bir çıkarımdır/gi,'hangisi doğru bir sonuçtur');
  s=tidy(s);
  if(s&&!/^(Birlikte düşün:|Boşluğu doldur:|Hatırla ve tamamla:|Kavramları doğru açıklamalarla eşleştir:|Üçü doğru\.)/i.test(s))s='Birlikte düşün: '+s;
  return tidy(s);
 }
 function teenExplain(text){
  let s=String(text??'');
  s=s.replace(/başlangıç düzeyinde\s*/gi,'').replace(/İslam;\s*/g,'İslam, ').replace(/;\s*/g,'. ');
  return tidy(s);
 }
 function childExplain(text,prefix=true){
  let s=teenExplain(text);
  const replacements=[
   [/Bu ünitede/gi,'Bu derste'],
   [/belirli bir topluluğa veya coğrafyaya sıkıştırılamaz/gi,'sadece bir topluma veya bölgeye ait değildir'],
   [/belirli bir soy, dil veya ülkeye bağlı değildir/gi,'soy, dil veya ülke şartına bağlı değildir'],
   [/birbirini tamamlayan yönlerini görmek/gi,'birbirini nasıl tamamladığını görmek'],
   [/çerçevesinde ele alınır/gi,'çerçevesinde öğrenilir'],
   [/yalnızca dışarıdan görülen bir hareket değildir/gi,'sadece dışarıdan görülen bir hareket değildir'],
   [/genel çerçevesini anlamayı kolaylaştırır/gi,'bütünü anlamayı kolaylaştırır']
  ];
  replacements.forEach(([a,b])=>s=s.replace(a,b));s=tidy(s);
  return prefix&&s?'Kısaca: '+s:s;
 }
 function presentQuestion(text,profile=active()){return profile==='child'?childQuestion(text):profile==='teen'?teenQuestion(text):String(text??'')}
 function presentExplanation(text,profile=active(),prefix=true){return profile==='child'?childExplain(text,prefix):profile==='teen'?(prefix&&text?'Özet: '+teenExplain(text):teenExplain(text)):String(text??'')}
 function promptFor(q,profile=active()){
  const t=String(q?.activity_type||q?.question_type||'');
  const child={matching:'Kavramı doğru açıklamayla eşleştir.',true_false:'Doğru mu, yanlış mı? Kararını ver.',fill_blank:'Boşluğu doğru kelimeyle tamamla.',scenario:'Durumu oku. En doğru davranışı seç.',odd_one_out:'Üçü uyuyor, biri uymuyor. Farklı olanı bul.',card_choice:'Kartları oku. En doğru olanı seç.',multiple_choice:'Soruyu oku. En doğru cevabı seç.',missing_item:'Eksik olan doğru parçayı bul.',speed_review:'Hızlı düşün ve doğru cevabı seç.'};
  const teen={matching:'Kavramlarla açıklamaları eşleştir.',true_false:'İfadeyi değerlendir: doğru mu, yanlış mı?',fill_blank:'Boşluğu doğru ifadeyle tamamla.',scenario:'Durumu değerlendir ve en uygun seçimi yap.',odd_one_out:'Kavram haritasına uymayan ifadeyi bul.',card_choice:'Kartları karşılaştır ve en doğru seçimi yap.',multiple_choice:'En doğru seçeneği belirle.',missing_item:'Eksik parçayı tamamla.',speed_review:'Hızlı kontrol: en doğru cevabı seç.'};
  if(profile==='adult')return null;return (profile==='child'?child:teen)[t]||(profile==='child'?'Soruyu oku ve cevabını seç.':'Soruyu değerlendir ve cevabını seç.');
 }
 function currentQuestion(){const s=getSession();return s?.questions?.[Number(s?.index)||0]||null}
 function setBase(el,qid){if(!el)return'';if(el.dataset.v950Qid!==String(qid||'')){el.dataset.v950Qid=String(qid||'');el.dataset.v950Base=el.textContent||''}return el.dataset.v950Base||''}
 function applyQuestionUI(){

  const title=document.getElementById('lessonNoteTitle'),note=document.getElementById('lessonNoteText');
  if(title){const base=setBase(title,qid+'-note-title');title.textContent=profile==='child'?'Küçük adım, kalıcı öğrenme.':profile==='teen'?'Kısa odak, net cevap.':base}
  if(note){const base=setBase(note,qid+'-note');note.textContent=profile==='child'?'Önce soruyu anla, sonra cevabı seç. Yanlış yaparsan birlikte düzeltiriz.':profile==='teen'?'Soruyu dikkatle değerlendir; hata olursa açıklamayla hemen pekiştir.':base}

  document.querySelectorAll('.v857-scene-body').forEach(el=>{const base=setBase(el,qid+'-scene');el.textContent=profile==='adult'?base:presentExplanation(base,profile,false)});

 }
 function applyFeedback(correct,q=currentQuestion()){

  feedbacks++;return true;
 }

 function audit(){
  const questions=activeQuestions(),byStage={};let pass=true;
  for(const stage of TARGETS){const qs=questions.filter(q=>q.stage_id===stage),issues=[];if(qs.length!==12)issues.push('count:'+qs.length);for(const q of qs){for(const p of Object.keys(PROFILES)){const stem=presentQuestion(q.question_tr||'',p),exp=presentExplanation(q.explanation_tr||'',p,true);if(!stem.trim())issues.push(q.id+':'+p+':stem');if((q.explanation_tr||'')&&!exp.trim())issues.push(q.id+':'+p+':exp')}if(Array.isArray(q.options_tr)&&q.correct_answer!=null&&!q.options_tr.includes(q.correct_answer))issues.push(q.id+':answer')};byStage[stage]={count:qs.length,issues,pass:!issues.length};if(issues.length)pass=false}
  const changed={child:questions.filter(q=>presentQuestion(q.question_tr||'', 'child')!==String(q.question_tr||'')).length,teen:questions.filter(q=>presentQuestion(q.question_tr||'', 'teen')!==String(q.question_tr||'')).length};
  pass=pass&&questions.length===84&&changed.child===84&&changed.teen===84;
  lastAudit={version:VERSION,build:BUILD,pass,total:questions.length,expected:84,profile:active(),byStage,changedStems:changed,answerKeysUntouched:true,at:Date.now()};return clone(lastAudit)
 }
 ensure();try{saveState()}catch(_){}setTimeout(()=>{try{updateUI();injectProfileCard();lastAudit=audit()}catch(e){console.warn('V9.5 install',e)}},120);
 window.DEEN_AGE_PROFILE={version:VERSION,build:BUILD,profiles:clone(PROFILES),get:active,set:setProfile,openPicker,presentQuestion,presentExplanation,apply:()=>{applyQuestionUI();adaptOverlay(document)},audit,check:()=>lastAudit||audit()};
 document.documentElement.dataset.deenAgeProfiles='ready';
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v960-narration-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV960Narration)return;window.__deenV960Narration=true;
 const VERSION='9.6.0-ACCESSIBLE-NARRATION',BUILD='960tts1',STATE_VERSION=19;
 let speaks=0,stops=0,questionButtons=0,feedbackButtons=0,lastAudit=null,lastVoice='',lastError='';
 const rateMap={slow:.82,normal:.95,fast:1.08};
 function getState(){try{return state}catch(_){return window.state}}
 function getSession(){try{return session}catch(_){return window.session}}
 function ensure(){
  const s=getState();if(!s||typeof s!=='object')return null;
  s.settings=s.settings&&typeof s.settings==='object'?s.settings:{};
  if(typeof s.settings.narration!=='boolean')s.settings.narration=true;
  if(typeof s.settings.narrationAutoQuestion!=='boolean')s.settings.narrationAutoQuestion=false;
  if(typeof s.settings.narrationAutoFeedback!=='boolean')s.settings.narrationAutoFeedback=false;
  if(!rateMap[s.settings.narrationRate])s.settings.narrationRate='normal';
  s.narrationVersion=1;s.stateVersion=Math.max(STATE_VERSION,Number(s.stateVersion)||0);
  return s;
 }
 function supported(){return !!(window.speechSynthesis&&window.SpeechSynthesisUtterance)}
 function currentQuestion(){const s=getSession();return s?.questions?.[Number(s?.index)||0]||null}
 function profile(){try{return window.DEEN_AGE_PROFILE?.get?.()||'adult'}catch(_){return 'adult'}}
 function isMediaQuestion(q){
  const type=String(q?.activity_type||q?.question_type||q?.type||'').toLowerCase();
  const req=String(q?.asset_requirement||'').toLowerCase();
  return ['listening','listen_select','pronunciation','audio'].some(x=>type.includes(x))||req.includes('audio_')||req.includes('recitation')||req.includes('pronunciation');
 }
 function stripArabic(text){return String(text??'').replace(/[\u0600-\u06FF\u0750-\u077F\u08A0-\u08FF]+(?:[\s\u0600-\u06FF\u0750-\u077F\u08A0-\u08FF]+)*/g,' Arapça metin ekranda gösteriliyor. ')}
 function clean(text){return stripArabic(text).replace(/[◇✦✓×→↗★☾]/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').replace(/\s+([?.!,;:])/g,'$1').replace(/([.!?])(?:\s*[.!?])+/g,'$1').trim()}
 function visibleText(el){if(!el)return'';const st=getComputedStyle(el);if(st.display==='none'||st.visibility==='hidden'||el.closest('[hidden]'))return'';return clean(el.innerText||el.textContent||'')}
 function uniqueTexts(nodes){const seen=new Set(),out=[];nodes.forEach(el=>{const t=visibleText(el);if(t&&!seen.has(t)){seen.add(t);out.push(t)}});return out}
 function questionText(q=currentQuestion()){
  if(!q)return'';
  const frame=document.querySelector('.question-frame');if(!frame)return clean(q.question_tr||q.prompt||'');
  const instruction=visibleText(document.getElementById('qType'));
  const prompt=visibleText(document.getElementById('questionText')||frame.querySelector('.question')||frame.querySelector('.v856-swipe-text'));
  const selectors=['#answerArea .option-label','#answerArea .fill-choice','#answerArea .fill-chip','#answerArea .seq-chip','#answerArea .v853-match-card','#answerArea .match-card','#answerArea .v854-seq-card','#answerArea .v856-swipe-choice','#answerArea .v857-choice-card','#answerArea .choice-card'];
  const items=uniqueTexts([...frame.querySelectorAll(selectors.join(','))]);
  let text=[instruction,prompt].filter(Boolean).join('. ');
  if(items.length){text+=(text?'. ':'')+'Seçenekler. '+items.map((x,i)=>`${i+1}. ${x}`).join('. ')}
  return clean(text);
 }
 function feedbackText(){
  const title=visibleText(document.getElementById('feedbackTitle'));
  const body=visibleText(document.getElementById('feedbackText'));
  return clean([title,body].filter(Boolean).join('. '));
 }
 function selectVoice(){
  if(!supported())return null;const voices=window.speechSynthesis.getVoices?.()||[];
  const tr=voices.filter(v=>/^tr([_-]|$)/i.test(v.lang||''));
  const voice=tr.find(v=>v.localService)||tr[0]||voices.find(v=>/^tr/i.test(v.lang||''))||null;
  lastVoice=voice?.name||'';return voice;
 }
 function stop(reason='manual'){
  if(!supported())return false;try{window.speechSynthesis.cancel();stops++;document.querySelectorAll('.v960-speak.active').forEach(b=>{b.classList.remove('active');b.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');b.querySelector('[data-v960-label]')&&(b.querySelector('[data-v960-label]').textContent=b.dataset.idleLabel||'Dinle')});document.documentElement.dataset.deenNarrating='false';return true}catch(e){lastError=String(e?.message||e);return false}
 }
 function speak(text,{kind='question',button=null}={}){
  const s=ensure();if(!s?.settings?.narration||!supported())return false;
  const q=currentQuestion();if(isMediaQuestion(q)){try{toast?.('Bu içerikte yalnız doğrulanmış ses kaydı kullanılır.')}catch(_){}return false}
  const value=clean(text);if(!value)return false;stop('replace');
  try{
   const u=new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(value);u.lang='tr-TR';u.rate=rateMap[s.settings.narrationRate]||.95;u.pitch=1;u.volume=1;
   const voice=selectVoice();if(voice)u.voice=voice;
   if(button){button.classList.add('active');button.setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');const label=button.querySelector('[data-v960-label]');if(label)label.textContent='Durdur'}
   document.documentElement.dataset.deenNarrating=kind;speaks++;
   const done=()=>{if(button){button.classList.remove('active');button.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');const label=button.querySelector('[data-v960-label]');if(label)label.textContent=button.dataset.idleLabel||'Dinle'}document.documentElement.dataset.deenNarrating='false'};
   u.onend=done;u.onerror=e=>{lastError=String(e?.error||'speech_error');done()};window.speechSynthesis.speak(u);return true;
  }catch(e){lastError=String(e?.message||e);return false}
 }
 function makeButton(label,kind,reader,extra=''){
  const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='v960-speak '+extra;b.dataset.idleLabel=label;b.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');b.setAttribute('aria-label',label);b.innerHTML='<span aria-hidden="true">🔊</span><span data-v960-label>'+label+'</span>';
  b.onclick=()=>{if(b.classList.contains('active'))stop('toggle');else speak(reader(),{kind,button:b})};return b;
 }
 function mountQuestion(){
  ensure();stop('question-change');document.querySelector('.v960-narration-bar')?.remove();
  const q=currentQuestion(),frame=document.querySelector('.question-frame');if(!q||!frame||isMediaQuestion(q))return false;
  const bar=document.createElement('div');bar.className='v960-narration-bar';
  const b=makeButton('Soruyu dinle','question',()=>questionText(q));bar.append(b);
  const note=document.createElement('span');note.className='v960-narration-note';note.textContent='Soru ve ekrandaki seçenekler okunur.';bar.append(note);
  const anchor=document.getElementById('answerArea')||frame.querySelector('.options');anchor?.before(bar);questionButtons++;
  const s=ensure();bar.hidden=!s.settings.narration;
  if(s.settings.narrationAutoQuestion&&s.settings.narration&&supported())setTimeout(()=>{if(currentQuestion()===q&&!document.getElementById('feedback')?.classList.contains('show'))speak(questionText(q),{kind:'question',button:b})},180);
  return true;
 }
 function mountFeedback(correct,q=currentQuestion()){
  document.querySelector('.v960-feedback-speak')?.remove();const s=ensure();if(!q||isMediaQuestion(q))return false;
  const box=document.getElementById('feedbackBox');if(!box)return false;const b=makeButton('Açıklamayı dinle','feedback',feedbackText,'v960-feedback-speak');b.hidden=!s.settings.narration;box.append(b);feedbackButtons++;
  if(s.settings.narrationAutoFeedback&&s.settings.narration&&supported())setTimeout(()=>{if(document.getElementById('feedback')?.classList.contains('show'))speak(feedbackText(),{kind:'feedback',button:b})},180);
  return true;
 }
 function setSetting(key,value){const s=ensure();if(!s)return false;s.settings[key]=value;if(key==='narration'&&!value)stop('disabled');try{saveState()}catch(_){}try{refresh()}catch(_){}return true}
 function setRate(value){if(!rateMap[value])return false;setSetting('narrationRate',value);try{settingsPanel?.()}catch(_){}return true}
 function injectSettings(){
  const body=document.getElementById('v5SheetBody');if(!body||body.querySelector('.v960-narration-settings'))return false;const s=ensure();
  const group=document.createElement('div');group.className='v690-setting-group v960-narration-settings';
  group.innerHTML=`<small>SESLİ ANLATIM</small><p class="v960-setting-copy">Soru, seçenek ve açıklama metinlerini cihazın Türkçe konuşma motoruyla dinle. Kur’an kıraati, şehadet telaffuzu ve doğrulanmış dinleme içerikleri yapay sesle okunmaz.</p><label class="v5-toggle"><span>Sesli anlatım düğmeleri</span><input type="checkbox" ${s.settings.narration?'checked':''}></label><label class="v5-toggle"><span>Soruyu otomatik oku</span><input data-v960-autoq type="checkbox" ${s.settings.narrationAutoQuestion?'checked':''}></label><label class="v5-toggle"><span>Açıklamayı otomatik oku</span><input data-v960-autof type="checkbox" ${s.settings.narrationAutoFeedback?'checked':''}></label><div class="v960-rate-row">${[['slow','Yavaş'],['normal','Normal'],['fast','Hızlı']].map(([k,t])=>`<button type="button" class="v960-rate-btn ${s.settings.narrationRate===k?'selected':''}" data-rate="${k}">${t}</button>`).join('')}</div>${supported()?'':'<p class="v960-tts-unavailable">Bu tarayıcı konuşma sentezini desteklemiyor. Metinler normal biçimde kullanılmaya devam eder.</p>'}`;
  const toggles=group.querySelectorAll('input');toggles[0].onchange=e=>setSetting('narration',e.target.checked);toggles[1].onchange=e=>setSetting('narrationAutoQuestion',e.target.checked);toggles[2].onchange=e=>setSetting('narrationAutoFeedback',e.target.checked);group.querySelectorAll('[data-rate]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>setRate(b.dataset.rate));
  const first=body.querySelector('.v690-setting-group');first?first.after(group):body.prepend(group);return true;
 }
 function refresh(){const q=currentQuestion();const bar=document.querySelector('.v960-narration-bar');if(bar)bar.hidden=!ensure().settings.narration;const fb=document.querySelector('.v960-feedback-speak');if(fb)fb.hidden=!ensure().settings.narration;if(q&&!bar&&!document.getElementById('resultView')?.classList.contains('show'))mountQuestion()}
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendering',()=>stop('render'));

 });

 });

<script id="deen-v970-learning-analytics-js">
(()=>{

 let recorded=0,reordered=0,lastAudit=null,lastError='';

 function profile(){return state?.learningProfile||'adult'}
 function activity(q){return String(q?.activity_type||q?.question_type||q?.type||'question')}
 function activityLabel(t){return ({multiple_choice:'Çoktan seçmeli',choice_cards:'Kart seçimi',card_choice:'Kart seçimi',matching:'Eşleştirme',match:'Eşleştirme',fill_blank:'Boşluk doldurma',true_false:'Doğru / yanlış',binary:'Doğru / yanlış',rapid_recall:'Hızlı hatırlama',sequence:'Sıralama',drag_match:'Sürükle eşleştir',word_builder:'Kelime kurma',word_choice:'Kelime seçimi',typed_recall:'Yazarak hatırlama',branch_scenario:'Senaryo',bug_hunt:'Hata bulma',listen_pick:'Dinle ve seç'})[t]||t.replaceAll('_',' ')}
 function ensure(s=state){
  if(!s||typeof s!=='object')return null;
  const a=s.questionAnalytics=safe(s.questionAnalytics);a.version=2;a.items=safe(a.items);a.history=Array.isArray(a.history)?a.history.slice(-MAX_HISTORY):[];a.startedAt=Number(a.startedAt)||Date.now();
  if(!a.seededFromReview){
   for(const [id,r0] of Object.entries(s.reviewSchedule||{})){
    const r=safe(r0),attempts=Math.max(0,(Number(r.correctCount)||0)+(Number(r.wrongCount)||0));if(!attempts)continue;const q=qById(id),x=a.items[id]=safe(a.items[id]);
    x.id=id;x.stageId=x.stageId||q?.stage_id||'';x.unit=x.unit||q?.stage_id?.slice?.(0,3)||'';x.topic=x.topic||q?.topic||q?.stage_title||'';x.concept=x.concept||q?.concept_id||'';x.activity=x.activity||activity(q);x.difficulty=x.difficulty||q?.difficulty||'';
    if(!x.attempts){x.attempts=attempts;x.correct=Number(r.correctCount)||0;x.wrong=Number(r.wrongCount)||0;x.lastSeen=Number(r.lastSeen)||0;x.firstSeenCorrect=null;x.totalMs=0;x.timedAttempts=0;x.reviewAttempts=attempts;x.lessonAttempts=0;x.correctStreak=Array.isArray(r.recent)?r.recent.slice().reverse().findIndex(v=>!v):-1;if(x.correctStreak<0)x.correctStreak=Array.isArray(r.recent)?r.recent.length:0;x.maxCorrectStreak=x.correctStreak;x.profiles={};x.recent=Array.isArray(r.recent)?r.recent.slice(-8):[]}
   }
   a.seededFromReview=true;
  }
  s.stateVersion=Math.max(21,Number(s.stateVersion)||0);return a;
 }
 function itemFor(q){const a=ensure(),id=String(q?.id||'');if(!id)return null;const x=a.items[id]=safe(a.items[id]);x.id=id;x.stageId=q?.stage_id||x.stageId||'';x.unit=q?.stage_id?.slice?.(0,3)||x.unit||'';x.topic=q?.topic||q?.stage_title||x.topic||'';x.concept=q?.concept_id||x.concept||'';x.activity=activity(q);x.difficulty=q?.difficulty||x.difficulty||'';x.attempts=Number(x.attempts)||0;x.correct=Number(x.correct)||0;x.wrong=Number(x.wrong)||0;x.totalMs=Number(x.totalMs)||0;x.timedAttempts=Number(x.timedAttempts)||0;x.reviewAttempts=Number(x.reviewAttempts)||0;x.lessonAttempts=Number(x.lessonAttempts)||0;x.correctStreak=Number(x.correctStreak)||0;x.maxCorrectStreak=Number(x.maxCorrectStreak)||0;x.profiles=safe(x.profiles);x.recent=Array.isArray(x.recent)?x.recent.slice(-8):[];return x}
 function shown(q=currentQuestion()){
  if(!q||!session)return false;const key=String(session.index)+'|'+q.id;session.__v970Shown={key,id:q.id,index:session.index,at:Date.now()};return true;
 }
 function record(correct,q=currentQuestion(),elapsed=null){
  if(!q||!session)return false;const key=String(session.index)+'|'+q.id;session.__v970Recorded=safe(session.__v970Recorded);if(session.__v970Recorded[key])return false;session.__v970Recorded[key]=true;
  const x=itemFor(q),a=ensure();if(!x||!a)return false;const now=Date.now(),ms=elapsed==null?null:clamp(elapsed,250,MAX_MS),first=x.attempts===0;
  x.attempts++;if(correct)x.correct++;else x.wrong++;x.lastSeen=now;x.lastCorrect=!!correct;x.firstSeenCorrect=first?!!correct:x.firstSeenCorrect;x.correctStreak=correct?x.correctStreak+1:0;x.maxCorrectStreak=Math.max(x.maxCorrectStreak,x.correctStreak);x.recent=[...x.recent,correct?1:0].slice(-8);
  if(ms!=null&&Number.isFinite(ms)){x.totalMs+=ms;x.timedAttempts++;x.lastMs=ms;if(correct)x.fastestCorrectMs=x.fastestCorrectMs?Math.min(x.fastestCorrectMs,ms):ms}
  const isReview=!!session.review;if(isReview)x.reviewAttempts++;else x.lessonAttempts++;const pk=profile();const p=x.profiles[pk]=safe(x.profiles[pk]);p.attempts=(Number(p.attempts)||0)+1;p.correct=(Number(p.correct)||0)+(correct?1:0);p.wrong=(Number(p.wrong)||0)+(correct?0:1);
  const event={at:now,id:q.id,stageId:q.stage_id||'',unit:q.stage_id?.slice?.(0,3)||'',concept:q.concept_id||'',topic:q.topic||q.stage_title||'',activity:activity(q),difficulty:q.difficulty||'',correct:!!correct,ms:ms==null?null:Math.round(ms),profile:pk,review:isReview,spaced:!!session.__v940Spaced};a.history=[...a.history,event].slice(-MAX_HISTORY);a.lastAnswer=event;recorded++;
  try{saveState()}catch(_){}return true;
 }
 function avgMs(x){return x?.timedAttempts?Math.round(x.totalMs/x.timedAttempts):null}
 function scoreItem(x){if(!x?.attempts)return 50;const acc=x.correct/x.attempts,recent=x.recent?.length?x.recent.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/x.recent.length:acc;let speed=.55;const m=avgMs(x);if(m!=null)speed=clamp(1-(m-12000)/78000,0,1);return Math.round(clamp(acc*.62+recent*.28+speed*.10,0,1)*100)}
 function priority(id){const x=ensure()?.items?.[id];if(!x?.attempts)return 0;const err=x.wrong/x.attempts,recentWrong=(x.recent||[]).slice(-3).filter(v=>!v).length,slow=avgMs(x)!=null?clamp((avgMs(x)-25000)/65000,0,1):0;return Number((err*6+recentWrong*1.3+slow*2+(x.wrong>=2?1.5:0)).toFixed(2))}
 function aggregate(kind){
  const a=ensure(),map=new Map();for(const x of Object.values(a.items)){if(!x?.attempts)continue;const key=kind==='concept'?(x.concept||x.topic||'Diğer'):(x.activity||'question'),label=kind==='concept'?(x.topic||x.concept||'Diğer'):activityLabel(x.activity||'question');let r=map.get(key);if(!r){r={key,label,attempts:0,correct:0,wrong:0,totalMs:0,timed:0,ids:[]};map.set(key,r)}r.attempts+=x.attempts;r.correct+=x.correct;r.wrong+=x.wrong;r.totalMs+=x.totalMs;r.timed+=x.timedAttempts;r.ids.push(x.id)}
  return [...map.values()].map(r=>{r.accuracy=r.attempts?Math.round(r.correct/r.attempts*100):0;r.avgMs=r.timed?Math.round(r.totalMs/r.timed):null;r.score=Math.round(r.ids.reduce((s,id)=>s+scoreItem(a.items[id]),0)/Math.max(1,r.ids.length));return r}).sort((a,b)=>a.score-b.score||b.attempts-a.attempts);
 }
 function trend(){const h=ensure().history.filter(e=>typeof e.correct==='boolean');if(h.length<8)return null;const recent=h.slice(-10),prev=h.slice(-20,-10);if(prev.length<4)return null;const a=recent.filter(e=>e.correct).length/recent.length,b=prev.filter(e=>e.correct).length/prev.length;return Math.round((a-b)*100)}
 function summary(){
  const a=ensure(),items=Object.values(a.items).filter(x=>x?.attempts),attempts=items.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.attempts,0),correct=items.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.correct,0),timed=items.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.timedAttempts,0),totalMs=items.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.totalMs,0),first=items.filter(x=>typeof x.firstSeenCorrect==='boolean'),concepts=aggregate('concept'),types=aggregate('type'),weak=concepts.filter(x=>x.attempts>=2&&x.score<65),repeat=items.filter(x=>x.attempts>=2&&x.wrong>=2).sort((a,b)=>priority(b.id)-priority(a.id));
  return{attempts,questions:items.length,accuracy:attempts?Math.round(correct/attempts*100):0,avgMs:timed?Math.round(totalMs/timed):null,firstTry:first.length?Math.round(first.filter(x=>x.firstSeenCorrect).length/first.length*100):null,trend:trend(),weakConcepts:weak.length,repeatedErrors:repeat.length,concepts,types,repeat,profile:profile(),lastAnswer:a.lastAnswer||null};
 }
 function fmtMs(ms){if(ms==null)return'—';const s=Math.round(ms/1000);return s<60?s+' sn':Math.floor(s/60)+' dk '+String(s%60).padStart(2,'0')+' sn'}
 function row(r,weak=false){return `<div class="v970-row ${weak?'weak':''}"><div class="row"><b>${esc970(r.label)}</b><em>%${r.accuracy}</em></div><div class="v970-track"><i style="--pct:${clamp(r.accuracy,0,100)}%"></i></div><small>${r.attempts} cevap · ${r.wrong} yanlış${r.avgMs!=null?' · ort. '+fmtMs(r.avgMs):''}</small></div>`}
 function insight(s){if(!s.attempts)return'İlk cevaplarından sonra sistem hangi soru ve kavramlarda zorlandığını göstermeye başlayacak.';if(s.repeatedErrors)return`${s.repeatedErrors} soruda hata tekrar ediyor. Akıllı tekrar bu sinyalleri önceliklendirecek.`;if(s.weakConcepts)return`${s.weakConcepts} kavram henüz dengeli değil. Tekrar planında bunlar daha görünür olacak.`;if(s.trend!=null&&s.trend>5)return`Son cevaplarında doğruluk önceki döneme göre ${s.trend} puan yükseldi.`;return'Belirgin tekrar eden hata yok. Sistem yeni cevaplarla öğrenme profilini güncellemeye devam edecek.'}
 function analyticsHtml(){
  const s=summary();if(!s.attempts)return `<div class="v970-analytics"><div class="v970-empty">Henüz analiz edilecek cevap yok. İlk mini dersi tamamladığında doğruluk, cevap süresi, soru türleri ve tekrar eden hatalar burada oluşacak.</div></div>`;
  const base=`<div class="v970-analytics"><div class="v970-summary"><div class="v970-metric"><strong>%${s.accuracy}</strong><span>GENEL DOĞRULUK</span></div><div class="v970-metric"><strong>${fmtMs(s.avgMs)}</strong><span>ORT.   if(!state.premium)return base+`<div class="v970-section-title">TEMEL ANALİZ</div>${s.types.slice(0,3).map(r=>row(r,r.score<60)).join('')}<div class="v970-lock">Premium’da kavram bazlı zayıflıklar, tekrar eden soru hataları ve ayrıntılı soru türü karşılaştırmaları açılır. Dinî içerik erişimi değişmez.</div></div>`;
  const weak=s.concepts.filter(r=>r.attempts>=1).slice(0,6),types=s.types.slice(0,6),repeat=s.repeat.slice(0,5).map(x=>({label:x.topic||x.id,accuracy:Math.round(x.correct/x.attempts*100),attempts:x.attempts,wrong:x.wrong,avgMs:avgMs(x),score:scoreItem(x)}));
  return base+`<div class="v970-section-title">KAVRAM BAZINDA</div>${weak.map(r=>row(r,r.score<65)).join('')||'<div class="v970-empty">Kavram verisi henüz yok.</div>'}<div class="v970-section-title">SORU TÜRÜ BAZINDA</div>${types.map(r=>row(r,r.score<60)).join('')||'<div class="v970-empty">Soru türü verisi henüz yok.</div>'}${repeat.length?`<div class="v970-section-title">TEKRAR EDEN HATALAR</div>${repeat.map(r=>row(r,true)).join('')}`:''}<div><span class="v970-tag">Profil: ${esc970(s.profile)}</span><span class="v970-tag">${s.questions} farklı soru</span><span class="v970-tag">${s.attempts} toplam cevap</span></div></div>`;
 }

<script id="deen-v994-animal-companion-js">
(()=>{

   const id=String(state.profile.companion||'');
   if(id&&!ANIMALS[id])state.profile.companion=null;
   state.stateVersion=Math.max(STATE_VERSION,Number(state.stateVersion)||0);
  }catch(e){}
 }
 function current(){ensure();const id=String(state?.profile?.companion||'');return ANIMALS[id]?{id,...ANIMALS[id]}:null}
 function cards(){
  const active=current()?.id||'';
  return '<div class="v994-animal-grid">'+Object.entries(ANIMALS).map(([id,a])=>'<button type="button" class="v994-animal-card '+(active===id?'selected':'')+'" onclick="DEEN_COMPANION.select(\''+id+'\')"><span class="animal" aria-hidden="true">'+a.emoji+'</span><b>'+esc(a.name)+'</b><small>'+esc(a.trait)+'</small></button>').join('')+'</div>';
 }
 function setRequired(on){
  const d=document.getElementById('v5Sheet');if(!d)return;
  if(on)d.dataset.companionRequired='1';else delete d.dataset.companionRequired;
 }
 function open(mode='profile'){
  ensure();pickerMode=mode;
  const required=mode==='onboarding'||mode==='required';
  if(typeof sheet!=='function')return false;
  sheet('Yol arkadaşını seç','<p>DEEN boyunca sana eşlik edecek hayvanı seç. Bu bir kişilik testi değil; sadece senin görsel yol arkadaşın.</p>'+cards()+'<p class="v994-picker-note">Seçimini daha sonra Profil ekranındaki hayvana dokunarak değiştirebilirsin.</p>');
  setRequired(required);
  return true;
 }
 function select(id){
  if(!ANIMALS[id])return false;ensure();
  state.profile.companion=id;
  try{saveState()}catch(e){}
  const mode=pickerMode;setRequired(false);
  if(mode==='onboarding'){
   try{BASE_ONBOARDING(0)}catch(e){try{closeSheet()}catch(_){}}
  }else{
   try{closeSheet()}catch(e){}
   try{updateUI()}catch(e){}
   setTimeout(()=>{decorateProfile();retireLegacyCharacterUi()},0);
  }
  return true;
 }
 function decorateProfile(){
  const a=current(),root=document.getElementById('profileScreenBody');if(!a||!root)return false;
  const avatar=root.querySelector('.v5-profilehead .v5-avatar.large');if(!avatar)return false;
  avatar.classList.add('v994-companion-avatar');avatar.innerHTML='<span class="v994-profile-animal" aria-hidden="true">'+a.emoji+'</span>';
  avatar.title=a.name+' · '+a.trait;avatar.setAttribute('aria-label','Yol arkadaşın '+a.name+'. Değiştirmek için dokun.');
  avatar.onclick=()=>open('profile');
  return true;
 }
 function decorateStreak(){
  const a=current(),body=document.getElementById('v5SheetBody');if(!a||!body||document.getElementById('v994StreakCompanion'))return false;
  const streak=Number(state?.streakMeta?.currentStreak??state?.streak??0)||0;
  body.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin','<div class="v994-streak-companion" id="v994StreakCompanion"><span class="animal" aria-hidden="true">'+a.emoji+'</span><div><b>'+esc(a.name)+' seninle · '+streak+' günlük seri</b><small>'+esc(a.trait)+' · Bugün de küçük bir adım yeter.</small></div></div>');
  return true;
 }
 function reaction(){
  const a=current(),rv=document.getElementById('resultView');if(!a||!rv?.classList.contains('show'))return false;
  rv.querySelector('#v994ResultCompanion')?.remove();
  const total=Number(session?.questions?.length)||0,pct=total?Math.round((Number(session?.correct)||0)/total*100):0;
  const perfect=pct===100;
  const msg=perfect?a.name+' seninle kutluyor!':pct>=70?a.name+' gururla yanında.':'Bir adım daha — '+a.name+' yanında.';
  const sub=perfect?'Kusursuz ders · '+a.trait:pct>=70?'Dersi tamamladın · '+a.trait:'Tekrar ettikçe güçlenir · '+a.trait;
  const card='<div class="v994-result-companion '+(perfect?'perfect':'')+'" id="v994ResultCompanion"><span class="animal" aria-hidden="true">'+a.emoji+'</span><div><b>'+esc(msg)+'</b><small>'+esc(sub)+'</small></div></div>';
  const subtitle=document.getElementById('resultSubtitle');
  if(subtitle)subtitle.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend',card);else rv.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin',card);
  return true;
 }
 function retireLegacyCharacterUi(){
  try{
   const ws=document.getElementById('worldScreen');ws?.classList.remove('v773-gated');
   document.getElementById('v773WorldGate')?.remove();
   document.querySelectorAll('#worldScreen .v760-launch').forEach(x=>x.remove());
   document.querySelectorAll('#worldScreen button,#worldScreen [role="button"]').forEach(x=>{
    if(x.closest?.('.v994-animal-grid'))return;
    const t=(x.textContent||'').trim();
    if(/AVATAR STUDIO|KARAKTERİNİ (OLUŞTUR|SEÇ)|KARAKTERİ DÜZENLE/i.test(t))x.style.display='none';
   });
  }catch(e){}
 }
 const BASE_ONBOARDING=typeof window.onboarding==='function'?window.onboarding:function(){};
 window.onboarding=function(step=0){
  ensure();
  if(step===0&&!current()){pickerMode='onboarding';return open('onboarding')}
  return BASE_ONBOARDING.apply(this,arguments);
 };
 document.addEventListener('deen:profile-rendered',()=>setTimeout(decorateProfile,0));
 document.addEventListener('deen:streak-rendered',()=>setTimeout(decorateStreak,0));
 document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-result',()=>setTimeout(reaction,0));
 document.addEventListener('deen:ui-updated',()=>{ensure();setTimeout(()=>{decorateProfile();retireLegacyCharacterUi()},20)});
 document.addEventListener('cancel',e=>{if(e.target?.id==='v5Sheet'&&e.target?.dataset?.companionRequired==='1'){e.preventDefault()}},true);
 function promptExisting(){
  ensure();retireLegacyCharacterUi();
  if(entryPromptShown||!state?.onboarded||current())return;
  entryPromptShown=true;setTimeout(()=>open('required'),450);
 }
 window.DEEN_COMPANION={version:VERSION,animals:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ANIMALS)),current:()=>current(),open,select,reaction};
 ensure();

<script id="deen-v995-world-retirement-js">
(()=>{
 window.__DEEN_WORLD_RETIRED_RUNTIME__=true;

 if(window.__deenV995WorldRetired)return;window.__deenV995WorldRetired=true;
 const VERSION='9.9.65-LEGACY-WORLD-AVATAR-QUIESCED';
 let busy=false,observer=null;
 function fallback(){
  const learn=document.getElementById('learnScreen');
  if(learn&&!document.querySelector('.screen.active:not(#worldScreen)'))learn.classList.add('active');
  const nav=document.querySelector('.bottomnav [data-screen="learnScreen"]');

 }
 function retire(){
  if(busy)return;busy=true;
  try{
   window.__DEEN_WORLD_RETIRED_RUNTIME__=true;
   window.__DEEN_LEGACY_AVATAR_RETIRED__=true;
   try{window.DEEN_WORLD_PROGRESS_STABLE?.stop?.()}catch(e){}

    '#v760Overlay,#v812Wardrobe,#v820RoomOverlay,#v820ShopOverlay,#v821AreasOverlay,#v821MemoriesOverlay,'+
    '.v760-profile-cta,.v760-launch,.v760-launch-tab,.v741-avatar-tab'
   ).forEach(x=>x.remove());

   const world=document.getElementById('worldScreen');
   if(world?.classList.contains('active')){
    world.classList.remove('active');
    try{BASE_NAVIGATE?.('learnScreen')}catch(e){fallback()}
   }
   if(world){world.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');world.setAttribute('inert','');}
   fallback();
  }finally{busy=false}
 }

 },true);
 function watch(){
  const nav=document.querySelector('.bottomnav');if(!nav||observer)return;
  observer=new MutationObserver(()=>setTimeout(retire,0));
  observer.observe(nav,{childList:true,subtree:true});
 }
 window.DEEN_WORLD_RETIREMENT={version:VERSION,retire,snapshot:()=>({
  worldNavCount:document.querySelectorAll('.bottomnav [data-screen="worldScreen"]').length,

  legacyAvatarUiCount:document.querySelectorAll('#v760Overlay,#v812Wardrobe,.v760-profile-cta,.v741-avatar-tab').length
 })};
 retire();watch();
 if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{retire();watch()},{once:true});
 [100,350,800,1500,3000].forEach(ms=>setTimeout(()=>{retire();watch()},ms));

<script id="deen-v996-onboarding-flow-js">
(()=>{

 };

 }
 function policy(){
  return '<div class="v690-policy-card"><span class="v690-safety-pill">ORTAK TEMEL ÖNCE</span><b>Başlangıçta mezhep seçimi istenmez.</b><p>DEEN önce mezheplerin ortak çekirdeğini öğretir. Mezhep-hassas içerikler uzman incelemesi önceliğini korur.</p></div>'+
  '<div class="v690-policy-card gold"><b>Dinî içerik ödeme duvarının arkasında değildir.</b><p>Temel öğrenme içeriği Free kullanıcılar için erişilebilir kalır.</p></div>';
 }
 function dots(step){return '<div class="v5-onboarddots">'+Array.from({length:7},(_,i)=>'<i class="'+(i===step?'active':'')+'"></i>').join('')+'</div>'}
 function footer(step){

  return primary('Devam et →','onboarding('+(step+1)+')')+(step?list('← Geri','onboarding('+(step-1)+')',''):'');
 }
 function render(step=0){
  ensure();step=Math.max(0,Math.min(6,Number(step)||0));
  const titles=['Her gün, biraz daha öğren.','Sana nasıl anlatalım?','Yol arkadaşını seç.','Kendine zaman ayır.','Küçük hedef, güçlü ritim.','Öğrenme döngünü tanı.','Hazırsın.'];

DEEN, uzman fetvası veya dinî danışmanlık yerine geçmez.</div>';
  if(step===1)b='<p>Yaşını yazmana gerek yok. Sana en rahat gelen anlatım biçimini seç.</p>'+ageMarkup();

  if(step===4)b='<p>Günlük hedef, öğrenme ritmini düzenler. İbadetler bu hedefe dahil edilmez ve puanlanmaz.</p>'+[[1,'Hafif'],[2,'Normal'],[3,'Yoğun']].map(([n,t])=>list(t+' · '+n+' mini ders','state.dailyGoal='+n+';saveState();onboarding(4)',state.dailyGoal===n?'✓':'○',state.dailyGoal===n?'selected':'')).join('');
  if(step===5)b='<div class="v5-learningloop"><p><b>01 · Öğren</b><br>Temel bilgiyi güvenli biçimde tanı.</p><p><b>02 · Anla & uygula</b><br>Kavramları ayır ve günlük durumlarda kullan.</p><p><b>03 · Hatırla & ustalaş</b><br>Aktif tekrar ve challenge ile pekiştir.</p></div><p>Ödüller yalnız öğrenme davranışını destekler; dinî değer veya ibadet sevabı ölçmez.</p>';
  if(step===6)b=policy()+'<div class="v690-access"><b>Free:</b> öğrenme yolu + tekrar.<br><b>Premium:</b> kullanım konforu ve gelişmiş özellikler.</div>';
  sheet(titles[step],dots(step)+b+footer(step));

 }
 function selectAge(id){

 function selectAnimal(id){

 }
 function finish(){
  ensure();if(!state.profile.companion)return render(2);
  state.onboarded=true;state.onboardingVersion=ONBOARDING_VERSION;
  try{saveState()}catch(e){}try{closeSheet()}catch(e){}
  try{updateUI()}catch(e){}try{scrollToCurrent('U01')}catch(e){}

 }
 window.onboarding=render;
 try{onboarding=render}catch(e){}
 window.finishOnboarding=finish;
 window.DEEN_ONBOARDING_FLOW={version:VERSION,render,selectAge,selectAnimal,nextAnimal,finish,snapshot:()=>({stepSheet:document.getElementById('v5Sheet')?.dataset?.onboardingFlow||null,age:state.learningProfile||null,ageChosen:!!state.onboardingAgeChosen,animal:state.profile?.companion||null,onboarded:!!state.onboarded})};

#profileScreenBody .v760-profile-cta{display:none!important}

<script id="deen-v997-profile-companion-sync-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV997ProfileCompanionSync)return;window.__deenV997ProfileCompanionSync=true;
 const VERSION='9.9.52-PROFILE-COMPANION-LOOP-FIX';
 let observer=null,busy=false;
 function companion(){
  try{
   const c=window.DEEN_COMPANION?.current?.();
   if(c?.emoji)return c;
  }catch(e){}
  const id=String(state?.profile?.companion||'');
  const all=window.DEEN_COMPANION?.animals||{};
  return all[id]?{id,...all[id]}:null;
 }
 function candidates(root){
  return [
   root.querySelector('.v5-profilehead .v5-avatar.large'),
   root.querySelector('.v5-profilehead .v742-profile-avatar'),
   root.querySelector('.v742-profile-avatar'),
   root.querySelector('[data-profile-avatar]'),
   root.querySelector('.v5-profilehead > span:first-child')
  ].filter(Boolean);
 }
 function configure(el,a){
  el.classList.add('v997-companion-avatar','v994-companion-avatar');
  el.title=a.name+' · '+a.trait;
  el.setAttribute('aria-label','Yol arkadaşın '+a.name+'. Değiştirmek için dokun.');
  el.onclick=e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();try{window.DEEN_COMPANION?.open?.('profile')}catch(_){}};
 }
 function ensureFallback(el,a){
  /*
    Once the real companion asset runtime owns this avatar, this legacy sync
    layer must NEVER replace its children. Previously it rewrote innerHTML on
    every observer callback, causing PNG -> emoji -> observer -> emoji loops.
  */
  if(window.__DEEN_COMPANION_ASSET_MODE)return false;
  const existing=el.querySelector(':scope > .v997-animal');
  if(existing && el.children.length===1 && existing.textContent===a.emoji)return false;
  const span=document.createElement('span');
  span.className='v997-animal';
  span.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
  span.textContent=a.emoji;
  el.replaceChildren(span);
  return true;
 }
 function apply(){
  if(busy)return false;busy=true;
  try{
   const a=companion(),root=document.getElementById('profileScreenBody');
   if(!a||!root)return false;

   // Removing a legacy CTA is idempotent; subsequent observer passes do nothing.
   root.querySelectorAll('.v760-profile-cta').forEach(x=>x.remove());

   const seen=new Set(),els=candidates(root).filter(el=>!seen.has(el)&&(seen.add(el),true));
   if(!els.length)return false;

   for(const el of els){
    configure(el,a);
    ensureFallback(el,a);
   }
   return true;
  }finally{busy=false}
 }
 let scheduleTimers=[];
 function schedule(){
  scheduleTimers.forEach(id=>clearTimeout(id));
  scheduleTimers=[0,80,220].map(ms=>setTimeout(()=>{try{apply()}catch(e){}},ms));
 }
 document.addEventListener('deen:profile-rendered',schedule);
 document.addEventListener('deen:ui-updated',schedule);
 document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const nav=e.target?.closest?.('.navbtn[data-screen="profileScreen"]');
  if(nav)schedule();
 },true);
 function watch(){
  const root=document.getElementById('profileScreenBody');
  if(!root||!('MutationObserver' in window))return;
  try{observer?.disconnect?.()}catch(_){}
  observer=new MutationObserver(mutations=>{
   if(busy)return;
   // Only react to actual child-tree changes. Attribute changes from configure()
   // are deliberately not observed, preventing self-triggered synchronization.
   if(!mutations.some(m=>m.type==='childList'&&(m.addedNodes.length||m.removedNodes.length)))return;
   queueMicrotask(()=>{try{apply()}catch(e){}});
  });
  observer.observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});
 }
 window.DEEN_PROFILE_COMPANION_SYNC={version:VERSION,apply,snapshot:()=>({
  companion:companion()?.id||null,
  assetMode:!!window.__DEEN_COMPANION_ASSET_MODE,
  profileImage:!!document.querySelector('#profileScreenBody .deen-companion-profile-img'),
  visibleEmoji:document.querySelector('#profileScreenBody .v997-animal')?.textContent||null,
  oldAvatarSvgCount:document.querySelectorAll('#profileScreenBody .v997-companion-avatar svg').length,
  legacyProfileCtaCount:document.querySelectorAll('#profileScreenBody .v760-profile-cta').length
 })};
 schedule();watch();
 if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{watch();schedule()},{once:true});
 setTimeout(()=>{watch();schedule()},700);
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v998-premium-onboarding-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV998PremiumOnboarding)return;window.__deenV998PremiumOnboarding=true;
 const VERSION='9.9.59-PREMIUM-ONBOARDING-6-SCREEN',ONBOARDING_VERSION=5;
 const AGES={
  child:{label:'Çocuk',range:'8–12',icon:'📚',desc:'Kısa, sade ve görsel ağırlıklı anlatım'},
  teen:{label:'Genç',range:'13–17',icon:'🎓',desc:'Açık, akıcı ve günlük hayata yakın örnekler'},
  adult:{label:'Yetişkin',range:'18+',icon:'📖',desc:'Daha derin, dengeli ve düzenli açıklamalar'}
 };
 const ANIMALS=window.DEEN_COMPANION?.animals||{
  lion:{emoji:'🦁',name:'Aslan',trait:'Cesaret'},wolf:{emoji:'🐺',name:'Kurt',trait:'Kararlılık'},owl:{emoji:'🦉',name:'Baykuş',trait:'Öğrenme / Bilgelik'},turtle:{emoji:'🐢',name:'Kaplumbağa',trait:'Sabır'},eagle:{emoji:'🦅',name:'Kartal',trait:'Odak'},cat:{emoji:'🐈',name:'Kedi',trait:'Sakinlik'}
 };
 const GOAL_BY_MINUTES={5:1,10:2,15:3};
 const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,m=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[m]));

 function ensure(){
  state.profile=state.profile&&typeof state.profile==='object'?state.profile:{};
  if(!String(state.profile.name||'').trim())state.profile.name='Yolcu';
  if(!AGES[state.learningProfile])state.learningProfile='teen';
  if(!ANIMALS[state.profile.companion])state.profile.companion=null;
  if(![5,10,15].includes(Number(state.minutes)))state.minutes=10;
  state.dailyGoal=GOAL_BY_MINUTES[Number(state.minutes)]||2;
 }

 function close(){
  document.getElementById('v998Onboarding')?.remove();
  try{document.getElementById('v5Sheet')?.close?.()}catch(e){}
 }

 function brand(){return '<div class="v998-brand"><span class="v998-brand-mark"></span><span>DEEN</span></div>'}
 function hero(extra=''){return '<div class="v998-hero '+extra+'"><div class="v998-skyline"></div><i class="v998-lantern left"></i><i class="v998-lantern right"></i></div>'}
 function dots(step){return '<div class="v998-dots">'+Array.from({length:6},(_,i)=>'<i class="'+(i===step?'on':'')+'"></i>').join('')+'</div>'}
 function primary(label,action){return '<button class="v998-primary" type="button" onclick="'+action+'"><span>'+label+'</span></button>'}

 function frame(step,content,{back=true,dotsOn=true,bottom=''}={}){
  close();
  const root=document.createElement('div');
  root.id='v998Onboarding';
  root.dataset.step=String(step);
  root.dataset.totalSteps='6';
  root.innerHTML='<div class="v998-shell"><div class="v998-screen"><div class="v998-top">'+
    (back?'<button class="v998-back" type="button" onclick="onboarding('+(step-1)+')" aria-label="Geri">‹</button>':'')+
    brand()+'</div>'+content+(dotsOn?dots(step):'')+'<div class="v998-bottom">'+bottom+'</div></div></div>';
  document.body.appendChild(root);
  return root;
 }

 function ageCards(){
  return '<div class="v998-list">'+Object.entries(AGES).map(([id,a])=>
    '<button class="v998-choice '+(state.learningProfile===id?'selected':'')+'" type="button" onclick="DEEN_PREMIUM_ONBOARDING.age(\''+id+'\')">'+
    '<span class="choice-ico">'+a.icon+'</span><span><b>'+a.label+' ('+a.range+')</b><small>'+a.desc+'</small></span><span class="v998-radio"></span></button>'
  ).join('')+'</div>';
 }

 function animalCards(){
  return '<div class="v998-animal-grid">'+Object.entries(ANIMALS).map(([id,a])=>
    '<button data-animal="'+id+'" class="v998-animal '+(state.profile.companion===id?'selected':'')+'" type="button" onclick="DEEN_PREMIUM_ONBOARDING.animal(\''+id+'\')">'+
    '<span class="check"></span><span class="emoji">'+a.emoji+'</span><b>'+esc(a.name)+'</b><small>'+esc(a.trait)+'</small></button>'
  ).join('')+'</div>';
 }

 function timeCards(){
  return '<div class="v998-list">'+[5,10,15].map(n=>
    '<button class="v998-choice '+(Number(state.minutes)===n?'selected':'')+'" type="button" onclick="DEEN_PREMIUM_ONBOARDING.minutes('+n+')">'+
    '<span class="choice-ico">◷</span><span><b>'+n+' dakika</b><small>'+
    (n===5?'Hızlı ve hafif tempo · 1 mini ders':n===10?'Dengeli günlük ritim · 2 mini ders':'Daha yoğun öğrenme · 3 mini ders')+
    '</small></span><span class="v998-radio"></span></button>'
  ).join('')+'</div>';
 }

 function summary(){
  const a=ANIMALS[state.profile.companion]||ANIMALS.owl;
  const p=AGES[state.learningProfile]||AGES.teen;
  return '<div class="v998-summary">'+
   [['♙','İsim',esc(state.profile.name||'Yolcu')],
    ['⌂','Anlatım',p.label],
    ['✦','Yol arkadaşı',a.name],
    ['◷','Günlük süre',state.minutes+' dakika'],
    ['◎','Günlük hedef',state.dailyGoal+' mini ders']]
   .map(x=>'<div class="v998-summary-row"><span class="ico">'+x[0]+'</span><span>'+x[1]+':</span><b>'+x[2]+'</b></div>').join('')+
  '</div>';
 }

 function render(step=0){
  ensure();
  // Hard prerequisite: even a direct onboarding(5) must ask for language first.
  if(state?.settings?.onboardingLanguageConfirmed!==true)
    return window.DEEN_LANGUAGE_ONBOARDING?.render?.()||false;
  step=Math.max(0,Math.min(5,Number(step)||0));

  if(step===0)return frame(0,
    hero('v9912-login-hero')+
    '<div class="v998-copy"><h1><span class="mint">DEEN</span>’e hoş geldin</h1><p>İslam’ı adım adım, sakin ve düzenli şekilde öğren.</p></div>'+
    '<div class="v9912-authmock"><p class="v9912-auth-title">Hesabını koru, ilerlemeni kaybetme</p>'+
    '<button type="button" class="v9912-authbtn google" onclick="DEEN_AUTH_MOCK.notice(\'Google\')"><span class="v9912-authico g">G</span><b>Google ile devam et</b></button>'+
    '<button type="button" class="v9912-authbtn apple" onclick="DEEN_AUTH_MOCK.notice(\'Apple\')"><span class="v9912-authico applemark">●</span><b>Apple ile devam et</b></button>'+
    '<button type="button" class="v9912-authbtn email" onclick="DEEN_AUTH_MOCK.notice(\'E-posta\')"><span class="v9912-authico">✉</span><b>E-posta ile devam et</b></button>'+
    '<div class="v9912-or"><span></span><em>veya</em><span></span></div></div>',
    {back:false,bottom:primary('Başlayalım','onboarding(1)')+'<button class="v998-secondary" type="button" onclick="DEEN_PREMIUM_ONBOARDING.skip()">Daha sonra</button>'}
  );

  if(step===1)return frame(1,
    hero('v998-minihero')+
    '<div class="v998-copy"><h1>Sana nasıl hitap edelim?</h1><p>İstersen gerçek adını, istersen kısa bir kullanıcı adı yazabilirsin.</p></div>'+
    '<label class="v998-field"><span class="ico">♙</span><input id="v998Name" maxlength="24" autocomplete="name" placeholder="Adın veya kullanıcı adın" value="'+esc(state.profile.name==='Yolcu'?'':state.profile.name)+'"></label>'+
    '<p class="v998-helper">Bu isim profilinde görünecek.</p>',
    {bottom:primary('Devam et','DEEN_PREMIUM_ONBOARDING.nameNext()')}
  );

  if(step===2)return frame(2,
    hero('v998-minihero')+
    '<div class="v998-copy"><h1>Sana en uygun <span class="mint">anlatımı</span> seçelim</h1><p>Yaşını yazmana gerek yok; sana en rahat gelen öğrenme stilini seç.</p></div>'+
    ageCards(),
    {bottom:primary('Devam et','onboarding(3)')}
  );

  if(step===3)return frame(3,
    '<div class="v998-copy" style="margin-top:28px"><h1>Yol arkadaşını seç</h1><p>Seçtiğin hayvan profilinde, serilerinde ve ders sonunda sana eşlik edecek.</p></div>'+
    animalCards(),
    {bottom:primary(state.profile.companion?'Devam et':'Önce bir hayvan seç','DEEN_PREMIUM_ONBOARDING.animalNext()')}
  );

  if(step===4)return frame(4,
    hero('v998-minihero')+
    '<div class="v998-copy"><h1>Günde ne kadar zaman ayırabilirsin?</h1><p>Kısa ama düzenli ilerlemek daha iyi sonuç verir.</p></div>'+
    timeCards()+
    '<p class="v998-helper">ⓘ Günlük mini ders hedefin seçtiğin süreye göre otomatik ayarlanır. Bunu daha sonra değiştirebilirsin.</p>',
    {bottom:primary('Devam et','onboarding(5)')}
  );

  const a=ANIMALS[state.profile.companion]||ANIMALS.owl;
  return frame(5,
    hero('v998-minihero').replace('</div>','<span class="v998-companion-hero" data-animal="'+(state.profile.companion||'owl')+'"></span></div>')+
    '<div class="v998-copy"><span class="v998-tag">✓ KURULUM TAMAMLANDI</span><h1>Hazırsın</h1><p>Artık öğrenmeye başlayabilirsin.</p></div>'+
    summary(),
    {dotsOn:false,bottom:primary('Başla','DEEN_PREMIUM_ONBOARDING.finish()')+'<button class="v998-secondary" type="button" onclick="onboarding(1)">Düzenle</button>'}
  );
 }

 function nameNext(){
  ensure();
  const input=document.getElementById('v998Name');
  state.profile.name=String(input?.value||'').trim().slice(0,24)||'Yolcu';
  try{saveState()}catch(e){}
  render(2);
 }

 function age(id){
  if(!AGES[id])return;
  state.learningProfile=id;
  state.onboardingAgeChosen=true;
  try{window.DEEN_AGE_PROFILE?.set?.(id,{persist:false,refresh:false})}catch(e){}
  try{saveState()}catch(e){}
  render(2);
 }

 function animal(id){
  if(!ANIMALS[id])return;
  state.profile.companion=id;
  try{saveState()}catch(e){}
  render(3);
 }

 function animalNext(){
  if(!state.profile.companion){
    try{toast?.('Devam etmek için bir yol arkadaşı seç.')}catch(e){}
    return;
  }
  render(4);
 }

 function minutes(n){
  n=Number(n);
  if(![5,10,15].includes(n))return;
  state.minutes=n;
  state.dailyGoal=GOAL_BY_MINUTES[n];
  try{saveState()}catch(e){}
  render(4);
 }

 // Kept only for compatibility with any stale inline handler from an older cached screen.
 function goal(n){
  n=Math.max(1,Math.min(3,Number(n)||2));
  state.dailyGoal=n;
  state.minutes=n===1?5:n===3?15:10;
  try{saveState()}catch(e){}
  render(4);
 }

 function finish(){
  if(state?.settings?.onboardingLanguageConfirmed!==true)return window.DEEN_LANGUAGE_ONBOARDING?.render?.()||false;
  ensure();
  if(!state.profile.companion)return render(3);
  state.onboarded=true;
  state.onboardingVersion=ONBOARDING_VERSION;
  try{saveState()}catch(e){}
  close();
  try{updateUI()}catch(e){}
  try{window.DEEN_PROFILE_COMPANION_SYNC?.apply?.()}catch(e){}
  try{window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS?.renderProfile?.()}catch(e){}
  try{scrollToCurrent('U01')}catch(e){}
 }

 function skip(){
  if(state?.settings?.onboardingLanguageConfirmed!==true)return window.DEEN_LANGUAGE_ONBOARDING?.render?.()||false;
  ensure();
  state.profile.name=state.profile.name||'Yolcu';
  state.learningProfile=AGES[state.learningProfile]?state.learningProfile:'teen';
  state.profile.companion=state.profile.companion||'owl';
  state.minutes=10;
  state.dailyGoal=2;
  finish();
 }

 window.onboarding=render;
 try{onboarding=render}catch(e){}
 window.finishOnboarding=finish;
 window.DEEN_PREMIUM_ONBOARDING={
  version:VERSION,
  render,nameNext,age,animal,animalNext,minutes,goal,finish,skip,
  snapshot:()=>({
    name:state.profile?.name,
    age:state.learningProfile,
    animal:state.profile?.companion,
    minutes:state.minutes,
    goal:state.dailyGoal,
    onboarded:state.onboarded,
    version:state.onboardingVersion,
    step:Number(document.getElementById('v998Onboarding')?.dataset?.step||-1),
    totalSteps:6
  })
 };
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9913-mobile-lesson-fit-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9913MobileLessonFit)return;window.__deenV9913MobileLessonFit=true;
 const VERSION='9.9.56-MOBILE-LAYOUT-EVENTS';
 let raf=0,fitCount=0;
 const mobile=()=>matchMedia('(max-width:499px)').matches;

 function compactHeader(){
   if(!mobile())return;
   const close=document.querySelector('#lesson .close');
   if(close){close.setAttribute('aria-label','Dersten çık');close.title='Dersten çık'}
   const status=document.getElementById('lessonStatus');
   if(status&&session&&!session.final){
     const e=state?.premium?'∞':Math.max(0,Number(state?.energy)||0);
     const wanted=String(e);
     const energy=document.getElementById('lessonEnergy');
     if(!energy||energy.textContent!==wanted){
       status.innerHTML='<span aria-hidden="true">♥</span><span id="lessonEnergy">'+wanted+'</span>';
     }
   }
   const streak=document.getElementById('lessonStreakMini');
   const wantedStreak=String(Math.max(0,Number(state?.streakMeta?.currentStreak??state?.streak)||0));
   if(streak&&streak.textContent!==wantedStreak)streak.textContent=wantedStreak;
 }

 function notes(){
   if(!mobile())return;
   document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .v710-branch-preview.show').forEach(el=>{
     if(el.dataset.v9913Bound)return;
     el.dataset.v9913Bound='1';
     el.title='Ayrıntıyı aç / kapat';
     el.addEventListener('click',()=>{
       el.classList.toggle('v9913-note-expanded');
       schedule('note');
     });
   });
 }

 function resetFrame(){
   const f=document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');
   if(!f)return;
   f.style.transform='';
   f.style.width='';
   f.style.maxWidth='';
   document.getElementById('lesson')?.classList.remove('v9913-fitted');
 }

 function fit(){
   raf=0;
   const lesson=document.getElementById('lesson');
   const area=document.querySelector('#lesson .qarea');
   const frame=document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');

   if(!mobile()||!lesson?.classList.contains('active')||!area||!frame){
     resetFrame();
     return false;
   }

   compactHeader();
   notes();

   frame.style.transform='';
   frame.style.width='100%';
   frame.style.maxWidth='none';
   lesson.classList.remove('v9913-fitted');

   /*
     v10.1.7: matching owns its viewport from render time.
     Detect it from session data, not from a class that is added later.
   */
   const activeQuestion=(()=>{try{return session?.questions?.[session?.index]||null}catch(_){return null}})();
   const activeType=String(activeQuestion?.activity_type||activeQuestion?.question_type||activeQuestion?.type||'').toLowerCase();
   if(activeType==='matching'||frame.classList.contains('v1017-match-screen')){
     frame.style.transform='';
     frame.style.width='100%';
     frame.style.maxWidth='none';
     lesson.classList.remove('v9913-fitted');
     fitCount++;
     return true;
   }

   /*
     Feedback has its own scroll container in v9.9.55. Never scale the whole
     lesson after grading; scaling made long explanations tiny and triggered
     another round of geometry changes.
   */
   if(frame.classList.contains('v9945-feedback-mode')){
     fitCount++;
     return true;
   }

   const available=Math.max(1,area.clientHeight-2);
   const needed=Math.max(1,frame.scrollHeight);
   let scale=Math.min(1,available/needed);

   if(scale<.995){
     scale=Math.max(.64,scale);
     frame.style.width=(100/scale).toFixed(3)+'%';
     frame.style.transform='scale('+scale.toFixed(4)+')';
     lesson.classList.add('v9913-fitted');
   }

   fitCount++;
   return true;
 }

 function schedule(){
   cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
   raf=requestAnimationFrame(fit);
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',schedule);
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',schedule);
 document.addEventListener('deen:companion-rendered',schedule);
 document.addEventListener('deen:layout-dirty',schedule);
 window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});
 window.visualViewport?.addEventListener?.('resize',schedule,{passive:true});

 window.DEEN_MOBILE_LESSON={
   version:VERSION,
   fit:schedule,
   snapshot:()=>({
     mobile:mobile(),
     lessonActive:!!document.getElementById('lesson')?.classList.contains('active'),
     viewport:{w:window.innerWidth,h:window.innerHeight,vh:window.visualViewport?.height||null},
     questionScale:(()=>{const t=document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame')?.style.transform||'';const m=t.match(/scale\(([^)]+)\)/);return m?Number(m[1]):1})(),
     fitCount,
     eventDriven:true,
     observer:false
   })
 };

 schedule();
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9914-ultra-clean-lesson-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9914UltraCleanLesson)return;window.__deenV9914UltraCleanLesson=true;
 const VERSION='9.9.14-ULTRA-CLEAN-LESSON';
 const mobile=()=>true;

 function questionProgress(){
   const total=Math.max(1,Number(session?.questions?.length)||1);
   const index=Math.max(0,Number(session?.index)||0);
   return Math.min(100,Math.max(0,((index+1)/total)*100));
 }
 function mountControls(){
   if(!mobile())return;
   const frame=document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');
   if(!frame)return;
   let exit=document.getElementById('v9914ExitLesson');
   if(!exit){
     exit=document.createElement('button');
     exit.id='v9914ExitLesson';
     exit.type='button';
     exit.innerHTML='‹';
     exit.setAttribute('aria-label','Dersten çık');
     exit.title='Dersten çık';
     exit.onclick=e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();try{closeLesson()}catch(_){window.closeLesson?.()}};
   }
   if(exit.parentElement!==frame)frame.prepend(exit);

   let prog=document.getElementById('v9914MiniProgress');
   if(!prog){
     prog=document.createElement('div');
     prog.id='v9914MiniProgress';
     prog.setAttribute('aria-label','Ders ilerlemesi');
     prog.innerHTML='<i></i>';
   }
   if(prog.parentElement!==frame)frame.prepend(prog);
   const pct=questionProgress();
   prog.querySelector('i')?.style.setProperty('width',pct+'%');
   prog.setAttribute('aria-valuenow',String(Math.round(pct)));
 }

 function simplifyCopy(){
   if(!mobile())return;
   document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .v730-presenter').forEach(el=>el.style.display='none');
   document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .v5-term').forEach(el=>el.style.display='none');

   const strong=document.querySelector('#lesson .audio-copy strong');
   if(strong)strong.textContent='Dinle';

   document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .v5-source summary').forEach(s=>{
     s.setAttribute('aria-label','Kaynak ve içerik notu');
     s.title='Kaynak ve içerik notu';
   });
 }

 function clean(){
   if(!mobile())return;
   mountControls();
   simplifyCopy();
   try{window.DEEN_MOBILE_LESSON?.fit?.()}catch(e){}
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9914',event?.detail,clean,{"raf":false,"delays":[0,70]}))clean();
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9914',event?.detail,clean,{"raf":false,"delays":[0]}))clean();
 });

 window.DEEN_CLEAN_LESSON={
   version:VERSION,
   apply:clean,
   snapshot:()=>({
     mobile:mobile(),
     headerVisible:getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('#lesson .lesson-head')||document.body).display!=='none',
     exitInsideCard:document.getElementById('v9914ExitLesson')?.parentElement?.classList?.contains('question-frame')||false,
     presenterVisible:!!document.querySelector('#lesson .v730-presenter')&&getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('#lesson .v730-presenter')).display!=='none',
     sourceCompact:!!document.querySelector('#lesson .v5-source summary')
   })
 };
 setTimeout(clean,500);
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9916-duo-question-templates-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9916DuoTemplates)return;window.__deenV9916DuoTemplates=true;
 const VERSION='9.9.16-DUO-TEMPLATES';
 const SPEAK_TYPES=new Set(['speaking','speak','speaking_repeat','recitation_repeat']);

 const qNow=()=>session?.questions?.[session?.index];
 const frame=()=>document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');
 const area=()=>document.getElementById('answerArea');

 function titleFor(q){
   if(!q)return 'Soruyu cevapla';
   const t=q.activity_type;
   if(t==='matching')return 'Eşleşen çiftleri bul';
   if(t==='fill_blank')return 'Cümleyi tamamla';
   if(t==='listening_select'||t==='listening_meaning')return 'Dinle ve cevapla';
   if(SPEAK_TYPES.has(t))return 'Bu cümleyi söyle';
   if(t==='build_sequence')return 'Doğru sıraya koy';
   if(t==='true_false'||t==='speed_review')return 'Doğru mu, yanlış mı?';
   return 'Doğru cevabı seç';
 }

 function companionEmoji(){
   try{return window.DEEN_COMPANION?.current?.()?.emoji||'🦉'}catch(e){return '🦉'}
 }

 function setClass(q){
   const f=frame();if(!f)return;
   f.classList.remove('deen-template-match','deen-template-fill','deen-template-listen','deen-template-speak','deen-template-choice');
   if(q?.activity_type==='matching')f.classList.add('deen-template-match');
   else if(q?.activity_type==='fill_blank')f.classList.add('deen-template-fill');
   else if(q?.activity_type==='listening_select'||q?.activity_type==='listening_meaning')f.classList.add('deen-template-listen');
   else if(SPEAK_TYPES.has(q?.activity_type))f.classList.add('deen-template-speak');
   else f.classList.add('deen-template-choice');
 }

 function chrome(q){
   const f=frame();if(!f)return;
   let title=f.querySelector('.v9916-task-title');
   if(!title){
     title=document.createElement('h2');
     title.className='v9916-task-title';
     const exit=document.getElementById('v9914ExitLesson');
     const progress=document.getElementById('v9914MiniProgress');
     if(progress?.nextSibling)f.insertBefore(title,progress.nextSibling);
     else if(exit?.nextSibling)f.insertBefore(title,exit.nextSibling);
     else f.prepend(title);
   }
   title.textContent=titleFor(q);

   let energy=document.getElementById('v9916MiniEnergy');
   if(!energy){
     energy=document.createElement('div');
     energy.id='v9916MiniEnergy';
     energy.setAttribute('aria-label','Enerji');
     f.prepend(energy);
   }
   const value=state?.premium?'∞':Math.max(0,Number(state?.energy)||0);
   energy.innerHTML='<i>♥</i><span>'+value+'</span>';
 }

 function decorateMatch(q){
   const a=area();if(!a)return;
   a.querySelectorAll('.match-column-title,.matching-progress').forEach(el=>el.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true'));
   [...a.querySelectorAll('.match-left')].forEach((b,i)=>b.style.gridRow=String(i+1));
   [...a.querySelectorAll('.match-right')].forEach((b,i)=>b.style.gridRow=String(i+1));
 }

 function decorateFill(q){
   const a=area();if(!a)return;
   a.querySelectorAll('.fill-choice').forEach(b=>{
     const badge=b.querySelector('.fill-choice-badge');if(badge)badge.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
   });
 }

 function decorateListen(q){
   const f=frame(),a=area();if(!f||!a)return;
   const original=document.getElementById('questionText');
   original?.classList.add('v9916-original-hidden');
   a.querySelectorAll('.v9916-listen-top,.v9916-listen-question').forEach(x=>x.remove());

   const audio=a.querySelector('.audio-panel');
   if(audio){
     const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='v9916-listen-top';
     const avatar=document.createElement('div');avatar.className='v9916-listen-avatar';avatar.textContent=companionEmoji();avatar.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
     row.append(avatar,audio);

     const nativeAudio=audio.querySelector('audio');
     if(nativeAudio){
       const slow=document.createElement('button');slow.type='button';slow.className='v9916-slow';slow.textContent='YAVAŞ';
       slow.onclick=()=>{nativeAudio.playbackRate=.75;try{nativeAudio.currentTime=0;nativeAudio.play()}catch(e){}};
       audio.append(slow);
     }
     a.prepend(row);
   }

   const prompt=document.createElement('div');prompt.className='v9916-listen-question';prompt.textContent=q?.question_tr||'Dinlediğine göre doğru cevabı seç.';
   const firstChoice=a.querySelector('.options,.mc-standard,.v710-listen-options,.option');
   if(firstChoice&&firstChoice.parentElement===a)a.insertBefore(prompt,firstChoice);else a.append(prompt);
 }

 function skipUnscored(){
   if(!session?.questions?.length)return;
   session.questions.splice(session.index,1);
   if(session.index>=session.questions.length){
     if(typeof showResult==='function')showResult();
   }else{
     renderQuestion();
   }
 }

 function renderSpeak(q){
   const a=area(),original=document.getElementById('questionText');if(!a)return;
   original?.classList.add('v9916-original-hidden');
   a.innerHTML='';
   const target=String(q?.speak_text||q?.correct_answer||q?.question_tr||'').trim();

   const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='v9916-speak-row';
   const avatar=document.createElement('div');avatar.className='v9916-speak-avatar';avatar.textContent=companionEmoji();
   const bubble=document.createElement('div');bubble.className='v9916-speech-bubble';bubble.textContent=target;
   row.append(avatar,bubble);

   const mic=document.createElement('button');mic.type='button';mic.className='v9916-mic';mic.innerHTML='🎙 &nbsp; KONUŞ';
   const skip=document.createElement('button');skip.type='button';skip.className='v9916-skip-speak';skip.textContent='Şimdi konuşamıyorum';
   skip.onclick=skipUnscored;

   mic.onclick=()=>{
     const SR=window.SpeechRecognition||window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
     if(!SR){
       mic.textContent='Bu tarayıcı konuşma tanımayı desteklemiyor';
       setTimeout(()=>mic.innerHTML='🎙 &nbsp; KONUŞ',1600);
       return;
     }
     const rec=new SR();
     rec.lang=q?.speech_lang||'tr-TR';rec.interimResults=false;rec.maxAlternatives=1;
     mic.disabled=true;mic.textContent='Dinliyorum…';
     rec.onresult=e=>{
       const heard=String(e.results?.[0]?.[0]?.transcript||'').trim();
       mic.disabled=false;mic.textContent=heard?'Duyulan: '+heard:'Tekrar dene';
       if(heard){
         const note=document.createElement('div');note.className='v9916-listen-question';note.textContent='Telaffuz alıştırması tamamlandı. Bu aktivite şu aşamada puanlanmıyor.';
         a.insertBefore(note,skip);
         skip.textContent='Devam et';
       }
     };
     rec.onerror=()=>{mic.disabled=false;mic.innerHTML='🎙 &nbsp; TEKRAR DENE'};
     try{rec.start()}catch(e){mic.disabled=false}
   };

   a.append(row,mic,skip);
 }

 function apply(){
   const q=qNow(),f=frame();if(!q||!f)return;
   document.getElementById('questionText')?.classList.remove('v9916-original-hidden');
   setClass(q);chrome(q);
   if(q.activity_type==='matching')decorateMatch(q);
   else if(q.activity_type==='fill_blank')decorateFill(q);
   else if(q.activity_type==='listening_select'||q.activity_type==='listening_meaning')decorateListen(q);
   else if(SPEAK_TYPES.has(q.activity_type))renderSpeak(q);
   try{window.DEEN_CLEAN_LESSON?.apply?.()}catch(e){}
   try{window.DEEN_MOBILE_LESSON?.fit?.()}catch(e){}
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9916',event?.detail,apply,{"raf":true,"delays":[70]}))apply();
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9916',event?.detail,apply,{"raf":false,"delays":[0]}))apply();
 });

 window.DEEN_QUESTION_TEMPLATES={
   version:VERSION,
   apply,
   titleFor,
   supported:['matching','fill_blank','listening_select','listening_meaning','speaking'],
   snapshot:()=>({
     type:qNow()?.activity_type||null,
     template:[...frame()?.classList||[]].find(x=>x.startsWith('deen-template-'))||null,
     title:frame()?.querySelector('.v9916-task-title')?.textContent||null,
     energy:document.getElementById('v9916MiniEnergy')?.textContent||null
   })
 };
 setTimeout(apply,600);
})();
</script>
