<script id="deen-v9918-character-question-row-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9918CharacterQuestionRow)return;window.__deenV9918CharacterQuestionRow=true;
 const VERSION='9.9.18-CHARACTER-QUESTION';
 const TYPES=new Set([
   'multiple_choice','scenario','missing_item','error_spotting','progressive_recall',
   'true_false','speed_review','listening_select','listening_meaning','next_word','fill_blank'
 ]);
 const qNow=()=>session?.questions?.[session?.index]||null;
 const frame=()=>document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');
 const area=()=>document.getElementById('answerArea');

 function companion(){
   try{
     const c=window.DEEN_COMPANION?.current?.();
     if(c?.emoji)return c.emoji;
   }catch(e){}
   const id=String(state?.profile?.companion||'').toLowerCase();
   return ({lion:'🦁',wolf:'🐺',owl:'🦉',turtle:'🐢',eagle:'🦅',cat:'🐈'})[id]||'🦉';
 }

 function listening(q){
   const t=String(q?.activity_type||'');
   return t==='listening_select'||t==='listening_meaning';
 }

 function clear(){
   const f=frame();
   f?.classList.remove('deen-character-question-mode');
   f?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-row')?.remove();
 }

 function audioTrigger(){
   const panel=document.querySelector('#lesson .audio-panel');
   if(!panel)return null;
   const audio=panel.querySelector('audio');
   const play=panel.querySelector('button,.audio-play');
   return ()=>{
     if(audio){
       try{audio.currentTime=0;audio.play()}catch(e){}
       return;
     }
     if(play){
       try{play.click()}catch(e){}
     }
   };
 }

 function mount(q){
   const f=frame(),a=area();
   if(!f||!a||!q)return;
   const type=String(q.activity_type||'');
   if(!TYPES.has(type)){clear();return}

   f.classList.add('deen-character-question-mode');
   f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-row')?.remove();

   const row=document.createElement('div');
   row.className='deen-character-question-row';

   const avatar=document.createElement('div');
   avatar.className='deen-character-question-avatar';
   avatar.textContent=companion();
   avatar.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');

   const bubble=document.createElement('div');
   bubble.className='deen-character-question-bubble';

   const text=document.createElement('div');
   text.className='deen-character-question-text';
   let questionText=String(q.question_tr||q.prompt_tr||q.question||'').trim();
   if(type==='fill_blank')questionText=questionText.replace(/^\s*Boşluğu\s+tamamla\s*:\s*/i,'');
   text.textContent=questionText;
   bubble.appendChild(text);

   if(listening(q)){
     const trigger=audioTrigger();
     if(trigger){
       const b=document.createElement('button');
       b.type='button';
       b.className='deen-character-question-audio';
       b.textContent='🔊';
       b.setAttribute('aria-label','Dinle');
       b.title='Dinle';
       b.onclick=e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();trigger()};
       bubble.appendChild(b);
     }
   }

   row.append(avatar,bubble);
   f.insertBefore(row,a);
 }

 function apply(){
   const q=qNow();
   if(!q){clear();return}
   mount(q);
   try{window.DEEN_CLEAN_LESSON?.apply?.()}catch(e){}
   try{window.DEEN_MOBILE_LESSON?.fit?.()}catch(e){}
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9918',event?.detail,apply,{"raf":true,"delays":[60]}))apply();
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',event=>{
  /*
    Do not rebuild the question row after grading. mount() removes the whole
    row, which also destroys the runtime companion <img>. The question has not
    changed here, so preserve the existing DOM and only repair if the row is
    genuinely missing.
  */
  const preserve=()=>{
    const f=frame();
    const row=f?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-row');
    if(!row)return apply();
    f.classList.add('deen-character-question-mode');
    try{window.DEEN_MOBILE_LESSON?.fit?.()}catch(e){}
    return true;
  };
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9918-grade-preserve',event?.detail,preserve,{"raf":true,"delays":[80]}))requestAnimationFrame(preserve);
 });

 window.DEEN_CHARACTER_QUESTION={
   version:VERSION,
   apply,
   supported:[...TYPES],
   snapshot:()=>({
     type:qNow()?.activity_type||null,
     active:!!frame()?.classList.contains('deen-character-question-mode'),
     question:frame()?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-text')?.textContent||null,
     character:frame()?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar')?.textContent||null
   })
 };
 setTimeout(apply,700);
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9920-premium-fill-reference-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9920PremiumFill)return;window.__deenV9920PremiumFill=true;
 const VERSION='9.9.20-PREMIUM-FILL';

 const current=()=>session?.questions?.[session?.index]||null;
 const frame=()=>document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');

 function animalId(){
   const id=String(state?.profile?.companion||'owl').toLowerCase();
   return ['lion','wolf','owl','turtle','eagle','cat'].includes(id)?id:'owl';
 }

 function audioTrigger(){
   const panel=document.querySelector('#lesson .audio-panel');
   if(!panel)return null;
   const audio=panel.querySelector('audio');
   const play=panel.querySelector('button,.audio-play');
   return ()=>{
     if(audio){try{audio.currentTime=0;audio.play()}catch(e){};return}
     try{play?.click?.()}catch(e){}
   };
 }

 function decorate(){
   const q=current(),f=frame();
   if(!f)return;
   const isFill=String(q?.activity_type||'')==='fill_blank';
   f.classList.toggle('deen-premium-fill',isFill);
   f.querySelector('.v9920-lesson-scene')?.remove();
   if(!isFill)return;

   const scene=document.createElement('div');
   scene.className='v9920-lesson-scene v998-hero';
   scene.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
   f.prepend(scene);

   const avatar=f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar');
   if(avatar && !window.__DEEN_COMPANION_ASSET_MODE && !avatar.classList.contains('deen-companion-runtime-ready')){
     avatar.classList.add('v998-companion-hero');
     avatar.dataset.animal=animalId();
     avatar.textContent='';
   }

   const bubble=f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-bubble');
   if(bubble&&!bubble.querySelector('.deen-character-question-audio')){
     const trigger=audioTrigger();
     if(trigger){
       const b=document.createElement('button');
       b.type='button';
       b.className='deen-character-question-audio';
       b.textContent='🔊';
       b.setAttribute('aria-label','Soruyu dinle');
       b.title='Soruyu dinle';
       b.onclick=e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();trigger()};
       bubble.appendChild(b);
     }
   }

   try{window.DEEN_MOBILE_LESSON?.fit?.()}catch(e){}
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9920',event?.detail,decorate,{"raf":true,"delays":[80]}))decorate();
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9920',event?.detail,decorate,{"raf":false,"delays":[0]}))decorate();
 });

 window.DEEN_PREMIUM_FILL={
   version:VERSION,
   apply:decorate,
   snapshot:()=>({
     active:!!frame()?.classList.contains('deen-premium-fill'),
     animal:frame()?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar')?.dataset?.animal||null,
     scene:!!frame()?.querySelector('.v9920-lesson-scene'),
     choices:frame()?.querySelectorAll('.fill-choice')?.length||0
   })
 };
 setTimeout(decorate,700);
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9921-fill-dom-hotfix-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9921FillDomHotfix)return;window.__deenV9921FillDomHotfix=true;
 const VERSION='9.9.21-FILL-DOM-HOTFIX';
 function clean(){
   const q=session?.questions?.[session?.index];
   const f=document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');
   if(!f||q?.activity_type!=='fill_blank')return;
   f.classList.add('deen-premium-fill');

   f.querySelectorAll('.v950-age-badge,.v710-type-pill,.audio-panel,#v624LessonEnergy,.v624-lesson-energy').forEach(el=>el.remove());

   const avatar=f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar');
   if(avatar && !window.__DEEN_COMPANION_ASSET_MODE && !avatar.classList.contains('deen-companion-runtime-ready')){
     avatar.classList.add('v998-companion-hero');
     const id=String(state?.profile?.companion||avatar.dataset.animal||'owl').toLowerCase();
     avatar.dataset.animal=['lion','wolf','owl','turtle','eagle','cat'].includes(id)?id:'owl';
     avatar.textContent='';
   }

   const bank=f.querySelector('.v710-word-bank');
   if(bank){
     bank.querySelectorAll('.v710-word-chip').forEach(btn=>{
       btn.removeAttribute('style');
     });
   }

   try{window.DEEN_MOBILE_LESSON?.fit?.()}catch(e){}
 }
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9921',event?.detail,clean,{"raf":true,"delays":[40,120]}))clean();
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9921',event?.detail,clean,{"raf":false,"delays":[0]}))clean();
 });

 window.DEEN_FILL_DOM_HOTFIX={version:VERSION,apply:clean,snapshot:()=>({
   premium:!!document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame.deen-premium-fill'),
   ageBadge:!!document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame .v950-age-badge'),
   wordLabel:!!document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame .v710-type-pill'),
   audioPanel:!!document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame .audio-panel'),
   wordChoices:document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .question-frame .v710-word-chip').length
 })};
 setTimeout(clean,700);
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9922-premium-fill-polish-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9922PremiumFillPolish)return;window.__deenV9922PremiumFillPolish=true;
 const VERSION='9.9.22-PREMIUM-FILL-POLISH';
 const qNow=()=>session?.questions?.[session?.index]||null;
 const frame=()=>document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');

 function ensureBubbleAudio(f){
   const bubble=f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-bubble');
   if(!bubble)return;
   let b=bubble.querySelector('.deen-character-question-audio');
   const canNarrate=typeof window.DEEN_NARRATION?.speakQuestion==='function';
   if(!canNarrate){
     if(b)b.remove();
     return;
   }
   if(!b){
     b=document.createElement('button');
     b.type='button';
     b.className='deen-character-question-audio';
     b.textContent='🔊';
     bubble.appendChild(b);
   }
   b.setAttribute('aria-label','Soruyu dinle');
   b.title='Soruyu dinle';
   b.onclick=e=>{
     e.preventDefault();
     e.stopPropagation();
     try{window.DEEN_NARRATION.stop?.('bubble-replay')}catch(_){}
     try{window.DEEN_NARRATION.speakQuestion()}catch(_){}
   };
 }

 function polish(){
   const q=qNow(),f=frame();
   if(!f||String(q?.activity_type||'')!=='fill_blank')return;
   f.classList.add('deen-premium-fill');

   f.querySelectorAll('.v950-age-badge,.v710-type-pill,.audio-panel,#v624LessonEnergy,.v624-lesson-energy').forEach(el=>el.remove());

   const narration=f.querySelector('.v960-narration-bar');
   if(narration)narration.hidden=true;

   const avatar=f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar');
   if(avatar && !window.__DEEN_COMPANION_ASSET_MODE && !avatar.classList.contains('deen-companion-runtime-ready')){
     avatar.classList.add('v998-companion-hero');
     const id=String(state?.profile?.companion||avatar.dataset.animal||'owl').toLowerCase();
     avatar.dataset.animal=['lion','wolf','owl','turtle','eagle','cat'].includes(id)?id:'owl';
     avatar.textContent='';
   }

   const text=f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-text');
   if(text && !f.classList.contains('v1039-fill-duo')){
     text.textContent=String(q?.question_tr||q?.prompt_tr||q?.question||'')
       .replace(/^\s*Boşluğu\s+tamamla\s*:\s*/i,'')
       .trim();
   }

   const bank=f.querySelector('.v710-word-bank');
   bank?.querySelectorAll('.v710-word-chip').forEach(btn=>btn.removeAttribute('style'));

   ensureBubbleAudio(f);
   try{window.DEEN_MOBILE_LESSON?.fit?.()}catch(_){}
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9922',event?.detail,polish,{"raf":true,"delays":[50,180,360]}))polish();
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9922',event?.detail,polish,{"raf":false,"delays":[0,160]}))polish();
 });

 window.DEEN_PREMIUM_FILL_POLISH={
   version:VERSION,
   apply:polish,
   snapshot:()=>({
     active:!!frame()?.classList.contains('deen-premium-fill'),
     narrationRowVisible:!!frame()?.querySelector('.v960-narration-bar:not([hidden])'),
     bubbleAudio:!!frame()?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-audio'),
     answers:frame()?.querySelectorAll('.v710-word-chip').length||0,
     animal:frame()?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar')?.dataset?.animal||null
   })
 };
 setTimeout(polish,700);
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9923-companion-hero-cleanup-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9923CompanionHeroCleanup)return;window.__deenV9923CompanionHeroCleanup=true;
 const VERSION='9.9.23-COMPANION-HERO-CLEANUP';
 const frame=()=>document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');
 const qNow=()=>session?.questions?.[session?.index]||null;

 function apply(){
   const f=frame(),q=qNow();
   if(!f||String(q?.activity_type||'')!=='fill_blank')return;
   f.classList.add('deen-premium-fill');

   /* Remove legacy narration UI and any surviving lesson chrome. */
   f.querySelectorAll('.v960-narration-bar,.v950-age-badge,.v710-type-pill,.audio-panel,#v624LessonEnergy,.v624-lesson-energy').forEach(el=>el.remove());

   const avatar=f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar');
   if(avatar && !window.__DEEN_COMPANION_ASSET_MODE && !avatar.classList.contains('deen-companion-runtime-ready')){
     avatar.classList.add('v998-companion-hero');
     const id=String(state?.profile?.companion||avatar.dataset.animal||'owl').toLowerCase();
     avatar.dataset.animal=['lion','wolf','owl','turtle','eagle','cat'].includes(id)?id:'owl';
     avatar.textContent='';
   }

   /* Keep narration only as the small speaker inside the bubble. */
   const bubble=f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-bubble');
   if(bubble){
     let b=bubble.querySelector('.deen-character-question-audio');
     if(typeof window.DEEN_NARRATION?.speakQuestion==='function'){
       if(!b){
         b=document.createElement('button');
         b.type='button';
         b.className='deen-character-question-audio';
         b.textContent='🔊';
         bubble.appendChild(b);
       }
       b.setAttribute('aria-label','Soruyu dinle');
       b.title='Soruyu dinle';
       b.onclick=e=>{
         e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
         try{window.DEEN_NARRATION.stop?.('bubble-replay')}catch(_){}
         try{window.DEEN_NARRATION.speakQuestion()}catch(_){}
       };
     }else b?.remove();
   }

   f.querySelectorAll('.v5-source').forEach(el=>el.hidden=true);
   try{window.DEEN_MOBILE_LESSON?.fit?.()}catch(_){}
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9923',event?.detail,apply,{"raf":true,"delays":[50,160,320]}))apply();
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9923',event?.detail,apply,{"raf":false,"delays":[0]}))apply();
 });

 window.DEEN_COMPANION_HERO_CLEANUP={
   version:VERSION,
   apply,
   snapshot:()=>({
     active:!!frame()?.classList.contains('deen-premium-fill'),
     companion:frame()?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar')?.dataset?.animal||null,
     sourceVisible:!!frame()?.querySelector('.v5-source:not([hidden])'),
     narrationRow:!!frame()?.querySelector('.v960-narration-bar'),
     bubbleAudio:!!frame()?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-audio')
   })
 };
 setTimeout(apply,700);
})();
</script>

(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9927CompanionStage)return;
 window.__deenV9927CompanionStage=true;
 const VERSION='9.9.27-COMPANION-STAGE';
 const frame=()=>document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');
 const qNow=()=>session?.questions?.[session?.index]||null;

 function apply(){
   const f=frame(),q=qNow();
   if(!f||String(q?.activity_type||'')!=='fill_blank')return;
   f.classList.add('deen-premium-fill');

   /* Let the stability layer own the mascot DOM; this patch only owns presentation. */
   if(!window.__DEEN_COMPANION_ASSET_MODE){try{window.DEEN_MASCOT_STABILITY?.apply?.()}catch(_){}}

   f.querySelectorAll('.v960-narration-bar,.v960-narration-note,.audio-panel,.v5-source,details.v5-source,.source,[class*="source-note"]').forEach(el=>el.remove());
   try{window.DEEN_MOBILE_LESSON?.fit?.()}catch(_){}
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9927',event?.detail,apply,{"raf":true,"delays":[80,220,520]}))apply();
 });

 window.DEEN_COMPANION_STAGE={
   version:VERSION,
   apply,
   snapshot:()=>({
     active:!!frame()?.classList.contains('deen-premium-fill'),
     mascot:!!frame()?.querySelector('.v9926-mascot'),
     bubble:!!frame()?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-bubble')
   })
 };
 setTimeout(apply,750);
})();
</script>

(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9930CenterCompanionRow)return;

 const VERSION='9.9.30-CENTER-COMPANION-ROW';

   const row=f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-row');

     row.style.marginLeft='auto';
     row.style.marginRight='auto';

     bank.style.marginLeft='auto';

   const cta=bank?.nextElementSibling;

     cta.style.marginLeft='auto';
     cta.style.marginRight='auto';

   try{window.DEEN_MOBILE_LESSON?.fit?.()}catch(_){}

<script id="deen-v9931-safe-center-rollback-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9931ClassicCompanion)return;
 window.__deenV9931ClassicCompanion=true;

 const VERSION='9.9.31-SAFE-CENTER';
 const EMOJI={lion:'🦁',wolf:'🐺',owl:'🦉',turtle:'🐢',eagle:'🦅',cat:'🐈'};
 const frame=()=>document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');
 const qNow=()=>session?.questions?.[session?.index]||null;

 function restoreMascot(f){
   if(window.__DEEN_COMPANION_ASSET_MODE)return;
   const avatar=f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar');
   if(!avatar)return;
   if(avatar.classList.contains('deen-companion-runtime-ready'))return;
   const id=String(state?.profile?.companion||avatar.dataset.animal||'owl').toLowerCase();
   const animal=EMOJI[id]?id:'owl';
   avatar.dataset.animal=animal;

   let mascot=avatar.querySelector('.v9926-mascot');
   if(!mascot){
     mascot=document.createElement('span');
     mascot.className='v9926-mascot';
     avatar.replaceChildren(mascot);
   }
   mascot.dataset.animal=animal;
   delete mascot.dataset.vectorAnimal;
   mascot.innerHTML='<span class="v9926-mascot-glyph" aria-hidden="true">'+EMOJI[animal]+'</span>';
 }

 function apply(){
   const f=frame(),q=qNow();
   if(!f||String(q?.activity_type||'')!=='fill_blank')return;
   f.classList.add('deen-premium-fill');

   restoreMascot(f);

   const row=f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-row');
   if(row){
     row.style.removeProperty('width');
     row.style.removeProperty('margin-left');
     row.style.removeProperty('margin-right');
   }

   const bank=f.querySelector('.v710-word-bank');
   if(bank){
     bank.style.removeProperty('width');
     bank.style.removeProperty('margin-left');
     bank.style.removeProperty('margin-right');
   }

   const cta=bank?.nextElementSibling;
   if(cta?.classList?.contains('template-action')){
     cta.style.removeProperty('width');
     cta.style.removeProperty('margin-left');
     cta.style.removeProperty('margin-right');
   }

   f.querySelectorAll('.v960-narration-bar,.v960-narration-note,.audio-panel,.v5-source,details.v5-source,.source,[class*="source-note"]').forEach(el=>el.remove());
   try{window.DEEN_MOBILE_LESSON?.fit?.()}catch(_){}
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9931',event?.detail,apply,{"raf":true,"delays":[60,160,360,720]}))apply();
 });

 window.DEEN_SAFE_CENTER={
   version:VERSION,
   apply,
   snapshot:()=>({
     active:!!frame()?.classList.contains('deen-premium-fill'),
     mascot:frame()?.querySelector('.v9926-mascot-glyph')?.textContent||null,
     rowDisplay:getComputedStyle(frame()?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-row')||document.body).display
   })
 };
 setTimeout(apply,760);
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9934-fixed-vertical-center-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9934FixedVerticalCenter)return;
 window.__deenV9934FixedVerticalCenter=true;

 const VERSION='9.9.34-FIXED-VERTICAL-CENTER';
 const frame=()=>document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');
 const qNow=()=>session?.questions?.[session?.index]||null;

 function apply(){
   const f=frame(),q=qNow();
   if(!f||String(q?.activity_type||'')!=='fill_blank')return;

   /* Neutralize old centering state. */
   f.classList.remove('v9932-screen-centered','v9933-screen-centered');
   f.style.removeProperty('--v9932-center-shift');
   f.style.removeProperty('--v9933-center-shift');
   f.classList.add('deen-premium-fill','v9934-fixed-center');

   const row=f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-row');
   const area=f.querySelector('#answerArea');
   const bank=f.querySelector('.v710-word-bank');
   const cta=f.querySelector('.v710-word-bank + .template-action,.v710-word-bank + .btn');

   [row,area,bank,cta].forEach(el=>{
     if(!el)return;
     el.style.removeProperty('transform');
     el.style.removeProperty('top');
   });

   try{window.DEEN_MOBILE_LESSON?.fit?.()}catch(_){}
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9934',event?.detail,apply,{"raf":true,"delays":[80,220,520,900]}))apply();
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9934',event?.detail,apply,{"raf":false,"delays":[80,260]}))apply();
 });

 window.DEEN_FIXED_VERTICAL_CENTER={
   version:VERSION,
   apply,
   snapshot:()=>({
     active:!!frame()?.classList.contains('v9934-fixed-center'),
     marginTop:frame()?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-row')
       ? getComputedStyle(frame().querySelector('.deen-character-question-row')).marginTop
       : null
   })
 };

 setTimeout(apply,800);
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9936-companion-runtime-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9936CompanionRuntime)return;
 window.__deenV9936CompanionRuntime=true;
 window.__DEEN_COMPANION_ASSET_MODE=true;

 const VERSION='9.9.58-COMPANION-ASSET-CACHE';
 const IDS=['lion','wolf','owl','turtle','eagle','cat'];
 const STATES=['lesson_idle','correct','try_again','celebrate','avatar'];
 const DEFAULT='owl';
 const availability=new Map();
 const pending=new Map();
 let renderToken=0;

 const ASSETS={};
 IDS.forEach(id=>{
   ASSETS[id]={
     lesson_idle:'assets/companions/'+id+'/'+id+'_lesson_idle.png',
     correct:'assets/companions/'+id+'/'+id+'_correct.png',
     try_again:'assets/companions/'+id+'/'+id+'_try_again.png',
     celebrate:'assets/companions/'+id+'/'+id+'_celebrate.png',
     avatar:'assets/companions/'+id+'/'+id+'_avatar.png'
   };
 });

 function selectedId(){
   const id=String(state?.profile?.companion||DEFAULT).toLowerCase();
   return IDS.includes(id)?id:DEFAULT;
 }

 function path(id,mood){
   const safeId=IDS.includes(id)?id:DEFAULT;
   const safeMood=STATES.includes(mood)?mood:'lesson_idle';
   return ASSETS[safeId]?.[safeMood]||ASSETS[safeId]?.lesson_idle||ASSETS[DEFAULT].lesson_idle;
 }

 function assetVersion(){
   return String(window.__DEEN_DEPLOY_ID__||window.DEEN_BUILD_ID||'9958assetcache1');
 }

 function versioned(src){
   const sep=src.includes('?')?'&':'?';
   return src+sep+'v='+encodeURIComponent(assetVersion());
 }

 function dispatchRendered(id,mood){
   try{document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('deen:companion-rendered',{detail:{animal:id,mood}}))}catch(_){}
 }

 function probe(src){
   const url=versioned(src);
   if(availability.has(url))return Promise.resolve(availability.get(url));
   if(pending.has(url))return pending.get(url);
   const p=new Promise(resolve=>{
     const img=new Image();
     let settled=false;
     const done=ok=>{
       if(settled)return;
       settled=true;
       availability.set(url,ok);
       pending.delete(url);
       resolve(ok);
     };
     img.onload=()=>done(true);
     img.onerror=()=>done(false);
     img.src=url;
     if(img.complete&&img.naturalWidth>0)done(true);
     setTimeout(()=>done(false),3500);
   });
   pending.set(url,p);
   return p;
 }

 function fallbackLesson(){
   try{window.DEEN_SAFE_CENTER?.apply?.()}catch(_){}
   try{window.DEEN_MASCOT_STABILITY?.apply?.()}catch(_){}
 }

 function markLayout(avatar,id){
   const row=avatar?.closest?.('.deen-character-question-row');
   if(!row)return;
   row.classList.add('deen-companion-runtime-row');
   row.dataset.companionAnimal=id;
 }

 async function renderLesson(mood='lesson_idle'){
   if(mood==='lesson_idle'&&Number(window.__DEEN_COMPANION_REACTION_LOCK||0)>Date.now())return false;
   const q=session?.questions?.[session?.index];
   const f=document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');
   if(!f||!q)return false;

   let avatar=f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar');
   if(!avatar)return false;

   const id=selectedId();
   let resolvedMood=STATES.includes(mood)?mood:'lesson_idle';
   let src=path(id,resolvedMood);

   const existing=avatar.querySelector('.deen-companion-runtime-img');
   if(
     avatar.classList.contains('deen-companion-runtime-ready')&&
     avatar.dataset.animal===id&&
     avatar.dataset.deenCompanionMood===resolvedMood&&
     existing?.dataset?.companion===id&&
     existing?.dataset?.mood===resolvedMood
   ){
     markLayout(avatar,id);
     dispatchRendered(id,resolvedMood);
     return true;
   }

   const token=++renderToken;
   avatar.dataset.deenCompanionMood=resolvedMood;
   avatar.dataset.animal=id;

   let ok=await probe(src);
   if(token!==renderToken)return false;

   const liveFrame=document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');
   avatar=liveFrame?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar');
   if(!avatar||!avatar.isConnected)return false;
   avatar.dataset.deenCompanionMood=resolvedMood;
   avatar.dataset.animal=id;
   markLayout(avatar,id);

   if(!ok&&resolvedMood!=='lesson_idle'){
     resolvedMood='lesson_idle';
     src=path(id,resolvedMood);
     ok=await probe(src);
     if(token!==renderToken)return false;
     const liveFrame2=document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');
     avatar=liveFrame2?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar');
     if(!avatar||!avatar.isConnected)return false;
     avatar.dataset.deenCompanionMood=resolvedMood;
     avatar.dataset.animal=id;
     markLayout(avatar,id);
   }

   if(!ok){
     avatar.classList.remove('deen-companion-runtime-ready');
     fallbackLesson();
     return false;
   }

   const img=new Image();
   img.className='deen-companion-runtime-img';
   img.alt='';
   img.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
   img.dataset.companion=id;
   img.dataset.mood=resolvedMood;
   img.src=versioned(src);

   avatar.classList.add('deen-companion-runtime-ready');
   avatar.replaceChildren(img);
   dispatchRendered(id,resolvedMood);
   return true;
 }

 async function renderProfile(){
   const root=document.getElementById('profileScreenBody');
   if(!root)return false;
   const id=selectedId();
   const src=path(id,'avatar');
   const ok=await probe(src);
   if(!ok)return false;

   const candidates=[
     root.querySelector('.v997-companion-avatar'),
     root.querySelector('.v994-companion-avatar'),
     root.querySelector('.v5-profilehead .v5-avatar.large'),
     root.querySelector('.v742-profile-avatar'),
     root.querySelector('[data-profile-avatar]')
   ].filter(Boolean);

   if(!candidates.length)return false;
   const el=candidates[0];
   const current=el.querySelector('.deen-companion-profile-img');
   if(current?.dataset?.companion===id)return true;

   const img=new Image();
   img.className='deen-companion-profile-img';
   img.alt='';
   img.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
   img.dataset.companion=id;
   img.src=versioned(src);
   el.replaceChildren(img);
   return true;
 }

 function preload(){
   const id=selectedId();
   ['lesson_idle','correct','try_again','avatar','celebrate'].forEach(mood=>probe(path(id,mood)));
 }

 /*
   Question lifecycle is now owned by DEEN_RUNTIME_ARBITER. This runtime only
   reacts to those canonical events; it no longer wraps render/grade/result.
 */
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',event=>{
   const run=()=>renderLesson('lesson_idle');
   if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9936-companion',event?.detail,run,{raf:true}))requestAnimationFrame(run);
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',e=>{
   const mood=e?.detail?.correct===true?'correct':'try_again';
   const run=()=>{
     const avatar=document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame .deen-character-question-avatar');
     const img=avatar?.querySelector('.deen-companion-runtime-img');
     if(avatar&&img){
       // Keep the already-visible mascot asset mounted during feedback.
       // The reaction layer animates this image; swapping to a mood asset here
       // could briefly blank the companion when an alternate asset is missing.
       avatar.dataset.deenCompanionMood=mood;
       return true;
     }
     return renderLesson('lesson_idle');
   };
   if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9936-companion',e?.detail,run,{raf:true}))requestAnimationFrame(run);
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-result',()=>{
   requestAnimationFrame(()=>renderLesson('celebrate'));
 });

 document.addEventListener('deen:profile-rendered',()=>requestAnimationFrame(renderProfile));
 document.addEventListener('deen:ui-updated',()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{preload();renderProfile()}));

 if(window.DEEN_COMPANION&&typeof window.DEEN_COMPANION.select==='function'&&!window.DEEN_COMPANION.select.__deen9936){
   const baseSelect=window.DEEN_COMPANION.select;
   const wrapped=function(...args){
     const r=baseSelect.apply(this,args);
     requestAnimationFrame(()=>{preload();renderLesson('lesson_idle');renderProfile()});
     return r;
   };
   wrapped.__deen9936=true;
   window.DEEN_COMPANION.select=wrapped;
 }

 window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS={
   version:VERSION,
   ids:[...IDS],
   states:[...STATES],
   assets:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ASSETS)),
   selected:selectedId,
   asset:(mood='lesson_idle')=>path(selectedId(),mood),
   preload,
   renderLesson,
   renderProfile,
   assetVersion,
   availability:()=>Object.fromEntries(availability.entries())
 };

 preload();
 requestAnimationFrame(()=>renderProfile());
})();
</script>



<!-- DEEN v9.9.37 — Companion ownership lock -->
<script id="deen-v9937-companion-lock-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9938StableCompanionLock)return;
 window.__deenV9938StableCompanionLock=true;
 window.__DEEN_COMPANION_ASSET_MODE=true;

 const VERSION='9.9.56-EVENT-COMPANION-LOCK';
 let observer=null;
 let repairing=false;
 let scheduled=false;
 let lastMood='lesson_idle';
 let repairCount=0;

 const frame=()=>document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');

 function desiredMood(){
   const avatar=frame()?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar');
   return avatar?.dataset?.deenCompanionMood||lastMood||'lesson_idle';
 }

 async function repair(forceMood){
   if(repairing)return false;
   const api=window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS;
   const f=frame();
   const avatar=f?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar');
   if(!api||!avatar)return false;

   const mood=forceMood||desiredMood();
   lastMood=mood;

   if(avatar.classList.contains('deen-companion-runtime-ready')&&
      avatar.querySelector('.deen-companion-runtime-img'))return true;

   repairing=true;
   try{
     const ok=!!(await api.renderLesson(mood));
     if(ok)repairCount++;
     return ok;
   }finally{
     repairing=false;
   }
 }

 function queueRepair(){
   if(scheduled)return;
   scheduled=true;
   queueMicrotask(async()=>{
     scheduled=false;
     await repair(desiredMood());
   });
 }

 function watch(){
   try{observer?.disconnect?.()}catch(_){}
   const f=frame();
   if(!f||!('MutationObserver' in window))return;

   /*
     Watch only structural replacement. No characterData/attribute watching,
     no polling timer. If a legacy renderer swaps the avatar node, repair once.
   */
   observer=new MutationObserver(mutations=>{
     if(repairing)return;
     if(!mutations.some(m=>m.type==='childList'&&(m.addedNodes.length||m.removedNodes.length)))return;
     const avatar=f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar');
     if(!avatar)return;
     if(!avatar.querySelector('.deen-companion-runtime-img'))queueRepair();
   });
   observer.observe(f,{childList:true,subtree:true});
 }

 function mount(mood){
   if(mood)lastMood=mood;
   watch();
   return repair(lastMood);
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',event=>{
   lastMood='lesson_idle';
   const run=()=>mount('lesson_idle');
   if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9937-companion-lock',event?.detail,run,{raf:true}))requestAnimationFrame(run);
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',e=>{
   lastMood=e?.detail?.correct===true?'correct':'try_again';
   const mood=lastMood,run=()=>repair(mood);
   if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9937-companion-lock',e?.detail,run,{raf:true}))requestAnimationFrame(run);
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:companion-rendered',()=>watch());
 document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{
   if(document.visibilityState==='visible'&&document.getElementById('lesson')?.classList.contains('active')){
     requestAnimationFrame(()=>mount(desiredMood()));
   }
 });

 window.DEEN_COMPANION_LOCK={
   version:VERSION,
   apply:mount,
   repair,
   snapshot:()=>({
     ready:!!frame()?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar.deen-companion-runtime-ready'),
     image:!!frame()?.querySelector('.deen-companion-runtime-img'),
     animal:frame()?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar')?.dataset?.animal||null,
     mood:desiredMood(),
     repairCount,
     polling:false
   })
 };
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9941-boolean-screen-repair-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9941BooleanRepair)return;
 window.__deenV9941BooleanRepair=true;

 const VERSION='9.9.41-BOOLEAN-SCREEN-REPAIR';
 const qNow=()=>session?.questions?.[session?.index]||null;
 const frame=()=>document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');

 function isBoolean(q){
   return String(q?.activity_type||q?.question_type||q?.type||'').toLowerCase()==='true_false';
 }

 function mountBubbleAudio(f){
   const bubble=f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-bubble');
   if(!bubble||bubble.querySelector('.deen-boolean-narration'))return;
   const button=document.createElement('button');
   button.type='button';
   button.className='deen-character-question-audio deen-boolean-narration';
   button.textContent='🔊';
   button.setAttribute('aria-label','Soruyu dinle');
   button.title='Soruyu dinle';
   button.onclick=e=>{
     e.preventDefault();
     e.stopPropagation();
     try{window.DEEN_NARRATION?.speakQuestion?.()}catch(_){}
   };
   bubble.appendChild(button);
 }

 function apply(){
   const f=frame(),q=qNow();
   if(!f)return;

   if(!isBoolean(q)){
     f.classList.remove('deen-premium-boolean');
     return;
   }

   f.classList.add('deen-premium-boolean');

   /* Remove legacy age/type/narration chrome after old renderers re-inject it. */
   [
     '.v950-age-badge','.v710-type-pill','.question-type-pill','.lesson-age-pill',
     '.v960-narration-bar','.v960-narration-note','.audio-panel'
   ].forEach(sel=>f.querySelectorAll(sel).forEach(el=>el.style.setProperty('display','none','important')));

   mountBubbleAudio(f);

   const action=f.querySelector('.template-action');
   if(action && /kararını\s+kilitle/i.test(action.textContent||'')) action.textContent='Kontrol Et';

   try{window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS?.renderLesson?.('lesson_idle')}catch(_){}
   try{window.DEEN_COMPANION_LOCK?.apply?.()}catch(_){}
   try{window.DEEN_MOBILE_LESSON?.fit?.()}catch(_){}
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9941',event?.detail,apply,{"raf":true,"delays":[70,180,420,850]}))apply();
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',event=>{
  if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9941',event?.detail,apply,{"raf":false,"delays":[90]}))apply();
 });

 window.DEEN_BOOLEAN_SCREEN_REPAIR={
   version:VERSION,
   apply,
   snapshot:()=>({
     active:!!frame()?.classList.contains('deen-premium-boolean'),
     type:qNow()?.activity_type||null,
     animal:frame()?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar')?.dataset?.animal||null,
     image:!!frame()?.querySelector('.deen-companion-runtime-img'),
     legacyNarration:!!frame()?.querySelector('.v960-narration-bar:not([style*="display: none"])')
   })
 };

 [250,650,1200].forEach(ms=>setTimeout(apply,ms));
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9943-companion-weight-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9943CompanionWeight)return;
 window.__deenV9943CompanionWeight=true;
 const VERSION='9.9.43-COMPANION-WEIGHT';

 function apply(){
   const row=document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-character-question-row.deen-companion-runtime-row');
   const avatar=row?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar.deen-companion-runtime-ready');
   if(!row||!avatar)return false;
   const animal=avatar.dataset.animal||row.dataset.companionAnimal||'';
   if(animal)row.dataset.companionAnimal=animal;
   row.dataset.deenCompanionWeight='9943';
   try{window.DEEN_MOBILE_LESSON?.fit?.()}catch(_){}
   return true;
 }

 window.DEEN_COMPANION_WEIGHT={
   version:VERSION,
   apply,
   snapshot:()=>({
     animal:document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-character-question-avatar')?.dataset?.animal||null,
     tuned:document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-character-question-row')?.dataset?.deenCompanionWeight||null
   })
 };

 [120,350,700,1200].forEach(ms=>setTimeout(apply,ms));
 window.addEventListener('resize',()=>setTimeout(apply,80),{passive:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9944-companion-reactions-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9944CompanionReactions)return;
 window.__deenV9944CompanionReactions=true;

 const VERSION='9.9.54-COMPANION-REACTIONS-ONCE';
 let reactionToken=0;
 let cleanupTimer=0;
 let lastGradeEpoch=-1;
 let reactionCount=0;

 const frame=()=>document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');
 const avatar=()=>frame()?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar.deen-companion-runtime-ready')||null;

 function clearVisuals(){
   clearTimeout(cleanupTimer);
   const f=frame(),a=avatar();
   if(f){
     f.classList.remove('v9944-feedback-correct','v9944-feedback-wrong','v9944-feedback-celebrate');
     f.querySelectorAll('.v9944-reaction-chip,.v9944-particles').forEach(el=>el.remove());
   }
   if(a)a.classList.remove('v9944-reaction-correct','v9944-reaction-wrong','v9944-reaction-celebrate');
 }

 function chip(text,kind){
   const a=avatar();
   if(!a)return;
   a.querySelectorAll('.v9944-reaction-chip').forEach(el=>el.remove());
   const el=document.createElement('div');
   el.className='v9944-reaction-chip';
   el.dataset.kind=kind;
   el.textContent=text;
   a.appendChild(el);
 }

 function particles(count){
   const a=avatar();
   if(!a)return;
   a.querySelectorAll('.v9944-particles').forEach(el=>el.remove());
   const root=document.createElement('div');
   root.className='v9944-particles';
   for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
     const p=document.createElement('i');
     p.className='v9944-particle p'+(i%10);
     root.appendChild(p);
   }
   a.appendChild(root);
 }

 function render(kind,{vibrate=true}={}){
   const token=++reactionToken;
   const f=frame(),a=avatar();
   if(!f||!a)return false;

   clearVisuals();

   if(kind==='correct'){
     f.classList.add('v9944-feedback-correct');
     a.classList.add('v9944-reaction-correct');
     chip('Harika!','correct');
     particles(8);
     window.__DEEN_COMPANION_REACTION_LOCK=Date.now()+1050;
     if(vibrate)try{navigator.vibrate?.([12,24,12])}catch(_){}
     cleanupTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(token===reactionToken)clearVisuals()},1080);
     reactionCount++;
     return true;
   }

   if(kind==='wrong'){
     f.classList.add('v9944-feedback-wrong');
     a.classList.add('v9944-reaction-wrong');
     chip('Tekrar dene','wrong');
     window.__DEEN_COMPANION_REACTION_LOCK=Date.now()+900;
     if(vibrate)try{navigator.vibrate?.(28)}catch(_){}
     cleanupTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(token===reactionToken)clearVisuals()},920);
     reactionCount++;
     return true;
   }

   if(kind==='celebrate'){
     f.classList.add('v9944-feedback-celebrate');
     a.classList.add('v9944-reaction-celebrate');
     chip('Bölüm tamamlandı!','celebrate');
     particles(10);
     window.__DEEN_COMPANION_REACTION_LOCK=Date.now()+1700;
     if(vibrate)try{navigator.vibrate?.([18,35,18,35,28])}catch(_){}
     cleanupTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(token===reactionToken)clearVisuals()},1750);
     reactionCount++;
     return true;
   }

   return false;
 }

 /*
   v9.9.54: This feature no longer wraps renderQuestion / completeQuestion /
   showResult. Those lifecycle boundaries are owned by DEEN_RUNTIME_ARBITER.
   The mascot artwork itself is owned by DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS (v9936).
   This layer only adds the chip/particles/haptic reaction exactly once.
 */
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',()=>{
   window.__DEEN_COMPANION_REACTION_LOCK=0;
   reactionToken++;
   clearVisuals();
 });

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',e=>{
   const epoch=Number(e?.detail?.epoch);
   if(Number.isFinite(epoch) && epoch===lastGradeEpoch)return;
   if(Number.isFinite(epoch))lastGradeEpoch=epoch;

   const kind=e?.detail?.correct===true?'correct':'wrong';
   let done=false;
   const run=()=>{
     if(done)return;
     done=!!render(kind);
   };
   // Arbiter validates epoch/key before both the first frame and retry.
   if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.('v9944-reaction',e?.detail,run,{raf:true,delays:[80]})){
     requestAnimationFrame(()=>{if(render(kind))return;setTimeout(()=>render(kind),80)});
   }
 });

 window.DEEN_COMPANION_REACTIONS={
   version:VERSION,
   correct:()=>render('correct'),
   wrong:()=>render('wrong'),
   celebrate:()=>render('celebrate'),
   clear:clearVisuals,
   snapshot:()=>({
     mood:avatar()?.dataset?.deenCompanionMood||null,
     correct:!!frame()?.classList.contains('v9944-feedback-correct'),
     wrong:!!frame()?.classList.contains('v9944-feedback-wrong'),
     celebrate:!!frame()?.classList.contains('v9944-feedback-celebrate'),
     lockedUntil:Number(window.__DEEN_COMPANION_REACTION_LOCK||0),
     lastGradeEpoch,
     reactionCount,
     eventDriven:true
   })
 };
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9945-feedback-layout-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9945FeedbackLayout)return;
 window.__deenV9945FeedbackLayout=true;

 const VERSION='9.9.55-FEEDBACK-FULL-TEXT';
 let token=0;
 let lastGradeEpoch=-1;

 const frame=()=>document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');
 const qNow=()=>session?.questions?.[session?.index]||null;

 function answerText(q){
   const a=q?.correct_answer;
   if(Array.isArray(a))return a.join(' · ');
   if(a==null)return '';
   return String(a).trim();
 }

 function removeAnswerLine(){
   document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .v9945-correct-answer').forEach(el=>el.remove());
 }

 function reset(){
   token++;
   const f=frame();
   f?.classList.remove('v9945-feedback-mode','v9945-feedback-correct','v9945-feedback-wrong');
   removeAnswerLine();
   const fb=document.getElementById('feedback');
   if(fb)fb.scrollTop=0;
   const box=document.getElementById('feedbackBox');
   if(box)box.scrollTop=0;
 }

 function compact(correct,q=qNow()){
   const f=frame(),fb=document.getElementById('feedback'),box=document.getElementById('feedbackBox');
   if(!f||!fb||!box||!fb.classList.contains('show'))return false;

   const my=++token;
   f.classList.add('v9945-feedback-mode');
   f.classList.toggle('v9945-feedback-correct',correct===true);
   f.classList.toggle('v9945-feedback-wrong',correct!==true);

   document.getElementById('feedbackIcon')?.style.setProperty('display','none','important');
   box.querySelectorAll('.v732-reaction-avatar,[class*="v732-reaction"]').forEach(el=>el.style.setProperty('display','none','important'));

   const title=document.getElementById('feedbackTitle');
   if(title)title.textContent=correct===true?'Harika!':'Birlikte düzeltelim';

   removeAnswerLine();
   if(correct!==true){
     const answer=answerText(q);
     if(answer){
       const line=document.createElement('div');
       line.className='v9945-correct-answer';
       const label=document.createElement('span');
       label.textContent='Doğru cevap';
       const strong=document.createElement('strong');
       strong.textContent=answer;
       line.append(label,strong);
       document.getElementById('feedbackText')?.after(line);
     }
   }

   const continueBtn=document.getElementById('continueBtn');
   if(continueBtn)continueBtn.textContent='Devam et';

   box.scrollTop=0;
   try{document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('deen:layout-dirty',{detail:{source:'feedback'}}))}catch(_){}

   requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
     if(my!==token)return;
     try{window.DEEN_MOBILE_LESSON?.fit?.()}catch(_){}
   });
   setTimeout(()=>{
     if(my!==token)return;
     try{window.DEEN_MOBILE_LESSON?.fit?.()}catch(_){}
   },90);

   return true;
 }

 /*
   v9.9.55: feedback no longer wraps renderQuestion / completeQuestion.
   The authoritative lifecycle is DEEN_RUNTIME_ARBITER.
 */
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',()=>reset());

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',e=>{
   const epoch=Number(e?.detail?.epoch);
   if(Number.isFinite(epoch)&&epoch===lastGradeEpoch)return;
   if(Number.isFinite(epoch))lastGradeEpoch=epoch;

   const correct=e?.detail?.correct===true;
   const q=qNow();
   const expected=++token;

   // Preserve the short right/wrong flash, then switch to the explanation.
   setTimeout(()=>{
     if(expected!==token)return;
     if(compact(correct,q))return;
     // Defensive retry only if a legacy renderer delayed .feedback.show.
     setTimeout(()=>{
       if(expected!==token)return;
       compact(correct,q);
     },90);
   },330);
 });

 window.DEEN_FEEDBACK_LAYOUT={
   version:VERSION,
   apply:compact,
   reset,
   snapshot:()=>({
     active:!!frame()?.classList.contains('v9945-feedback-mode'),
     wrong:!!frame()?.classList.contains('v9945-feedback-wrong'),
     correct:!!frame()?.classList.contains('v9945-feedback-correct'),
     answer:document.querySelector('#lesson .v9945-correct-answer strong')?.textContent||null,
     explanationLength:(document.getElementById('feedbackText')?.textContent||'').length,
     explanationScrollHeight:document.getElementById('feedbackBox')?.scrollHeight||0,
     explanationClientHeight:document.getElementById('feedbackBox')?.clientHeight||0,
     scrollable:(()=>{const b=document.getElementById('feedbackBox');return !!b&&b.scrollHeight>b.clientHeight+1})(),
     lastGradeEpoch,
     eventDriven:true
   })
 };
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9946-unified-companion-layout-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9946UnifiedCompanionLayout)return;
 window.__deenV9946UnifiedCompanionLayout=true;

 const VERSION='9.9.56-UNIFIED-COMPANION-EVENT-LAYOUT';
 let applyCount=0;

 function dirty(source){
   try{document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('deen:layout-dirty',{detail:{source}}))}catch(_){}
 }

 function classify(){
   const row=document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-character-question-row.deen-companion-runtime-row');
   const text=row?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-text');
   const avatar=row?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar.deen-companion-runtime-ready');
   if(!row||!text||!avatar)return false;

   const animal=avatar.dataset.animal||row.dataset.companionAnimal||'';
   if(animal)row.dataset.companionAnimal=animal;

   const raw=(text.textContent||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
   const len=raw.length;
   const before=row.className;

   row.classList.remove('deen-question-long','deen-question-very-long','deen-question-ultra-long');

   /*
     Length-only classification is deterministic. The old ResizeObserver used
     measured line count, then changed font/width, which could retrigger itself.
   */
   if(len>=112){
     row.classList.add('deen-question-ultra-long');
   }else if(len>=82){
     row.classList.add('deen-question-very-long');
   }else if(len>=55){
     row.classList.add('deen-question-long');
   }

   row.dataset.deenUnifiedCompanion='9956';
   row.dataset.deenPromptLength=String(len);
   delete row.dataset.deenPromptLines;
   applyCount++;

   if(before!==row.className)dirty('companion-classify');
   return true;
 }

 function apply(){return classify()}

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',()=>requestAnimationFrame(apply));
 document.addEventListener('deen:companion-rendered',()=>requestAnimationFrame(apply));
 window.addEventListener('resize',()=>requestAnimationFrame(apply),{passive:true});

 window.DEEN_UNIFIED_COMPANION_LAYOUT={
   version:VERSION,
   apply,
   snapshot:()=>({
     animal:document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-character-question-avatar')?.dataset?.animal||null,
     promptLength:document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-character-question-row')?.dataset?.deenPromptLength||null,
     long:document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-character-question-row')?.classList.contains('deen-question-long')||false,
     veryLong:document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-character-question-row')?.classList.contains('deen-question-very-long')||false,
     ultraLong:document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-character-question-row')?.classList.contains('deen-question-ultra-long')||false,
     applyCount,
     resizeObserver:false
   })
 };
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9947-final-companion-stabilization-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9947FinalCompanionStabilization)return;
 window.__deenV9947FinalCompanionStabilization=true;

 const VERSION='9.9.56-FINAL-COMPANION-EVENT-STABILIZATION';
 let applyCount=0;

 function dirty(source){
   try{document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('deen:layout-dirty',{detail:{source}}))}catch(_){}
 }

 function apply(){
   const row=document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-character-question-row.deen-companion-runtime-row');
   const avatar=row?.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar.deen-companion-runtime-ready');
   const img=avatar?.querySelector('.deen-companion-runtime-img');
   if(!row||!avatar||!img)return false;

   const animal=String(avatar.dataset.animal||row.dataset.companionAnimal||'').toLowerCase();
   const mood=String(avatar.dataset.deenCompanionMood||'lesson_idle').toLowerCase();
   const stateName=['lesson_idle','correct','try_again','celebrate'].includes(mood)?mood:'lesson_idle';

   let changed=false;
   if(animal&&row.dataset.companionAnimal!==animal){row.dataset.companionAnimal=animal;changed=true}
   if(row.dataset.companionState!==stateName){row.dataset.companionState=stateName;changed=true}
   if(row.dataset.deenCompanionStable!=='9956'){row.dataset.deenCompanionStable='9956';changed=true}

   img.setAttribute('draggable','false');
   img.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');

   try{window.DEEN_UNIFIED_COMPANION_LAYOUT?.apply?.()}catch(_){}
   applyCount++;
   if(changed)dirty('companion-stable');
   return true;
 }

 function mount(){return apply()}

 document.addEventListener('deen:companion-rendered',()=>requestAnimationFrame(mount));
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',()=>requestAnimationFrame(mount));

 window.DEEN_FINAL_COMPANION_STABILIZATION={
   version:VERSION,
   apply:mount,
   snapshot:()=>({
     animal:document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-character-question-avatar')?.dataset?.animal||null,
     state:document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-character-question-row')?.dataset?.companionState||null,
     stable:document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-character-question-row')?.dataset?.deenCompanionStable||null,
     src:document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-companion-runtime-img')?.getAttribute('src')||null,
     applyCount,
     observer:false
   })
 };
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9948-no-human-guide-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV9948NoHumanGuide)return;
  window.__deenV9948NoHumanGuide=true;

  const VERSION='9.9.48-NO-HUMAN-GUIDE';
  let observer=null;

  const SELECTORS=[
    '#lesson .v732-reaction-avatar',
    '#lesson .v732-reaction-name',
    '#lesson [class*="v732-reaction-avatar"]',
    '#lesson [class*="v732-reaction-name"]',
    '#lesson .v730-presenter',
    '#lesson .v730-avatar-wrap',
    '#lesson .v730-avatar',
    '#lesson [data-legacy-guide]',
    '#lesson [data-guide-avatar]',
    '#lesson .legacy-guide-avatar',
    '#lesson .legacy-human-guide'
  ];

  function kill(root=document){
    let removed=0;
    SELECTORS.forEach(sel=>{
      root.querySelectorAll?.(sel)?.forEach(el=>{
        try{
          el.style.setProperty('display','none','important');
          el.style.setProperty('visibility','hidden','important');
          el.style.setProperty('opacity','0','important');
          el.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
          el.dataset.deenLegacyGuideDisabled='9948';
          removed++;
        }catch(_){}
      });
    });

    const icon=document.getElementById('feedbackIcon');
    if(icon){
      icon.style.setProperty('display','none','important');
      icon.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .feedback-box.v732-feedback').forEach(box=>{
      box.style.setProperty('grid-template-columns','minmax(0,1fr)','important');
      box.style.setProperty('gap','0','important');
    });

    return removed;
  }

  function watch(){
    const lesson=document.getElementById('lesson');
    if(!lesson||!('MutationObserver' in window))return;
    try{observer?.disconnect?.()}catch(_){}
    observer=new MutationObserver(mutations=>{
      let needsKill=false;
      for(const m of mutations){
        if(m.addedNodes?.length){needsKill=true;break}
      }
      if(needsKill)kill(lesson);
    });
    observer.observe(lesson,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }

  /*
    Canonical lifecycle plus the structural observer is enough to suppress
    legacy guide nodes. This avoids the old multi-timeout render/grade burst.
  */
  document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',()=>{
    kill();
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>kill());
  });
  document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',()=>{
    kill();
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>kill());
  });

  /*
    Also neutralize the old celebration helper's visible reaction nodes.
    We keep the object itself intact for compatibility with older code.
  */
  function neutralizeCelebration(){
    try{
      const c=window.DEEN_CELEBRATION;
      if(c && !c.__deenNoHumanGuide){
        c.__deenNoHumanGuide=true;
        if(typeof c.test==='function'){
          const original=c.test.bind(c);
          c.test=(...args)=>{
            const r=original(...args);
            kill();
            requestAnimationFrame(()=>kill());
            return r;
          };
        }
      }
    }catch(_){}
  }

  window.DEEN_NO_HUMAN_GUIDE={
    version:VERSION,
    apply:()=>{neutralizeCelebration();return kill()},
    snapshot:()=>({
      visibleLegacyGuides:SELECTORS.reduce((n,sel)=>n+[...document.querySelectorAll(sel)].filter(el=>getComputedStyle(el).display!=='none'&&getComputedStyle(el).visibility!=='hidden').length,0),
      companion:document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-character-question-avatar')?.dataset?.animal||null
    })
  };

  kill();
  watch();
  neutralizeCelebration();
  [120,400,900,1600].forEach(ms=>setTimeout(()=>{
    kill();
    neutralizeCelebration();
  },ms));
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9950-companion-streak-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9950CompanionStreak)return;
 window.__deenV9950CompanionStreak=true;

 const VERSION='9.9.60-COMPANION-STREAK-ASSET-REPAIR';
 const IDS=['lion','wolf','owl','turtle','eagle','cat'];
 const LABELS={lion:'Aslan',wolf:'Kurt',owl:'Baykuş',turtle:'Kaplumbağa',eagle:'Kartal',cat:'Kedi'};
 const POSES={
   lion:[[0,0],[0,1]],
   wolf:[[1,0],[1,1]],
   owl:[[2,0],[2,1]],
   turtle:[[3,0],[3,1]],
   eagle:[[4,0],[4,1]],
   cat:[[5,0],[5,1]]
 };
 const COPY={
   3:'Harika gidiyorsun!',
   5:'Mükemmel seri!',
   10:'İnanılmaz odak!'
 };
 const DIRECT_ASSETS={
   lion:{3:'assets/companions/lion/lion_combo_3.webp?v=9292lioncatrepaired1',5:'assets/companions/lion/lion_combo_5.webp?v=9292lioncatrepaired1'},
   cat:{3:'assets/companions/cat/cat_combo_3.webp?v=9292lioncatrepaired1',5:'assets/companions/cat/cat_combo_5.webp?v=9292lioncatrepaired1'}
 };

 function assetFor(id,combo){
   const set=DIRECT_ASSETS[id];
   if(!set)return null;
   const pose=Number(combo)>=5?5:3;
   return{key:id+pose,url:set[pose],pose};
 }

 let lastKey='';
 let lastCombo=-1;
 let busy=false;

 function selected(){
   let id='';
   try{id=String(window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS?.selected?.()||'').toLowerCase()}catch(_){}
   if(!IDS.includes(id)){
     try{id=String(state?.profile?.companion||'').toLowerCase()}catch(_){}
   }
   return IDS.includes(id)?id:'owl';
 }

 function keyFor(q){
   return String(q?.id||q?.question_id||q?.prompt||q?.question||q?.text||session?.index||'');
 }

 function shouldCelebrate(n){
   return n===3||n===5||n===10||(n>10&&n%5===0);
 }

 function bgPos(row,col){
   return (col*100)+'% '+((row/5)*100)+'%';
 }

 function close(ov){
   if(!ov||!ov.isConnected)return;
   ov.classList.add('closing');
   setTimeout(()=>{ov.remove();busy=false},220);
 }

 function celebrate(combo){
   if(busy)return false;
   busy=true;

   document.getElementById('deenStreakOverlay')?.remove();

   const id=selected();
   const pair=POSES[id]||POSES.owl;
   const pose=pair[combo>=5?1:0];

   const ov=document.createElement('div');
   ov.id='deenStreakOverlay';
   ov.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');

   const card=document.createElement('div');
   card.className='deen9950-card';

   const mascot=document.createElement('div');
   mascot.className='deen9950-mascot';
   const direct=assetFor(id,combo);
   if(direct){
     mascot.dataset.directAsset=direct.key;
   }else{
     mascot.style.backgroundPosition=bgPos(pose[0],pose[1]);
   }

   const count=document.createElement('div');
   count.className='deen9950-count';
   count.textContent=combo+' SERİ';

   const copy=document.createElement('div');
   copy.className='deen9950-copy';
   copy.textContent=(COPY[combo]||'Seri devam ediyor!')+' '+LABELS[id]+' seninle.';

   card.append(mascot,count,copy);
   ov.appendChild(card);

   for(let i=0;i<12;i++){
     const p=document.createElement('i');
     p.className='deen9950-particle p'+i;
     ov.appendChild(p);
   }

   document.body.appendChild(ov);
   window.__DEEN_COMPANION_REACTION_LOCK=Date.now()+1400;

   try{window.DEEN_NO_HUMAN_GUIDE?.apply?.()}catch(_){}
   try{navigator.vibrate?.(combo>=10?[18,30,18,30,24]:[14,22,14])}catch(_){}

   setTimeout(()=>close(ov),combo>=10?1400:1150);
   return true;
 }

 function onGrade(correct,officialCombo,q){
   if(typeof correct!=='boolean')return false;

   const combo=correct?Math.max(0,Number(officialCombo)||0):0;
   const key=keyFor(q);

   // completeQuestion is authoritative. This only rejects accidental duplicate calls.
   if(key===lastKey && combo===lastCombo)return false;
   lastKey=key;
   lastCombo=combo;

   if(!correct)return true;

   if(shouldCelebrate(combo)){
     setTimeout(()=>celebrate(combo),180);
   }
   return true;
 }

 // Preload before the first milestone.
 try{
   const img=new Image();
   img.src='assets/combo/companions-sheet.png?v=9958assetcache1';
   Object.values(DIRECT_ASSETS).flatMap(x=>Object.values(x)).forEach(src=>{const p=new Image();p.src=src});
 }catch(_){}

 window.DEEN_COMPANION_STREAK={
   version:VERSION,
   onGrade,
   celebrate,
   selected,
   assetFor,
   get value(){try{return Number(session?.combo||0)||0}catch(_){return 0}},
   snapshot:()=>({
     combo:(()=>{try{return Number(session?.combo||0)||0}catch(_){return 0}})(),
     selected:selected(),
     busy,
     lastKey,
     lastCombo
   })
 };

 window.DEEN_COMBO=window.DEEN_COMPANION_STREAK;
 try{window.DEEN_NO_HUMAN_GUIDE?.apply?.()}catch(_){}
})();
</script>

 const questionJobs=new Map();
 let cancelledQuestionJobs=0;
 let executedQuestionJobs=0;

 function cancelQuestionJobs(label){
   const jobs=questionJobs.get(label);
   if(!jobs)return;
   for(const id of jobs.timeouts||[])try{clearTimeout(id)}catch(_){}
   for(const id of jobs.rafs||[])try{cancelAnimationFrame(id)}catch(_){}
   questionJobs.delete(label);
   cancelledQuestionJobs++;
 }
 function cancelAllQuestionJobs(){
   for(const label of [...questionJobs.keys()])cancelQuestionJobs(label);
 }
 function scheduleQuestion(label,detail,fn,opts={}){
   if(typeof fn!=='function')return false;
   const safeLabel=String(label||'anonymous');
   cancelQuestionJobs(safeLabel);
   const epoch=Number(detail?.epoch)||renderEpoch;
   const key=String(detail?.key||questionKey());
   const jobs={timeouts:[],rafs:[]};
   questionJobs.set(safeLabel,jobs);
   const valid=()=>epoch===renderEpoch&&key===questionKey();
   const run=()=>{
     if(!valid())return false;
     try{fn();executedQuestionJobs++;return true}
     catch(err){try{console.warn('DEEN scheduled question job failed',safeLabel,err)}catch(_){}return false}
   };
   if(opts?.raf===true)jobs.rafs.push(requestAnimationFrame(run));
   for(const ms of Array.isArray(opts?.delays)?opts.delays:[]){
     jobs.timeouts.push(setTimeout(run,Math.max(0,Number(ms)||0)));
   }
   return true;
 }

 function questionKey(q){
   let actual=q;
   try{actual=actual||session?.questions?.[Number(session?.index)||0]}catch(_){}
   let id='';
   try{id=String(actual?.id||actual?.question_id||actual?.prompt||actual?.question||actual?.text||'')}catch(_){}
   let idx='';
   try{idx=String(Number(session?.index)||0)}catch(_){idx='0'}
   return idx+'::'+id;
 }

 function dispatch(name,detail){
   try{document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(name,{detail}))}catch(_){}
 }
 function dispatchCancelable(name,detail){
   try{return document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(name,{detail,cancelable:true}))}catch(_){return true}
 }

 if(typeof LEGACY_NEXT_QUESTION==='function'){
   const releaseAdvanceLock=()=>{
     advanceBusy=false;
     advanceSession=null;
     advanceIndex=-1;
     advanceKey='';
   };

   document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',releaseAdvanceLock);
   document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-result',releaseAdvanceLock);
   document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-closed',releaseAdvanceLock);

   const guardedNextQuestion=function(...args){
     let activeSession=null,index=0,key='';
     try{activeSession=session||null;index=Number(session?.index)||0;key=questionKey()}catch(_){}

     /*
       The legacy game-feel nextQuestion schedules the real advance ~110 ms
       later. Keep the authoritative boundary locked until the next render or
       result so manual clicks and old auto-advance timers cannot create a
       second transition lifecycle for the same question.
     */
     if(!activeSession||advanceBusy||activeSession?.transitioning===true){
       blockedQuestionAdvances++;
       return false;
     }

     advanceBusy=true;
     advanceSession=activeSession;
     advanceIndex=index;
     advanceKey=key;
     cancelAllQuestionJobs();
     dispatch('deen:question-advancing',{session:activeSession,index,key});

     let out;
     try{
       out=LEGACY_NEXT_QUESTION.apply(this,args);
       questionAdvances++;
     }catch(err){
       releaseAdvanceLock();
       throw err;
     }

     dispatch('deen:question-advance-requested',{session:activeSession,index,key,count:questionAdvances,result:out});

     // Defensive unlock only when the legacy transition failed to reach render/result.
     setTimeout(()=>{
       if(!advanceBusy||advanceSession!==activeSession)return;
       let live=null,liveIndex=-1,transitioning=false;
       try{live=session||null;liveIndex=Number(session?.index)||0;transitioning=session?.transitioning===true}catch(_){}
       if(live!==activeSession||liveIndex!==index||!transitioning)releaseAdvanceLock();
     },900);

     return out;
   };
   guardedNextQuestion.__deen9953=true;
   guardedNextQuestion.__deen9953Original=LEGACY_NEXT_QUESTION;
   window.nextQuestion=guardedNextQuestion;
   try{nextQuestion=guardedNextQuestion}catch(_){}

if(typeof LEGACY_RENDER==='function'){
   const guardedRender=function(...args){
     /*
       One canonical render boundary. Pre-render cleanup/planning runs through
       deen:question-rendering; visual decorators run through question-rendered.
     */
     if(renderBusy)return false;
     renderBusy=true;
     cancelAllQuestionJobs();
     const nextEpoch=renderEpoch+1;
     dispatch('deen:question-rendering',{
       epoch:nextEpoch,
       index:(()=>{try{return Number(session?.index)||0}catch(_){return 0}})(),
       key:questionKey()
     });
     renderEpoch=nextEpoch;
     gradedEpoch=-1;
     lastGradeKey='';
     let out;
     try{
       out=LEGACY_RENDER.apply(this,args);
       renders++;
     }finally{
       renderBusy=false;
     }
     const epoch=renderEpoch;
     queueMicrotask(()=>dispatch('deen:question-rendered',{
       epoch,
       index:(()=>{try{return Number(session?.index)||0}catch(_){return 0}})(),
       key:questionKey()
     }));
     return out;
   };
   guardedRender.__deen9953=true;
   guardedRender.__deen9953Original=LEGACY_RENDER;
   window.renderQuestion=guardedRender;
   try{renderQuestion=guardedRender}catch(_){}
 }

 if(typeof LEGACY_COMPLETE==='function'){
   const guardedComplete=function(correct,q,...rest){
     /*
       completeQuestion mutates answered/correct/combo immediately. A double
       click or duplicated template callback therefore may grade one render
       epoch only once. Features that must run before grading use the canonical
       synchronous deen:question-grading event instead of wrapping this method.
     */
     let actualQuestion=q;
     try{actualQuestion=actualQuestion||session?.questions?.[Number(session?.index)||0]||null}catch(_){}
     const key=questionKey(actualQuestion);

     if(gradedEpoch===renderEpoch){
       blockedGrades++;
       return false;
     }

     gradedEpoch=renderEpoch;
     lastGradeKey=key;

     dispatch('deen:question-grading',{
       epoch:renderEpoch,
       key,
       correct:correct===true,
       combo:(()=>{try{return Number(session?.combo)||0}catch(_){return 0}})(),
       index:(()=>{try{return Number(session?.index)||0}catch(_){return 0}})(),
       question:actualQuestion
     });

     let out;
     try{
       out=LEGACY_COMPLETE.call(this,correct,q,...rest);
       completedGrades++;
     }catch(err){
       gradedEpoch=-1;
       lastGradeKey='';
       throw err;
     }

     const detail={
       epoch:renderEpoch,
       key,
       correct:correct===true,
       combo:(()=>{try{return Number(session?.combo)||0}catch(_){return 0}})(),
       index:(()=>{try{return Number(session?.index)||0}catch(_){return 0}})(),
       question:actualQuestion
     };
     queueMicrotask(()=>dispatch('deen:question-graded',detail));
     return out;
   };
   guardedComplete.__deen9953=true;
   guardedComplete.__deen9953Original=LEGACY_COMPLETE;
   window.completeQuestion=guardedComplete;
   try{completeQuestion=guardedComplete}catch(_){}
 }

 if(typeof LEGACY_RESULT==='function'){
   const guardedResult=function(...args){
     let activeSession=null;
     try{activeSession=session||null}catch(_){}
     dispatch('deen:lesson-result-before',{
       session:activeSession,
       correct:(()=>{try{return Number(activeSession?.correct)||0}catch(_){return 0}})(),
       total:(()=>{try{return Number(activeSession?.questions?.length)||0}catch(_){return 0}})()
     });
     const out=LEGACY_RESULT.apply(this,args);
     queueMicrotask(()=>dispatch('deen:lesson-result',{
       session:activeSession,
       correct:(()=>{try{return Number(activeSession?.correct)||0}catch(_){return 0}})(),
       total:(()=>{try{return Number(activeSession?.questions?.length)||0}catch(_){return 0}})()
     }));
     return out;
   };
   guardedResult.__deen9953=true;
   guardedResult.__deen9953Original=LEGACY_RESULT;
   window.showResult=guardedResult;
   try{showResult=guardedResult}catch(_){}
 }


 if(typeof LEGACY_UPDATE==='function'){
   const guardedUpdate=function(...args){
     const outer=uiDepth===0;
     if(outer)dispatch('deen:ui-updating',{count:uiUpdates+1});
     uiDepth++;
     let out;
     try{
       out=LEGACY_UPDATE.apply(this,args);
       if(outer)uiUpdates++;
     }finally{
       uiDepth=Math.max(0,uiDepth-1);
     }
     if(outer)queueMicrotask(()=>dispatch('deen:ui-updated',{count:uiUpdates}));
     return out;
   };
   guardedUpdate.__deen9953=true;
   guardedUpdate.__deen9953Original=LEGACY_UPDATE;
   window.updateUI=guardedUpdate;
   try{updateUI=guardedUpdate}catch(_){}
 }

 window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE={
   question:scheduleQuestion,
   cancel:cancelQuestionJobs,
   cancelAll:cancelAllQuestionJobs,
   snapshot:()=>({
     groups:questionJobs.size,
     cancelled:cancelledQuestionJobs,
     executed:executedQuestionJobs
   })
 };
 window.DEEN_RUNTIME_ARBITER={
   version:VERSION,
   snapshot:()=>({
     renderEpoch,
     gradedEpoch,
     lastGradeKey,
     renderBusy,
     blockedGrades,
     completedGrades,
     renders,
     renderGuarded:!!window.renderQuestion?.__deen9953,
     gradeGuarded:!!window.completeQuestion?.__deen9953,
     resultGuarded:!!window.showResult?.__deen9953,
     uiGuarded:!!window.updateUI?.__deen9953,
     uiDepth,
     uiUpdates,
     preRenderEvent:true,
     preGradeEvent:true,
     preResultEvent:true,
     uiLifecycleEvents:true,
     lessonStarts,
     blockedLessonStarts,
     lessonFinishes,
     pathRenders,
     startGuarded:!!window.startStage?.__deen9953,
     finishGuarded:!!window.finishLesson?.__deen9953,
     pathsGuarded:!!window.renderPaths?.__deen9953,
     lessonLifecycleEvents:true,
     pathLifecycleEvents:true,
     returnCardRenders,
     profileRenders,
     ceremonies,
     sectionResults,
     returnCardGuarded:!!window.returnCard?.__deen9953,
     profileGuarded:!!window.profileView?.__deen9953,
     ceremonyGuarded:!!window.ceremony?.__deen9953,
     sectionResultGuarded:!!window.showSectionFinalResult?.__deen9953,
     viewLifecycleEvents:true,

<script id="deen-v9993-question-transition-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9993QuestionTransition)return;
 window.__deenV9993QuestionTransition=true;

 const VERSION='9.9.93-QUESTION-TRANSITION';
 const BUILD='9993transition1';
 let advances=0,renderResets=0,blockedSynthetic=0,last=null;

 const frame=()=>document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');

 function fadeStreak(){
   const ov=document.getElementById('deenStreakOverlay');
   if(ov&&ov.isConnected)ov.classList.add('closing');
 }

 function clearTransient(source='rendering'){
   const f=frame();
   try{window.DEEN_FEEDBACK_LAYOUT?.reset?.()}catch(_){}
   try{window.DEEN_COMPANION_REACTIONS?.clear?.()}catch(_){}
   try{window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.cancel?.('v9992-feedback-companion')}catch(_){}
   window.__DEEN_COMPANION_REACTION_LOCK=0;

   const fb=document.getElementById('feedback');
   fb?.classList.remove('show');
   if(fb)fb.scrollTop=0;
   const box=document.getElementById('feedbackBox');
   if(box)box.scrollTop=0;

   if(f){
     f.classList.remove(
       'v9945-feedback-mode','v9945-feedback-correct','v9945-feedback-wrong',
       'v9944-feedback-correct','v9944-feedback-wrong','v9944-feedback-celebrate'
     );
     f.querySelectorAll('.v9944-reaction-chip,.v9944-particles').forEach(el=>el.remove());
   }

   if(source==='rendering')renderResets++;
   return true;
 }

 function onAdvancing(event){
   advances++;
   const f=frame();
   f?.classList.add('deen9993-transitioning');
   const btn=document.getElementById('continueBtn');
   if(btn)btn.disabled=true;
   fadeStreak();
   last={
     at:Date.now(),
     index:Number(event?.detail?.index)||0,
     key:String(event?.detail?.key||'')
   };
 }

 function onRendering(){
   clearTransient('rendering');
 }

 function onRendered(){
   const f=frame();
   f?.classList.remove('deen9993-transitioning','gf-question-out');
   const sc=document.querySelector('#quizView .qarea');
   if(sc){sc.scrollTop=0;requestAnimationFrame(()=>{if(sc.isConnected)sc.scrollTop=0})}
   try{window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS?.renderLesson?.('lesson_idle')}catch(_){}
 }

 /*
   Old v8.5x minigames call continueBtn.click() about one second after a
   correct answer. Feedback is now intentionally user-paced. Reject only those
   synthetic button clicks; real pointer/keyboard activation remains normal.
 */
 document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
   const btn=event.target?.closest?.('#continueBtn');
   if(!btn||event.isTrusted)return;
   if(window.__DEEN_ALLOW_PROGRAMMATIC_CONTINUE===true)return;
   event.preventDefault();
   event.stopImmediatePropagation();
   blockedSynthetic++;
 },true);

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-advancing',onAdvancing);
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendering',onRendering);
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',onRendered);
 document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-result',()=>{
   frame()?.classList.remove('deen9993-transitioning');
   fadeStreak();
 });

 window.DEEN_QUESTION_TRANSITION={
   version:VERSION,
   build:BUILD,
   clear:clearTransient,
   snapshot:()=>({
     version:VERSION,
     build:BUILD,
     advances,
     renderResets,
     blockedSynthetic,
     transitioning:!!frame()?.classList.contains('deen9993-transitioning'),
     feedbackVisible:!!document.getElementById('feedback')?.classList.contains('show'),
     runtime:window.DEEN_RUNTIME_ARBITER?.snapshot?.()||null,
     last:last?{...last}:null
   })
 };
})();
</script>

<!-- DEEN v9.9.87 — Runtime regression guard: non-destructive ownership/lifecycle self-check -->
<script id="deen-v9988-runtime-regression-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9988RuntimeRegression)return;
 window.__deenV9988RuntimeRegression=true;
 const VERSION='9.10.9-RUNTIME-REGRESSION';
 const BUILD='9109wolfhd1';
 let last=null,runs=0;

 function checks(){
  const finals=['startSectionFinal','startSection2Final','startSection3Final'];
  return {
   renderOwner:!!window.renderQuestion?.__deen9953,
   gradeOwner:!!window.completeQuestion?.__deen9953,
   resultOwner:!!window.showResult?.__deen9953,
   uiOwner:!!window.updateUI?.__deen9953,
   startOwner:!!window.startStage?.__deen9953,
   finishOwner:!!window.finishLesson?.__deen9953,
   nextOwner:!!window.nextQuestion?.__deen9953,
   closeOwner:!!window.closeLesson?.__deen9953,
   navigateOwner:!!window.navigate?.__deen9953,
   reviewOwner:!!window.startSmartReview?.__deen9953,
   macroOwner:!!window.startMacroStage?.__deen9953,
   finalOwners:finals.every(name=>!!window[name]?.__deen9953),
   streakOwner:!!window.streakPanel?.__deen9953,
   energyOwner:!!window.showEnergy?.__deen9953,
   rewardOwner:!!window.rewardAd?.__deen9953,
   scheduler:typeof window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question==='function',
   arbiter:window.DEEN_RUNTIME_ARBITER?.version==='9.9.93-RUNTIME-ARBITER',
   noLateRenderWrapper:!window.renderQuestion?.__v873&&!window.renderQuestion?.__v890&&!window.renderQuestion?.__v891,
   noLateStartWrapper:!window.startStage?.__v910LearningLoop,
   build:!window.DEEN_BUILD_ID||window.DEEN_BUILD_ID===BUILD
  };
 }

 function run(){
  const values=checks(),failed=Object.entries(values).filter(([,ok])=>ok!==true).map(([name])=>name);
  last={version:VERSION,build:BUILD,at:Date.now(),run:++runs,pass:failed.length===0,failed,checks:values};
  document.documentElement.dataset.deenRuntimeRegression=last.pass?'pass':'fail';
  if(!last.pass)try{console.error('DEEN runtime regression guard failed',last)}catch(_){}
  return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(last));
 }

 window.DEEN_RUNTIME_REGRESSION={
  version:VERSION,
  build:BUILD,
  run,
  snapshot:()=>last?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(last)):run()
 };

 [0,250,1200,2500].forEach(ms=>setTimeout(run,ms));
})();
</script>

<!-- DEEN v9.9.91 — Bridge authoritative graded lifecycle to companion streak reactions -->
<script id="deen-v9991-streak-reaction-bridge-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9991StreakReactionBridge)return;
 window.__deenV9991StreakReactionBridge=true;

 const VERSION='9.9.91-STREAK-REACTION-BRIDGE';
 const BUILD='9991streakbridge1';
 let graded=0,forwarded=0,milestones=0,last=null;

 function isMilestone(combo){
   return combo===3||combo===5||combo===10||(combo>10&&combo%5===0);
 }

 function forward(event){
   const detail=event?.detail||{};
   const correct=detail.correct===true;
   const combo=correct?Math.max(0,Number(detail.combo)||0):0;
   const question=detail.question||null;
   graded++;

   let handled=false,error='';
   try{
     const api=window.DEEN_COMPANION_STREAK||window.DEEN_COMBO;
     if(api&&typeof api.onGrade==='function'){
       handled=api.onGrade(correct,combo,question)!==false;
       if(handled)forwarded++;
       if(handled&&correct&&isMilestone(combo))milestones++;
     }else{
       error='streak-api-missing';
     }
   }catch(err){
     error=String(err?.message||err||'streak-forward-failed');
     try{console.warn('DEEN streak reaction bridge failed',err)}catch(_){}
   }

   last={
     at:Date.now(),
     epoch:Number(detail.epoch)||0,
     key:String(detail.key||''),
     correct,
     combo,
     handled,
     milestone:correct&&isMilestone(combo),
     error
   };
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',forward);

 window.DEEN_STREAK_REACTION_BRIDGE={
   version:VERSION,
   build:BUILD,
   forward,
   snapshot:()=>({
     version:VERSION,
     build:BUILD,
     graded,
     forwarded,
     milestones,
     listener:true,
     streakApi:typeof (window.DEEN_COMPANION_STREAK||window.DEEN_COMBO)?.onGrade==='function',
     last:last?{...last}:null
   })
 };
})();
</script>
