<script id="deen-v9992-feedback-companion-persistence-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9992FeedbackCompanionPersistence)return;
 window.__deenV9992FeedbackCompanionPersistence=true;

 const VERSION='9.9.92-FEEDBACK-COMPANION-PERSISTENCE';
 const BUILD='9992companionpersist1';
 let repairs=0,last=null;

 async function ensure(detail){
   const f=document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');
   if(!f)return false;

   let row=f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-row');
   if(!row){
     try{window.DEEN_CHARACTER_QUESTION?.apply?.()}catch(_){}
     row=f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-row');
   }
   if(!row)return false;

   let avatar=row.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar');
   if(!avatar)return false;

   avatar.style.setProperty('display','grid','important');
   avatar.style.setProperty('visibility','visible','important');
   avatar.style.setProperty('opacity','1','important');

   let img=avatar.querySelector('.deen-companion-runtime-img');
   if(!img){
     try{await window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS?.renderLesson?.('lesson_idle')}catch(_){}
     avatar=row.querySelector('.deen-character-question-avatar');
     img=avatar?.querySelector('.deen-companion-runtime-img')||null;
     if(img)repairs++;
   }

   if(img){
     img.style.setProperty('display','block','important');
     img.style.setProperty('visibility','visible','important');
     img.style.setProperty('opacity','1','important');
   }

   last={
     at:Date.now(),
     epoch:Number(detail?.epoch)||0,
     key:String(detail?.key||''),
     correct:detail?.correct===true,
     row:!!row,
     avatar:!!avatar,
     image:!!img,
     animal:avatar?.dataset?.animal||null
   };
   return !!img;
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',event=>{
   const detail=event?.detail||{};
   const run=()=>ensure(detail);
   if(!window.DEEN_RUNTIME_SCHEDULE?.question?.(
     'v9992-feedback-companion',
     detail,
     run,
     {raf:true,delays:[24,120,360]}
   )){
     requestAnimationFrame(run);
     setTimeout(run,120);
   }
 });

 window.DEEN_FEEDBACK_COMPANION_PERSISTENCE={
   version:VERSION,
   build:BUILD,
   ensure,
   snapshot:()=>({
     version:VERSION,
     build:BUILD,
     repairs,
     row:!!document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-character-question-row'),
     avatar:!!document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-character-question-avatar'),
     image:!!document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-companion-runtime-img'),
     last:last?{...last}:null
   })
 };
})();

<script id="deen-v9994-combo-media-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9994ComboMedia)return;
 window.__deenV9994ComboMedia=true;

 const VERSION='9.9.94-COMBO-MEDIA';
 const BUILD='9994combogif1';

 // Drop the user's real animated files at these paths when available.
 // Until then, onerror falls back to the selected companion celebrate asset.
 const MEDIA=[
   'assets/combo/combo-gif-1.gif?v=9994combogif1',
   'assets/combo/combo-gif-2.gif?v=9994combogif1'
 ];

 let active=null;
 let closeTimer=0;
 let lastPick=-1;
 let lastKey='';
 let shows=0;
 let fallbackLoads=0;

 function selected(){
   try{return String(window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS?.selected?.()||state?.profile?.companion||'owl').toLowerCase()}
   catch(_){return 'owl'}
 }

 function fallbackSrc(){
   const id=selected();
   try{
     const src=window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS?.asset?.('celebrate');
     if(src)return src+(src.includes('?')?'&':'?')+'v='+encodeURIComponent(window.DEEN_BUILD_ID||BUILD);
   }catch(_){}
   return 'assets/companions/'+id+'/'+id+'_celebrate.png?v='+BUILD;
 }

 function pick(){
   if(MEDIA.length<2)return 0;
   let n=Math.floor(Math.random()*MEDIA.length);
   if(n===lastPick)n=(n+1)%MEDIA.length;
   lastPick=n;
   return n;
 }

 function clear(immediate=false){
   clearTimeout(closeTimer);
   closeTimer=0;
   const node=active;
   active=null;
   if(!node)return false;
   if(immediate){
     try{node.remove()}catch(_){}
     return true;
   }
   node.classList.add('closing');
   setTimeout(()=>{try{node.remove()}catch(_){}},210);
   return true;
 }

 function show(key=''){
   if(window.__DEEN_COMBO_OWNER_V9102===true)return false;
   if(key&&key===lastKey)return false;
   if(key)lastKey=key;

   clear(true);
   const idx=pick();

   const ov=document.createElement('div');
   ov.id='deenComboMediaOverlay';
   ov.className=idx===1?'variant-b':'variant-a';
   ov.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');

   const img=document.createElement('img');
   img.className='deen9994-media';
   img.alt='';
   img.decoding='async';
   img.src=MEDIA[idx];

   let fallbackUsed=false;
   img.onerror=()=>{
     if(fallbackUsed)return;
     fallbackUsed=true;
     fallbackLoads++;
     img.src=fallbackSrc();
   };

   const badge=document.createElement('div');
   badge.className='deen9994-badge';
   badge.textContent='3 SERİ ✦';

   ov.append(img,badge);
   document.body.appendChild(ov);
   active=ov;
   shows++;

   try{navigator.vibrate?.([15,24,15])}catch(_){}
   closeTimer=setTimeout(()=>clear(false),1450);

<script id="deen-v9995-combo-ensure-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9995ComboEnsure)return;
 window.__deenV9995ComboEnsure=true;

 const VERSION='9.9.95-COMBO-ENSURE';
 const BUILD='9995comboensure1';
 let directTriggers=0;
 let eventTriggers=0;
 let patched=false;
 let lastKey='';

 function currentKey(q){
   let idx=0;
   try{idx=Number(session?.index)||0}catch(_){}
   return idx+'::'+String(q?.id||q?.question_id||q?.question||q?.prompt||'');
 }

 function enhance(){
   const ov=document.getElementById('deenComboMediaOverlay');
   if(!ov||ov.dataset.deen9995Enhanced==='1')return false;
   ov.dataset.deen9995Enhanced='1';

   const ring=document.createElement('i');
   ring.className='deen9995-ring';
   ov.prepend(ring);

   for(let i=0;i<10;i++){
     const s=document.createElement('i');
     s.className='deen9995-spark s'+i;
     ov.appendChild(s);
   }
   return true;
 }

 function forceShow(key=''){
   if(window.__DEEN_COMBO_OWNER_V9102===true)return false;
   if(key&&key===lastKey&&document.getElementById('deenComboMediaOverlay'))return false;
   if(key)lastKey=key;

   const api=window.DEEN_COMBO_MEDIA;
   let shown=false;
   try{shown=api?.show?.(key)!==false}catch(_){shown=false}

   /*
     The user's uploaded files currently decode as blank single-frame PNGs.
     Force the selected companion celebration image immediately so milestone
     feedback is still visible. When valid combo GIF assets exist, v9994 can
     use them without changing this trigger contract.
   */
   const applyFallback=()=>{
     const ov=document.getElementById('deenComboMediaOverlay');
     if(!ov)return;
     const img=ov.querySelector('.deen9994-media');
     if(img){
       let src='';
       try{src=window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS?.asset?.('celebrate')||''}catch(_){}
       if(!src){
         let id='owl';
         try{id=String(window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS?.selected?.()||state?.profile?.companion||'owl').toLowerCase()}catch(_){}
         src='assets/companions/'+id+'/'+id+'_celebrate.png';
       }
       const sep=src.includes('?')?'&':'?';
       img.src=src+sep+'v='+encodeURIComponent(window.DEEN_BUILD_ID||BUILD);
     }
     enhance();
   };

   requestAnimationFrame(applyFallback);
   setTimeout(applyFallback,45);
   return shown;
 }

 function installDirectBridge(){
   if(window.__DEEN_COMBO_TIERS_ACTIVE)return false;
   const api=window.DEEN_COMPANION_STREAK||window.DEEN_COMBO;
   if(!api||typeof api.onGrade!=='function'||api.onGrade.__deen9995)return false;

   const original=api.onGrade.bind(api);
   const wrapped=function(correct,combo,q){
     if(window.__DEEN_COMBO_TIERS_ACTIVE)return false;
     const result=original(correct,combo,q);
     const n=Math.max(0,Number(combo)||0);
     if(correct===true&&n===3){
       directTriggers++;
       forceShow(currentKey(q));
     }
     if(correct!==true){
       try{window.DEEN_COMBO_MEDIA?.clear?.(true)}catch(_){}
       lastKey='';
     }
     return result;
   };
   wrapped.__deen9995=true;
   wrapped.__deen9995Original=original;
   api.onGrade=wrapped;
   window.DEEN_COMPANION_STREAK=api;
   window.DEEN_COMBO=api;
   patched=true;
   return true;
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',event=>{
   if(window.__DEEN_COMBO_TIERS_ACTIVE)return;
   const d=event?.detail||{};
   const combo=Math.max(0,Number(d.combo)||0);
   if(d.correct===true&&combo===3){
     eventTriggers++;
     forceShow(String(d.key||currentKey(d.question)));
   }
 });

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',()=>installDirectBridge());
 document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-started',()=>installDirectBridge());

 installDirectBridge();
 [0,80,300,900].forEach(ms=>setTimeout(installDirectBridge,ms));

 window.DEEN_COMBO_ENSURE={
   version:VERSION,
   build:BUILD,
   forceShow,
   install:installDirectBridge,
   snapshot:()=>({
     version:VERSION,
     build:BUILD,
     patched,
     directTriggers,
     eventTriggers,
     active:!!document.getElementById('deenComboMediaOverlay'),
     lastKey
   })
 };
})();

<script id="deen-v9996-combo-webm-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9996ComboWebm)return;
 window.__deenV9996ComboWebm=true;

 const VERSION='9.9.96-COMBO-WEBM';
 const BUILD='9996combowebm1';
 const MEDIA=[
   'assets/combo/combo-1.webm?v='+BUILD,
   'assets/combo/combo-2.webm?v='+BUILD
 ];

 let active=null;
 let timer=0;
 let lastPick=-1;
 let lastKey='';
 let shows=0;
 let videoErrors=0;
 let directTriggers=0;
 let eventTriggers=0;

 function keyFor(detail,q){
   if(detail?.key)return String(detail.key);
   let idx=0;
   try{idx=Number(detail?.index ?? session?.index ?? 0)||0}catch(_){}
   return idx+'::'+String(q?.id||q?.question_id||q?.question||q?.prompt||'');
 }

 function pick(){
   if(MEDIA.length<2)return 0;
   let n=Math.floor(Math.random()*MEDIA.length);
   if(n===lastPick)n=(n+1)%MEDIA.length;
   lastPick=n;
   return n;
 }

 function fallback(video){
   videoErrors++;
   const wrap=video?.parentElement;
   if(!wrap)return;
   let src='';
   try{src=window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS?.asset?.('celebrate')||''}catch(_){}
   if(!src)return;
   const img=document.createElement('img');
   img.className='deen9996-video';
   img.alt='';
   img.src=src+(src.includes('?')?'&':'?')+'v='+BUILD;
   video.replaceWith(img);
 }

 function clear(immediate=false){
   clearTimeout(timer);
   timer=0;
   const node=active;
   active=null;
   if(!node)return false;
   try{
     const v=node.querySelector('video');
     if(v){v.pause();v.removeAttribute('src');v.load()}
   }catch(_){}
   if(immediate){try{node.remove()}catch(_){};return true}
   node.classList.add('closing');
   setTimeout(()=>{try{node.remove()}catch(_){}},190);
   return true;
 }

 function disableLegacy(){
   try{
     if(window.DEEN_COMBO_MEDIA && !window.DEEN_COMBO_MEDIA.__deen9996Disabled){
       window.DEEN_COMBO_MEDIA.__deen9996Disabled=true;
       window.DEEN_COMBO_MEDIA.__deen9996OriginalShow=window.DEEN_COMBO_MEDIA.show;
       window.DEEN_COMBO_MEDIA.show=()=>false;
       window.DEEN_COMBO_MEDIA.clear?.(true);
     }
   }catch(_){}
   document.getElementById('deenComboMediaOverlay')?.remove();
 }

 function show(detail={}){
   if(window.__DEEN_COMBO_OWNER_V9102===true)return false;
   if(window.__DEEN_COMBO_CANVAS_ACTIVE===true)return false;
   disableLegacy();
   const q=detail?.question||null;
   const key=keyFor(detail,q);
   if(key&&key===lastKey&&active)return false;
   lastKey=key;

   clear(true);

   const ov=document.createElement('div');
   ov.id='deenComboWebmOverlay';
   ov.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');

   const wrap=document.createElement('div');
   wrap.className='deen9996-wrap';

   const video=document.createElement('video');
   video.className='deen9996-video';
   video.autoplay=true;
   video.muted=true;
   video.loop=false;
   video.playsInline=true;
   video.preload='auto';
   video.src=MEDIA[pick()];
   video.onerror=()=>fallback(video);

   const badge=document.createElement('div');
   badge.className='deen9996-badge';
   badge.textContent='3 SERİ ✦';

   wrap.append(video,badge);
   ov.appendChild(wrap);
   document.body.appendChild(ov);
   active=ov;
   shows++;

   const play=()=>{try{video.play()?.catch?.(()=>{})}catch(_){}};
   play();
   video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',play,{once:true});
   video.addEventListener('ended',()=>clear(false),{once:true});

   timer=setTimeout(()=>clear(false),5200);
   try{navigator.vibrate?.([18,26,18])}catch(_){}
   return true;
 }

 function maybe(detail={}){
   const combo=Math.max(0,Number(detail.combo)||0);
   if(detail.correct===true&&combo===3)return show(detail);
   if(detail.correct!==true){
     clear(true);
     lastKey='';
   }
   return false;
 }

 function installDirect(){
   if(window.__DEEN_COMBO_TIERS_ACTIVE)return false;
   const api=window.DEEN_COMPANION_STREAK||window.DEEN_COMBO;
   if(!api||typeof api.onGrade!=='function'||api.onGrade.__deen9996)return false;
   const original=api.onGrade.bind(api);
   const wrapped=function(correct,combo,q){
     if(window.__DEEN_COMBO_TIERS_ACTIVE)return false;
     const out=original(correct,combo,q);
     if(correct===true&&Math.max(0,Number(combo)||0)===3){
       directTriggers++;
       show({correct:true,combo,question:q,key:keyFor({},q)});
     }else if(correct!==true){
       clear(true);
       lastKey='';
     }
     return out;
   };
   wrapped.__deen9996=true;
   wrapped.__deen9996Original=original;
   api.onGrade=wrapped;
   window.DEEN_COMPANION_STREAK=api;
   window.DEEN_COMBO=api;
   return true;
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',event=>{
   if(window.__DEEN_COMBO_TIERS_ACTIVE)return;
   const d=event?.detail||{};
   if(d.correct===true&&Math.max(0,Number(d.combo)||0)===3)eventTriggers++;
   maybe(d);
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-advancing',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendering',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-result',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-closed',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:navigating',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',()=>{disableLegacy();installDirect()});
 document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-started',()=>{disableLegacy();installDirect()});

 disableLegacy();
 installDirect();
 [0,100,350,900].forEach(ms=>setTimeout(()=>{disableLegacy();installDirect()},ms));

 window.DEEN_COMBO_WEBM={
   version:VERSION,
   build:BUILD,
   media:[...MEDIA],
   show,
   clear,
   snapshot:()=>({
     version:VERSION,build:BUILD,shows,videoErrors,directTriggers,eventTriggers,
     active:!!active,lastPick,lastKey
   })
 };
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9997-combo-canvas-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9997ComboCanvas)return;
 window.__deenV9997ComboCanvas=true;
 window.__DEEN_COMBO_CANVAS_ACTIVE=true;

 const VERSION='9.10.5-SINGLE-COMPANION-LEGACY';
 const BUILD='9105lionassetfix1';
 const RENDER_MIN_WIDTH=480;
 const RENDER_MAX_WIDTH=640;

 const MEDIA_BY_COMPANION={
   lion:[
     'assets/combo/combo-1.webm?v='+BUILD,
     'assets/combo/combo-2.webm?v='+BUILD
   ]
 };

 let active=null;
 let timer=0;
 let frameHandle=0;
 let lastPick=-1;
 let lastKey='';
 let shows=0;
 let keyedFrames=0;
 let renderErrors=0;
 let eventTriggers=0;

 function selectedCompanion(){
   try{return String(window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS?.selected?.()||state?.profile?.companion||'lion').toLowerCase()}
   catch(_){return 'lion'}
 }

 function mediaFor(id){
   return MEDIA_BY_COMPANION[id]||null;
 }

 function questionKey(detail={}){
   if(detail.key)return String(detail.key);
   const q=detail.question||null;
   let idx=0;
   try{idx=Number(detail.index ?? session?.index ?? 0)||0}catch(_){}
   return idx+'::'+String(q?.id||q?.question_id||q?.question||q?.prompt||'');
 }

 function pick(list){
   if(!list?.length)return null;
   if(list.length===1)return list[0];
   let n=Math.floor(Math.random()*list.length);
   if(n===lastPick)n=(n+1)%list.length;
   lastPick=n;
   return list[n];
 }

 function stopFrameLoop(video){
   if(!frameHandle)return;
   try{
     if(typeof video?.cancelVideoFrameCallback==='function')video.cancelVideoFrameCallback(frameHandle);
     else cancelAnimationFrame(frameHandle);
   }catch(_){}
   frameHandle=0;
 }

 function clear(immediate=false){
   clearTimeout(timer);
   timer=0;
   const node=active;
   active=null;
   if(!node)return false;

   const video=node.querySelector('video');
   stopFrameLoop(video);
   try{
     if(video){
       video.pause();
       video.removeAttribute('src');
       video.load();
     }
   }catch(_){}

   if(immediate){
     try{node.remove()}catch(_){}
     return true;
   }
   node.classList.add('closing');
   setTimeout(()=>{try{node.remove()}catch(_){}},190);
   return true;
 }

 function disableOldOverlays(){
   document.getElementById('deenComboMediaOverlay')?.remove();
   document.getElementById('deenComboWebmOverlay')?.remove();

   try{
     if(window.DEEN_COMBO_MEDIA){
       window.DEEN_COMBO_MEDIA.show=()=>false;
       window.DEEN_COMBO_MEDIA.clear?.(true);
     }
   }catch(_){}
   try{
     if(window.DEEN_COMBO_WEBM){
       window.DEEN_COMBO_WEBM.show=()=>false;
       window.DEEN_COMBO_WEBM.clear?.(true);
     }
   }catch(_){}
 }

 function fallbackCelebrate(stage){
   let src='';
   try{src=window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS?.asset?.('celebrate')||''}catch(_){}
   if(!src)return false;
   const img=document.createElement('img');
   img.className='deen9997-canvas';
   img.alt='';
   img.src=src+(src.includes('?')?'&':'?')+'v='+BUILD;
   stage.replaceChildren(img);
   requestAnimationFrame(()=>stage.classList.add('deen9998-ready'));
   return true;
 }

 function keyBlackPixels(ctx,w,h){
   const frame=ctx.getImageData(0,0,w,h);
   const d=frame.data;

   for(let i=0;i<d.length;i+=4){
     const a=d[i+3];
     if(a===0)continue;

     const r=d[i],g=d[i+1],b=d[i+2];
     const max=Math.max(r,g,b);
     const min=Math.min(r,g,b);
     const spread=max-min;

     // Pure/near-black matte -> transparent.
     if(max<=18){
       d[i+3]=0;
       continue;
     }

     // Feather neutral dark antialiasing so no black rectangle/halo remains.
     if(max<56 && spread<13){
       const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(max-18)/38));
       d[i+3]=Math.round(a*t*t);
     }
   }

   ctx.putImageData(frame,0,0);
   keyedFrames++;
 }

 function renderLoop(video,canvas,ctx,stage){
   let firstFrame=false;
   stage.dataset.deenCanvasFrame='0';
   const render=()=>{
     if(!active||!video.isConnected||video.ended)return;

     try{
       if(video.readyState>=2 && video.videoWidth>0 && video.videoHeight>0){
         const dpr=Math.max(1,Math.min(3,Number(window.devicePixelRatio)||1));
         const cssWidth=Math.max(1,Math.round(stage.getBoundingClientRect().width||300));
         const w=Math.max(RENDER_MIN_WIDTH,Math.min(RENDER_MAX_WIDTH,Math.round(cssWidth*dpr)));
         const h=Math.max(1,Math.round(w*(video.videoHeight/video.videoWidth)));
         if(canvas.width!==w||canvas.height!==h){
           canvas.width=w;
           canvas.height=h;
           ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;
           try{ctx.imageSmoothingQuality='high'}catch(_){}
         }
         ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);
         ctx.drawImage(video,0,0,w,h);
         keyBlackPixels(ctx,w,h);
         if(!firstFrame){
           firstFrame=true;
           stage.dataset.deenCanvasFrame='1';
           requestAnimationFrame(()=>stage.classList.add('deen9998-ready'));
         }
       }
     }catch(err){
       renderErrors++;
       try{console.warn('DEEN combo canvas render failed',err)}catch(_){}
     }

     if(typeof video.requestVideoFrameCallback==='function'){
       frameHandle=video.requestVideoFrameCallback(render);
     }else{
       frameHandle=requestAnimationFrame(render);
     }
   };

   if(typeof video.requestVideoFrameCallback==='function'){
     frameHandle=video.requestVideoFrameCallback(render);
   }else{
     frameHandle=requestAnimationFrame(render);
   }
 }

 function show(detail={}){
   if(window.__DEEN_COMBO_OWNER_V9102===true)return false;
   if(window.__DEEN_SINGLE_COMPANION_COMBO_ACTIVE===true)return false;
   disableOldOverlays();

   const companion=selectedCompanion();
   const media=mediaFor(companion);
   const key=questionKey(detail);

   if(key&&key===lastKey&&active)return false;
   lastKey=key;

   clear(true);

   const quiz=document.getElementById('quizView');
   if(!quiz)return false;

   const ov=document.createElement('div');
   ov.id='deenComboCanvasOverlay';
   ov.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');

   const head=document.querySelector('#quizView .lesson-head');
   ov.style.top=Math.max(0,Math.round(head?.getBoundingClientRect?.().height||70))+'px';

   const stage=document.createElement('div');
   stage.className='deen9997-stage';

   // Only lion videos exist for now. Other companions keep a safe static fallback
   // until their own combo media are added.
   if(!media?.length){
     ov.appendChild(stage);
     quiz.appendChild(ov);
     active=ov;
     shows++;
     fallbackCelebrate(stage);
     timer=setTimeout(()=>clear(false),1500);
     return true;
   }

   const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
   canvas.className='deen9997-canvas';
   canvas.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');

   const video=document.createElement('video');
   video.className='deen9997-video-source';
   video.muted=true;
   video.defaultMuted=true;
   video.autoplay=false;
   video.loop=false;
   video.playsInline=true;
   video.preload='auto';
   video.setAttribute('playsinline','');
   video.src=pick(media);

   stage.append(canvas,video);
   ov.appendChild(stage);
   quiz.appendChild(ov);
   active=ov;
   shows++;

   const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:true,willReadFrequently:true});
   if(!ctx){
     renderErrors++;
     fallbackCelebrate(stage);
     timer=setTimeout(()=>clear(false),1500);
     return true;
   }

   let watchdog=0;
   const failToFallback=()=>{
     if(!active||stage.dataset.deenCanvasFrame==='1')return;
     renderErrors++;
     stopFrameLoop(video);
     try{video.pause()}catch(_){}
     fallbackCelebrate(stage);
     clearTimeout(watchdog);
     watchdog=0;
     timer=setTimeout(()=>clear(false),1650);
   };

   const start=()=>{
     try{
       video.currentTime=0;
       watchdog=setTimeout(failToFallback,320);
       const p=video.play();
       p?.catch?.(failToFallback);
     }catch(_){
       failToFallback();
     }
   };

   video.addEventListener('playing',()=>{
     renderLoop(video,canvas,ctx,stage);
   },{once:true});

   video.addEventListener('ended',()=>{
     clearTimeout(watchdog);
     clear(false);
   },{once:true});
   video.addEventListener('error',failToFallback,{once:true});

   if(video.readyState>=1)start();
   else video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',start,{once:true});

   timer=setTimeout(()=>clear(false),5200);
   try{navigator.vibrate?.([18,26,18])}catch(_){}
   return true;
 }

 function maybe(detail={}){
   const combo=Math.max(0,Number(detail.combo)||0);
   if(detail.correct===true&&combo===3){
     eventTriggers++;
     return show(detail);
   }
   if(detail.correct!==true){
     clear(true);
     lastKey='';
   }
   return false;
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',event=>{
   if(window.__DEEN_COMBO_TIERS_ACTIVE)return;
   maybe(event?.detail||{});
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-advancing',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendering',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-result',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-closed',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:navigating',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',disableOldOverlays);

 disableOldOverlays();

 window.DEEN_COMBO_CANVAS={
   version:VERSION,
   build:BUILD,
   motion:'bottom-up',
   renderRange:[RENDER_MIN_WIDTH,RENDER_MAX_WIDTH],
   show,
   clear,
   mediaByCompanion:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(MEDIA_BY_COMPANION)),
   snapshot:()=>({
     version:VERSION,
     build:BUILD,
     selected:selectedCompanion(),
     shows,
     keyedFrames,
     renderErrors,
     eventTriggers,
     active:!!active,
     lastPick,
     lastKey
   })
 };
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v9101-single-companion-combo-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9101SingleCompanionCombo)return;
 window.__deenV9101SingleCompanionCombo=true;
 window.__DEEN_SINGLE_COMPANION_COMBO_ACTIVE=true;

 const VERSION='9.10.1-SINGLE-COMPANION-COMBO';
 const BUILD='9101singlecompanion1';

 let active=null;
 let timer=0;
 let lastKey='';
 let shows=0;
 let fallbacks=0;

 function selectedCompanion(){
   try{
     return String(
       window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS?.selected?.() ||
       state?.profile?.companion ||
       'owl'
     ).toLowerCase();
   }catch(_){
     return 'owl';
   }
 }

 function assetForSelected(){
   try{
     const src=window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS?.asset?.('celebrate');
     if(src)return src;
   }catch(_){}

   const id=selectedCompanion();
   return 'assets/companions/'+id+'/'+id+'_celebrate.png';
 }

 function keyFor(detail={}){
   if(detail.key)return String(detail.key);
   const q=detail.question||null;
   let index=0;
   try{index=Number(detail.index ?? session?.index ?? 0)||0}catch(_){}
   return index+'::'+String(q?.id||q?.question_id||q?.question||q?.prompt||'');
 }

 function destroyLegacyCombo(){
   try{window.DEEN_COMBO_MEDIA?.clear?.(true)}catch(_){}
   try{window.DEEN_COMBO_WEBM?.clear?.(true)}catch(_){}
   try{window.DEEN_COMBO_CANVAS?.clear?.(true)}catch(_){}

   document.getElementById('deenComboMediaOverlay')?.remove();
   document.getElementById('deenComboWebmOverlay')?.remove();
   document.getElementById('deenComboCanvasOverlay')?.remove();
   document.getElementById('deenStreakOverlay')?.classList?.add?.('closing');
 }

 function clear(immediate=false){
   clearTimeout(timer);
   timer=0;

   const node=active;
   active=null;
   if(!node)return false;

   if(immediate){
     try{node.remove()}catch(_){}
     return true;
   }

   node.classList.add('closing');
   setTimeout(()=>{try{node.remove()}catch(_){}},190);
   return true;
 }

 function show(detail={}){
   if(window.__DEEN_COMBO_OWNER_V9102===true)return false;
   destroyLegacyCombo();

   const key=keyFor(detail);
   if(key && key===lastKey && active)return false;
   lastKey=key;

   clear(true);

   const quiz=document.getElementById('quizView');
   if(!quiz)return false;

   if(getComputedStyle(quiz).position==='static'){
     quiz.style.position='relative';
   }

   const overlay=document.createElement('div');
   overlay.id='deenSingleCompanionCombo';
   overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');

   const head=document.querySelector('#quizView .lesson-head');
   const headHeight=Math.max(0,Math.round(head?.getBoundingClientRect?.().height||70));
   overlay.style.top=headHeight+'px';

   const img=document.createElement('img');
   img.className='deen9101-companion';
   img.alt='';
   img.decoding='async';
   img.src=assetForSelected()+(assetForSelected().includes('?')?'&':'?')+'v='+BUILD;

   img.onerror=()=>{
     fallbacks++;
     const id=selectedCompanion();
     img.src='assets/companions/'+id+'/'+id+'_idle.png?v='+BUILD;
   };

   overlay.appendChild(img);
   quiz.appendChild(overlay);
   active=overlay;
   shows++;

   timer=setTimeout(()=>clear(false),1800);
   try{navigator.vibrate?.([18,24,18])}catch(_){}
   return true;
 }

 function maybe(detail={}){
   const combo=Math.max(0,Number(detail.combo)||0);
   if(detail.correct===true && combo===3){
     return show(detail);
   }
   if(detail.correct!==true){
     clear(true);
     lastKey='';
   }
   return false;
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',event=>{
   if(window.__DEEN_COMBO_TIERS_ACTIVE)return;
   maybe(event?.detail||{});
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-advancing',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendering',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-result',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-closed',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:navigating',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',destroyLegacyCombo);

 destroyLegacyCombo();

 window.DEEN_SINGLE_COMPANION_COMBO={
   version:VERSION,
   build:BUILD,
   show,
   clear,
   selected:selectedCompanion,
   snapshot:()=>({
     version:VERSION,
     build:BUILD,
     selected:selectedCompanion(),
     shows,
     fallbacks,
     active:!!active,
     lastKey
   })
 };
})();

<script id="deen-v9102-authoritative-combo-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9102AuthoritativeCombo)return;
 window.__deenV9102AuthoritativeCombo=true;
 window.__DEEN_COMBO_OWNER_V9102=true;

 const VERSION='9.10.2-AUTHORITATIVE-COMBO';
 const BUILD='9102authoritativecombo1';
 const IDS=['lion','wolf','owl','turtle','eagle','cat'];

 let active=null;
 let timer=0;
 let lastKey='';
 let lastCombo=-1;
 let shows=0;
 let fallbacks=0;

 function selected(){
   let id='';
   try{id=String(window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS?.selected?.()||'').toLowerCase()}catch(_){}
   if(!IDS.includes(id)){
     try{id=String(state?.profile?.companion||'').toLowerCase()}catch(_){}
   }
   return IDS.includes(id)?id:'owl';
 }

 function currentQuestionKey(q){
   let idx=0;
   try{idx=Number(session?.index)||0}catch(_){}
   return idx+'::'+String(q?.id||q?.question_id||q?.prompt||q?.question||q?.text||'');
 }

 function removeLegacy(){
   for(const id of [
     'deenStreakOverlay',
     'deenComboMediaOverlay',
     'deenComboWebmOverlay',
     'deenComboCanvasOverlay',
     'deenSingleCompanionCombo'
   ]){
     try{document.getElementById(id)?.remove()}catch(_){}
   }

   try{window.DEEN_COMBO_MEDIA?.clear?.(true)}catch(_){}
   try{window.DEEN_COMBO_WEBM?.clear?.(true)}catch(_){}
   try{window.DEEN_COMBO_CANVAS?.clear?.(true)}catch(_){}
   try{window.DEEN_SINGLE_COMPANION_COMBO?.clear?.(true)}catch(_){}
 }

 function asset(){
   let src='';
   try{src=String(window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS?.asset?.('celebrate')||'')}catch(_){}
   if(src)return src;

   // The visible lesson companion is guaranteed to match the user's selection.
   try{
     src=String(document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-companion-runtime-img')?.src||'');
   }catch(_){}
   if(src)return src;

   const id=selected();
   return 'assets/companions/'+id+'/'+id+'_celebrate.png';
 }

 function preload(){
   try{
     const img=new Image();
     img.decoding='async';
     img.src=asset()+(asset().includes('?')?'&':'?')+'v='+BUILD;
   }catch(_){}
 }

 function clear(immediate=false){
   clearTimeout(timer);
   timer=0;

   const node=active;
   active=null;
   if(!node)return false;

   if(immediate){
     try{node.remove()}catch(_){}
     return true;
   }

   node.classList.add('closing');
   setTimeout(()=>{try{node.remove()}catch(_){}},230);
   return true;
 }

 function show(q){
   removeLegacy();
   clear(true);

   const frame=document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');
   if(!frame)return false;

   const overlay=document.createElement('div');
   overlay.id='deenAuthoritativeCombo';
   overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');

   const img=document.createElement('img');
   img.className='deen9102-animal';
   img.alt='';
   img.decoding='async';

   const src=asset();
   img.src=src+(src.includes('?')?'&':'?')+'v='+BUILD;

   img.onerror=()=>{
     fallbacks++;
     const live=document.querySelector('#lesson .deen-companion-runtime-img');
     const liveSrc=String(live?.src||'');
     if(liveSrc && img.src!==liveSrc){
       img.src=liveSrc;
       return;
     }
     const id=selected();
     const idle='assets/companions/'+id+'/'+id+'_idle.png?v='+BUILD;
     if(!img.src.includes(id+'_idle.png'))img.src=idle;
   };

   overlay.appendChild(img);
   frame.appendChild(overlay);
   active=overlay;
   shows++;

   timer=setTimeout(()=>clear(false),1850);
   try{navigator.vibrate?.([18,24,18])}catch(_){}
   return true;
 }

 function authoritativeOnGrade(correct,officialCombo,q){
   if(window.__DEEN_COMBO_TIERS_ACTIVE)return false;
   if(typeof correct!=='boolean')return false;

   const combo=correct?Math.max(0,Number(officialCombo)||0):0;
   const key=currentQuestionKey(q);

   if(key===lastKey && combo===lastCombo)return false;
   lastKey=key;
   lastCombo=combo;

   if(!correct){
     clear(true);
     return true;
   }

   if(combo===3){
     setTimeout(()=>show(q),110);
   }
   return true;
 }

 function installOwner(){
   if(window.__DEEN_COMBO_TIERS_ACTIVE)return false;
   const api=window.DEEN_COMPANION_STREAK||window.DEEN_COMBO;
   if(!api)return false;

   api.onGrade=authoritativeOnGrade;
   api.celebrate=combo=>Number(combo)===3?show(null):false;
   api.selected=selected;
   api.version=VERSION;

   window.DEEN_COMPANION_STREAK=api;
   window.DEEN_COMBO=api;
   return true;
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',()=>{
   removeLegacy();
   preload();
   installOwner();
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-advancing',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendering',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-result',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-closed',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:navigating',()=>clear(true));

 removeLegacy();
 installOwner();
 preload();
 [0,80,250,800].forEach(ms=>setTimeout(installOwner,ms));

 window.DEEN_AUTHORITATIVE_COMBO={
   version:VERSION,
   build:BUILD,
   show,
   clear,
   install:installOwner,
   selected,
   snapshot:()=>({
     version:VERSION,
     build:BUILD,
     selected:selected(),
     shows,
     fallbacks,
     active:!!active,
     lastKey,
     lastCombo,
     owner:window.DEEN_COMPANION_STREAK?.onGrade===authoritativeOnGrade
   })
 };
})();
</script>

@keyframes deen9103Rise{
  0%{
    transform:translate3d(-50%,calc(50vh + 240px),0) scale(.96);
  }
  60%{
    transform:translate3d(-50%,-55%,0) scale(1.04);
  }
  80%{
    transform:translate3d(-50%,-47%,0) scale(.995);

<script id="deen-v9103-direct-combo-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV9103DirectCombo)return;
 window.__deenV9103DirectCombo=true;
 window.__DEEN_COMBO_TIERS_ACTIVE=true;
 window.__DEEN_COMBO_OWNER_V9102=true;
 window.__DEEN_SINGLE_COMPANION_COMBO_ACTIVE=true;
 window.__DEEN_COMBO_CANVAS_ACTIVE=true;

 const VERSION='9.10.9-COMBO-TIERS';
 const BUILD='9293livecombo1';

 let active=null;
 let timer=0;
 let lastKey='';
 let shows=0;
 let source='';

 function keyFor(detail={}){
   if(detail.key)return String(detail.key);
   let idx=0;
   try{idx=Number(detail.index ?? session?.index ?? 0)||0}catch(_){}
   const q=detail.question||null;
   return idx+'::'+String(q?.id||q?.question_id||q?.prompt||q?.question||q?.text||'');
 }

 function removeOld(){
   for(const id of [
     'deenStreakOverlay','deenComboMediaOverlay','deenComboWebmOverlay',
     'deenComboCanvasOverlay','deenSingleCompanionCombo','deenAuthoritativeCombo'
   ]){
     try{document.getElementById(id)?.remove()}catch(_){}
   }
 }

 function clear(immediate=false){
   clearTimeout(timer);
   timer=0;
   const node=active;
   active=null;
   if(!node)return false;
   if(immediate){
     try{node.remove()}catch(_){}
     return true;
   }
   node.classList.add('closing');
   setTimeout(()=>{try{node.remove()}catch(_){}},210);
   return true;
 }

 const COMBO_ASSETS={
   // Use visually verified image files. The AVIF variants can render corrupt
   // rectangles / scanlines even while network loading succeeds (no onerror).
   lion:{3:'assets/companions/lion/lion_combo_3.webp',5:'assets/companions/lion/lion_combo_5.webp'},
   cat:{3:'assets/companions/cat/cat_combo_3.webp',5:'assets/companions/cat/cat_combo_5.webp'},
   eagle:{3:'assets/companions/eagle/eagle_combo_3.avif',5:'assets/companions/eagle/eagle_combo_5.avif'},
   turtle:{3:'assets/companions/turtle/turtle_combo_3.avif',5:'assets/companions/turtle/turtle_combo_5.avif'},
   owl:{3:'assets/companions/owl/owl_combo_3.avif',5:'assets/companions/owl/owl_combo_5.avif'},
   wolf:{3:'assets/companions/wolf/wolf_combo_3.avif',5:'assets/companions/wolf/wolf_combo_5.avif'}
 };

 function selectedCompanion(){
   let id='owl';
   try{id=String(window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS?.selected?.()||state?.profile?.companion||'owl').toLowerCase()}catch(_){}
   return COMBO_ASSETS[id]?id:'owl';
 }

 function fallbackCompanionSrc(){
   const id=selectedCompanion();
   const live=document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame .deen-companion-runtime-img');
   if(live?.complete && live?.naturalWidth>0 && live.src)return live.src;
   if(live?.src)return live.src;

   try{
     const s=window.DEEN_COMPANION_ASSETS?.asset?.('lesson_idle');
     if(s)return s;
   }catch(_){}

   return 'assets/companions/'+id+'/'+id+'_lesson_idle.png?v='+BUILD;
 }

 function comboCompanionSrc(combo){
   const id=selectedCompanion();
   const tier=(Number(combo)===5)?5:3;
   return (COMBO_ASSETS[id]?.[tier]||'')+'?v='+BUILD;
 }

 function show(detail={},combo=3){
   removeOld();
   clear(true);

   const frame=document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');
   if(!frame)return false;

   const src=comboCompanionSrc(combo);
   if(!src)return false;
   source=src;

   const overlay=document.createElement('div');
   overlay.id='deenDirectComboRise';
   overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');

   const img=document.createElement('img');
   img.className='deen9103-animal';
   img.alt='';
   img.decoding='async';
   img.src=src;

   img.onerror=()=>{
     const id=selectedCompanion();
     const tier=(Number(combo)===5)?5:3;
     const webp='assets/companions/'+id+'/'+id+'_combo_'+tier+'.webp?v='+BUILD;
     if(!img.dataset.webpFallback && /\.avif(?:\?|$)/i.test(img.src)){
       img.dataset.webpFallback='1';
       img.src=webp;
       return;
     }
     const fallback=fallbackCompanionSrc();
     if(fallback && img.src!==fallback)img.src=fallback;
   };

   overlay.appendChild(img);
   frame.appendChild(overlay);
   active=overlay;
   shows++;

   timer=setTimeout(()=>clear(false),1900);
   try{navigator.vibrate?.([18,24,18])}catch(_){}
   return true;
 }

 function checkAndShow(detail={}){
   if(detail.correct!==true)return false;

   let combo=0;
   try{combo=Number(session?.combo)||0}catch(_){}
   if(combo!==3 && combo!==5)combo=Math.max(0,Number(detail.combo)||0);
   if(combo!==3 && combo!==5)return false;

   const key=keyFor(detail)+'::'+combo;
   if(key && key===lastKey)return false;
   lastKey=key;
   return show(detail,combo);
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',event=>{
   const detail=event?.detail||{};
   if(detail.correct!==true){
     clear(true);
     return;
   }

   // Read authoritative session.combo after completeQuestion has committed it.
   queueMicrotask(()=>checkAndShow(detail));
   setTimeout(()=>checkAndShow(detail),80);
 });

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-advancing',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendering',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-result',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-closed',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:navigating',()=>clear(true));
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',removeOld);

 removeOld();

 window.DEEN_DIRECT_COMBO={
   version:VERSION,
   build:BUILD,
   show,
   clear,
   snapshot:()=>({
     version:VERSION,
     build:BUILD,
     shows,
     active:!!active,
     lastKey,
     source,
     selected:selectedCompanion(),
     renderedSrc:active?.querySelector?.('img')?.currentSrc||active?.querySelector?.('img')?.src||'',
     combo:(()=>{try{return Number(session?.combo)||0}catch(_){return 0}})()
   })
 };
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v1013-reference-matching-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV1013ReferenceMatching)return;window.__deenV1013ReferenceMatching=true;
 const VERSION='10.1.3-REFERENCE-MATCHING';
 let observer=null;

 function currentQuestion(){
   try{return session?.questions?.[session?.index]||null}catch(_){return null}
 }
 function isMatching(q=currentQuestion()){return q?.activity_type==='matching'}
 function frame(){return document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame')}
 function syncProgress(f){
   if(!f)return;
   const left=[...f.querySelectorAll('.match-card.match-left')];
   const total=left.length;
   const done=left.filter(x=>x.classList.contains('matched')).length;
   const progress=f.querySelector('.matching-progress');
   if(!progress||!total)return;
   let meter=progress.querySelector('.v1013-match-meter');
   if(!meter){
     meter=document.createElement('div');
     meter.className='v1013-match-meter';
     meter.innerHTML='<i></i>';
     progress.appendChild(meter);
   }
   meter.querySelector('i')?.style.setProperty('width',Math.round(done/total*100)+'%');
 }
 function scenery(f){
   let s=f.querySelector(':scope > .v1013-match-scenery');
   if(s)return s;
   s=document.createElement('div');
   s.className='v1013-match-scenery';
   s.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
   s.innerHTML='<span class="v1013-moon-cut"></span>';
   f.prepend(s);
   return s;
 }
 function title(f){
   const h=f.querySelector('.v9916-task-title');
   if(h)h.innerHTML='Eşleşen <span class="v1013-mint">çiftleri</span> bul';
   let sub=f.querySelector('.v1013-match-subtitle');
   if(!sub){
     sub=document.createElement('div');
     sub.className='v1013-match-subtitle';
     sub.textContent='Doğru kavramları anlamlarıyla eşleştir.';
     h?.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',sub);
   }
 }
 function watch(f){
   try{observer?.disconnect?.()}catch(_){}
   const grid=f.querySelector('.match-grid');
   if(!grid||!('MutationObserver' in window))return;
   observer=new MutationObserver(()=>syncProgress(f));
   observer.observe(grid,{subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
 }
 function clear(){
   try{observer?.disconnect?.()}catch(_){}
   observer=null;
   const f=frame();if(!f)return;
   f.classList.remove('v1013-match-ref');
   f.querySelector(':scope > .v1013-match-scenery')?.remove();
   f.querySelector('.v1013-match-subtitle')?.remove();
 }
 function apply(){
   const q=currentQuestion(),f=frame();
   if(!f)return false;
   if(f.classList.contains('v1017-match-screen')){
     clear();
     return false;
   }
   if(!isMatching(q)){clear();return false}
   f.classList.add('v1013-match-ref');
   scenery(f);title(f);syncProgress(f);watch(f);
   return true;
 }
 function schedule(){
   [0,90,180,320].forEach(ms=>setTimeout(()=>{try{apply()}catch(_){}},ms));
 }
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendering',()=>{try{clear()}catch(_){}});
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',schedule);
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',schedule);
 window.DEEN_REFERENCE_MATCHING={
   version:VERSION,
   apply,
   check:()=>({
     version:VERSION,
     active:!!document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame.v1013-match-ref'),
     title:document.querySelector('#lesson .v9916-task-title')?.textContent||'',
     cards:document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .v1013-match-ref .match-card').length,
     matched:document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .v1013-match-ref .match-left.matched').length,
     meter:!!document.querySelector('#lesson .v1013-match-ref .v1013-match-meter')
   })
 };
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v1015-v853-reference-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV1015V853Reference)return;window.__deenV1015V853Reference=true;
 let observer=null;

 function frame(){return document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame.v1013-match-ref')}
 function sync(){
   const f=frame();if(!f)return false;
   const cards=[...f.querySelectorAll('.v853-match-card[data-side="left"]')];
   if(!cards.length)return false;
   const total=cards.length;
   const done=cards.filter(x=>x.classList.contains('matched')).length;
   const status=f.querySelector('.v853-match-status');
   if(status){
     const b=status.querySelector('b');
     if(b)b.textContent=done+' / '+total+' eşleşme';
     let fill=status.querySelector('.v1015-match-fill');
     if(!fill){
       fill=document.createElement('i');
       fill.className='v1015-match-fill';
       status.appendChild(fill);
     }
     fill.style.width=Math.round(done/total*100)+'%';
   }
   f.classList.toggle('v1014-three-pairs',total===3);
   f.classList.toggle('v1014-four-pairs',total===4);
   f.style.transform='';
   f.style.width='100%';
   f.style.maxWidth='none';
   document.getElementById('lesson')?.classList.remove('v9913-fitted');
   return true;
 }
 function watch(){
   try{observer?.disconnect?.()}catch(_){}
   const f=frame();if(!f)return;
   const wrap=f.querySelector('.v853-match-wrap');
   if(!wrap)return;
   observer=new MutationObserver(()=>queueMicrotask(sync));
   observer.observe(wrap,{subtree:true,attributes:true,childList:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
 }
 function apply(){const ok=sync();if(ok)watch();return ok}
 function schedule(){[0,60,150,280].forEach(ms=>setTimeout(()=>{try{apply()}catch(_){}},ms))}
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',schedule);
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',schedule);
 window.DEEN_V853_REFERENCE_MATCHING={
   version:'10.1.5',
   apply,
   check:()=>({
     active:!!frame(),
     cards:document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .v1013-match-ref .v853-match-card').length,
     matched:document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .v1013-match-ref .v853-match-card[data-side="left"].matched').length,
     legacyScale:document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame')?.style.transform||''
   })
 };
})();
</script>

(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV1016ReferenceFinish)return;window.__deenV1016ReferenceFinish=true;

 function mosqueSvg(){
   const ns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
   const svg=document.createElementNS(ns,'svg');

 }

 function apply(){

   /* Legacy fit residue is never allowed on the reference matching screen. */
   f.style.transform='';
   f.style.width='100%';
   f.style.maxWidth='none';
   document.getElementById('lesson')?.classList.remove('v9913-fitted');
   return true;
 }

<script id="deen-v1017-card-normalizer-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV1017CardNormalizer)return;
 window.__deenV1017CardNormalizer=true;

 const VERSION='10.5.8-CARD-NORMALIZER-STABLE';
 let runs=0,repairs=0;

 function ensureCard(card){
   if(!card||!card.classList?.contains('v1017-card'))return false;

   let label=card.querySelector('.v1017-card-label');
   let indicator=card.querySelector('.v1017-choice-indicator');
   const raw=(label?.textContent||card.textContent||'').trim();

   if(!label){
     card.textContent='';
     label=document.createElement('span');
     label.className='v1017-card-label';
     label.textContent=raw;
     card.append(label);
     repairs++;
   }
   if(!indicator){
     indicator=document.createElement('span');
     indicator.className='v1017-choice-indicator';
     indicator.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
     card.append(indicator);
     repairs++;
   }

   label.style.setProperty('flex','1 1 auto','important');
   label.style.setProperty('min-width','0','important');
   label.style.setProperty('display','block','important');
   label.style.setProperty('text-align','left','important');

   indicator.style.setProperty('display','grid','important');
   indicator.style.setProperty('place-items','center','important');
   indicator.style.setProperty('flex','0 0 24px','important');
   indicator.style.setProperty('width','24px','important');
   indicator.style.setProperty('height','24px','important');
   indicator.style.setProperty('min-width','24px','important');
   indicator.style.setProperty('min-height','24px','important');
   indicator.style.setProperty('border','1.5px solid rgba(116,158,168,.74)','important');
   indicator.style.setProperty('border-radius','50%','important');
   indicator.style.setProperty('background','rgba(4,29,37,.32)','important');
   indicator.style.setProperty('box-sizing','border-box','important');
   indicator.style.setProperty('pointer-events','none','important');

   card.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');
   card.style.setProperty('align-items','center','important');
   card.style.setProperty('justify-content','flex-start','important');
   card.style.setProperty('text-align','left','important');
   card.style.setProperty('gap','10px','important');
   card.style.setProperty('padding-right','14px','important');

   if(card.classList.contains('matched')){
     if(indicator.textContent!=='✓')indicator.textContent='✓';
     indicator.style.setProperty('border-color','#83ebc2','important');
     indicator.style.setProperty('background','#83ebc2','important');
     indicator.style.setProperty('color','#073a37','important');
   }else if(card.classList.contains('selected')){
     if(indicator.textContent!=='')indicator.textContent='';
     indicator.style.setProperty('border-color','#7ce6bf','important');
     indicator.style.setProperty('background','#7ce6bf','important');
     indicator.style.setProperty('box-shadow','inset 0 0 0 5px #123f40','important');
   }else{
     if(indicator.textContent!=='')indicator.textContent='';
     indicator.style.removeProperty('box-shadow');
   }

   card.dataset.v1017Normalized='1';
   return true;
 }

 function apply(){
   runs++;
   const cards=[...document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .v1017-card')];
   cards.forEach(ensureCard);
   if(cards.length){
     document.documentElement.dataset.deenMatchingNormalizer='v1024';
     document.documentElement.dataset.deenMatchingIndicatorCount=String(document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .v1017-choice-indicator').length);
   }
   return cards.length;
 }

 function schedule(){
   [0,16,60,160,360].forEach(ms=>setTimeout(apply,ms));
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',schedule);
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',schedule);
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendering',()=>setTimeout(apply,0));

 let observerScheduled=false;
 const observer=new MutationObserver(mutations=>{
   const relevant=mutations.some(m=>
     m.type==='childList' &&
     m.target?.id==='answerArea' &&
     (m.addedNodes.length||m.removedNodes.length)
   );
   if(!relevant||observerScheduled)return;
   observerScheduled=true;
   requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
     observerScheduled=false;
     apply();
   });
 });
 const startObserver=()=>{
   const host=document.getElementById('answerArea');
   if(host)observer.observe(host,{subtree:false,childList:true});
 };
 if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',startObserver,{once:true});
 else startObserver();

 setTimeout(schedule,0);

 window.DEEN_MATCHING_CARD_NORMALIZER={
   version:VERSION,
   apply,
   check:()=>({
     version:VERSION,
     runs,repairs,
     cards:document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .v1017-card').length,
     labels:document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .v1017-card-label').length,
     indicators:document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .v1017-choice-indicator').length,
     normalized:document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .v1017-card[data-v1017-normalized="1"]').length,
     observerMode:'answerArea-direct-child-only'
   })
 };


})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v1017-authoritative-matching-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV1017AuthoritativeMatching)return;
 window.__deenV1017AuthoritativeMatching=true;

 // v10.2.1: visual CSS remains here, but rendering has one owner: segment-09.
 const VERSION='10.2.1-CORE09-GUARD';

 function currentRenderer(){
   return typeof window.renderMatching==='function'?window.renderMatching:null;
 }

 function render(q,area){
   const fn=currentRenderer();
   return fn?fn(q,area):false;
 }

 function takeoverCurrent(){
   try{
     const q=session?.questions?.[session?.index]||null;
     if(!q||q.activity_type!=='matching'||!Array.isArray(q.pairs))return false;
     const area=document.getElementById('answerArea');
     if(!area)return false;
     if(area.querySelector('.v1017-shell'))return true;
     const ok=render(q,area);
     if(ok)document.documentElement.dataset.deenMatchingTakeover='core09';
     return !!ok;
   }catch(err){
     console.error('DEEN matching core guard',err);
     document.documentElement.dataset.deenMatchingTakeover='error';
     return false;
   }
 }

 window.DEEN_AUTHORITATIVE_MATCHING={
   version:VERSION,
   render,
   takeover:takeoverCurrent,
   check:()=>({
     version:VERSION,
     owner:'segment-09',
     active:!!document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame.v1017-match-screen'),
     cards:document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .v1017-card').length,
     renderer:document.documentElement.dataset.deenMatchingRenderer||'core09-v1020',
     transform:document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame')?.style.transform||'',
     fitted:document.getElementById('lesson')?.classList.contains('v9913-fitted')||false
   })
 };

 document.documentElement.dataset.deenMatchingOwner='segment-09';
})();
</script>

  };

<script id="deen-v1038-fill-authoritative-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV1038FillAuthoritative)return;
 window.__deenV1038FillAuthoritative=true;
 const VERSION='10.3.8-FILL-AUTHORITATIVE';

 function apply(){
   let q=null;
   try{q=session?.questions?.[session?.index]||null}catch(_){}
   const f=document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame');
   if(!f||String(q?.activity_type||'')!=='fill_blank')return false;

   f.classList.add('deen-premium-fill','v1038-fill-authoritative');
   f.classList.remove('v9932-screen-centered','v9933-screen-centered','v9934-fixed-center','v9935-reference-balanced');
   f.style.removeProperty('--v9932-center-shift');
   f.style.removeProperty('--v9933-center-shift');

   const row=f.querySelector('.deen-character-question-row');
   const area=f.querySelector('#answerArea');
   [row,area].forEach(el=>{
     if(!el)return;
     el.style.removeProperty('transform');
     el.style.removeProperty('top');
     el.style.removeProperty('margin-top');
   });

   document.documentElement.dataset.deenFillLayout='v1038';
   try{window.DEEN_MOBILE_LESSON?.fit?.()}catch(_){}
   return true;
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendered',()=>{
   [0,80,220,520,900].forEach(ms=>setTimeout(apply,ms));
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-graded',()=>{
   [0,120,360].forEach(ms=>setTimeout(apply,ms));
 });
 document.addEventListener('deen:question-rendering',()=>setTimeout(apply,0));

 setTimeout(apply,900);

 window.DEEN_FILL_AUTHORITATIVE={
   version:VERSION,
   apply,
   snapshot:()=>({
     active:!!document.querySelector('#lesson .question-frame.v1038-fill-authoritative'),
     layout:document.documentElement.dataset.deenFillLayout||null,
     choices:document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .fill-choice').length,
     selected:document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .fill-choice.selected').length
   })
 };
})();
